# Audit 2026-11-11: every finding

> Companion of `AUDIT_2026-11-11.md`. The finders and verifiers wrote in English; kept as written.
> Part 1: the hub code (fixed on `server/p36-audit`). Part 2: apps/web, apps/android and agents (not fixed: the consult session's folders).

## Part 1: hub code (252)

### g01 · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain` · branch `audit/fix-g01`

- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/ads.py:255` · loosens() crashes on an explicit null and misses loosening changes the docstring lists → **fixed**
  - loosens() now also counts int(window_days) going up, and any scope change to something other than 'account' (account->111 and 111->222), as loosening; 111->account still counts as tightening. AdRuleEdit gets a model_validator that rejects an explicit null for scope, window_days, min_spend_before_judging, action and state (422 instead of 500). Null is still allowed for daily_cap, min_roas and note_fa. The PUT /companies/{code}/ad-rules/{rule_id} docstring changed (it now mentions a longer window, other campaigns and nulls), so the OpenAPI description is out of date; it was not regenerated. Tests: the loosens unit test now covers both new cases, the AdRuleEdit null cases are checked, and the r
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/ads.py:583` · Hub-revenue campaigns get 0 revenue for today and yesterday, so the ROAS floor pauses healthy campaigns → **fixed**
  - evaluate_due now works out hub-campaign revenue at judging time from revenue.days as it stands (via hub_confs/hub_spend/hub_credit). A day with no revenue.days rows, or a day where any row is incomplete, gets revenue=None. judge() leaves out None days for min_roas and ends the window at the last day whose revenue is known. If that day is older than STALE_DAYS it does not judge the floor. The trip day is that end day. daily_cap still uses the latest spend. The fetch range is widened by STALE_DAYS, and a new constant JUDGE_DAYS = 93 bounds the per-company hub spend lookup. Non-hub campaigns behave as before. Tests: judge unit cases for None days and for a stale last-known day, plus a new DB te
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/ads.py:584` · The whole day's hub revenue is credited to every hub campaign, so it is counted N times → **fixed**
  - hub_credit splits the day's hub commission across the hub campaigns of both ad connections, in proportion to their spend converted to USDT (spend / usdt_rate), or equally when nothing was spent. Each share is converted back with its own account's usdt_rate. store_read uses this for its rows and, in the same transaction, updates the other connection's ad_daily revenue and roas for those days. That connection's metrics_daily figures are refreshed only on its own next read. Revenue is no longer counted N times in ad_revenue, ad_roas or per-campaign ROAS. The old hub_revenue() helper was removed (it was used only here). Test: the daily-read test now also reads Meta with a hub campaign and assert
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/advisor.py:1194` · auto_memos raises inside the shared sweep transaction, so failed-memo marking and every trigger (including technical notices) roll back → **fixed**
  - auto_memos returns {made: 0, skipped: 'not_seated'} when _advisor_code is None. Each request() call runs in tx.conn.begin_nested(); an ApiError is logged, the card goes into out['refused'] and the loop continues. One card can no longer roll back fail_orphans and fire_triggers in the shared sweep transaction. Test: test_an_unseated_advisor_never_aborts_the_sweep (pauses HQ-ADV in try/finally).
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/advisor.py:443` · A failed memo can never be requested again for the same card version → **fixed**
  - request() now reads the existing memo FOR UPDATE. If its state is 'failed', the same row is reset to state 'writing' with state_reason and work_id set to NULL and facts/facts_sha256 refreshed, then a new work item is made as for a new memo (created=True, so the route records the interaction). Substitute seat calls are skipped for a seat whose opinion is already on the memo or has a queued/running substitutes.calls row for the memo. The memo's trigger column is immutable, so a failed auto memo that the owner asks for again keeps trigger='auto'; its work's run_meta still uses the new request's zone. auto_memos still skips failed memos on purpose, so there is no automatic retry loop. Test: adde
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/advisor.py:597` · memo.write can overwrite a substitute opinion that arrives while it runs → **fixed**
  - write() now reads the memo with find(..., lock=True), which adds FOR UPDATE. Lock order stays decision then memo, the same as request() and the stale trigger; apply_opinion takes only the memo lock, so this adds no deadlock. No test, since the race is hard to reproduce.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/advisor.py:875` · One revenue-drop alert wakes the advisor twice (the trigger key uses the owner's day, the lookup uses 24 hours) → **fixed**
  - detect() now builds the revenue-drop trigger key as revenue:{code}:{alert's own day}, falling back to the owner day if the alert day is NULL. An alert still under 24 h old after the owner's midnight now produces the same key and does not wake the advisor again. Test: test_a_revenue_drop_wakes_the_advisor_once_across_the_owners_midnight.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/affiliate_gaps.py:171` · On 29 February, listing() and scan() crash with ValueError → **fixed**
  - Added affiliate_gaps.year_back(d) = d.replace(day=1, year=d.year - 1), used in listing() and scan() instead of month_start(today.replace(year=...)). Test: test_a_year_back_from_29_february_is_the_first_of_february in test_p24_sales.py.

### g02 · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain` · branch `audit/fix-g02`

- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/affiliate.py:258` · deposits_usdt() reports 0 when the deposits call failed → **fixed**
  - DayPull.deposits_usdt() now returns None when any gap names 'money/detail/list'. This covers a failure on page 0 and a partial sum from a failure on a later page. Added the pure test test_a_failed_deposits_call_is_a_gap_not_zero in tests/test_p10_revenue.py.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/automations.py:1130` · The runaway-loop guard counts 'skipped' runs, so a busy event automation is switched off as 'failed' → **fixed**
  - The hourly loop guard in fire() now counts only runs with outcome <> 'skipped'. Condition checks that did not hold no longer switch the automation off as failed. Updated the MAX_PER_HOUR comment. Added the DB test test_skipped_checks_never_trip_the_loop_guard in tests/test_p22_ops.py (MAX_PER_HOUR+2 skipped events, automation stays on).
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/automations.py:1305` · The event cursor jumps to max(outbox.id), so events still being committed are skipped for good → **fixed**
  - tick() no longer jumps event_cursor to max(outbox.id). New EVENT_SETTLE (30 min) and settled_outbox() give the newest outbox id with created_at < now() - 30 min, using the DB clock and a backward PK scan computed once per tick. The cursor only advances to max(cursor, settled), or to min(settled, last id) for a full batch. Rows newer than that are read again each tick, and a NOT EXISTS on growth.automation_runs (automation_id, 'event:'||payload->>'id') uses the existing UNIQUE index, so each event still runs once. Assumption: no transaction that emits an event stays open longer than about EVENT_SETTLE. created_at is the transaction start, so this is conservative. No schema change. Added the D
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/automations.py:483` · The preview the owner seals shows only the first hour and minute of a schedule → **fixed**
  - when_fa now lists every by_hour x by_minute time via the new at_fa(), for example «هر روز ساعت 09:00 و 18:00». Above 6 combinations it gives «ساعت‌های … ، دقیقه‌ی …». HOURLY lists all its minutes, and HOURLY with INTERVAL now names its minute too («هر 6 ساعت، دقیقه‌ی 15»). Single-time output is unchanged, so existing assertions still hold. routines/playbooks reuse when_fa and only change for multi-value rules. Added the pure test test_the_preview_names_every_time_the_schedule_runs. UNTIL is still not shown in the preview, as before; that was not part of this finding.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/briefings.py:527` · Letter attempts are counted before the delivery is authorised or validated, so one failed deliver uses up the single send-back and attempts can hit the CHECK ceiling → **fixed**
  - letter_gate: the send-back now belongs to the first unsourced attempt while the letter is still in state 'writing'. Before, it depended on attempts == 1, so a clean delivery refused after the gate (no key, 423, expired evidence) no longer uses it up. attempts still counts every gated call but is capped with least(attempts + 1, LETTER_ATTEMPTS_MAX=10), so the CHECK can no longer fail. Existing tests are unchanged (attempts == 1 assertions still hold). Added test_a_refused_clean_delivery_does_not_use_up_the_send_back in tests/test_p12b_briefings.py. No route signature change.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/campaigns.py:198` · Campaign calendar includes items from the day after the requested `to` date (off-by-one) → **fixed**
  - Added _last_day(hi, tz) = _day(hi - 1µs, tz) and used it as the inclusive bound in _manual, _campaigns and _ads. The timestamp sources keep the exclusive hi. Extended test_the_campaign_calendar_merges_every_source_and_marks_conflicts in tests/test_p22_growth.py: a window ending the day before a campaign's start no longer contains it, and no item lies past `to`.
- **high** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/catalogue.py:940` · Map fallback job is created without catching ApiError, so a locked company or revoked server leaves the catalogue stuck and breaks the holding-wide watch sweep → **fixed**
  - on_job wraps the map-fallback SV.create_job in try/except ApiError, the same way _retry does. On refusal (423 lock, 409 revoked) no map entry is added, and the entry's reason_fa says the map could not be taken: _job_reason gained mapped=False. The entry still ends, so finish() runs and the catalogue leaves 'running'. The unread section is now appended after the map attempt so it carries the right reason. sweep() also ends each stale catalogue inside tx.conn.begin_nested() and logs any error, so one bad catalogue can no longer roll back watch.sweep. Added test_a_stale_run_on_a_detached_server_ends_and_never_fails_the_sweep in tests/test_p19_watch.py.
- **high** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/connections.py:444` · connection.set lets a company's CEO or media director point its Instagram publish/read commands at any company's server → **fixed**
  - New CN.ig_server_allowed and CN.ig_server allow a server only when its name equals IG-Hub's (the ig_hub setting's server, case-insensitive) or it belongs to the connection's own company. agent_config takes a new company= kwarg and refuses any other server with 422 on the 'server' field; the route passes company.code. Stored values are re-checked: session_publish._route gives ready=False and falls back to IG-Hub when the server is refused, read_instagram raises ReadError(IG_SERVER_REFUSED_FA), and best_hour.read_one returns 'skipped' (its query now also selects co.code). The match is on IG-Hub's server name, not the strict (company, name) pair, on purpose. Many existing tests (w11, w17, w18, 

### g03 · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain` · branch `audit/fix-g03`

- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/corpus.py:244` · git cat-file --batch is not killed or reaped when it hits its 300 s timeout → **fixed**
  - corpus.read_blobs: communicate() is wrapped like media.run_tool. On any BaseException the child is killed (ProcessLookupError suppressed), waited for up to 5 s, and the exception is re-raised. Added the contextlib import. No test added: no test covers corpus subprocess timeouts, and simulating one needs a hung git.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/council.py:154` · Council fact pack picks an arbitrary subject's value for each metrics_daily key → **fixed**
  - council.fact_pack now uses a CTE that finds max(day) per (kind, key) and returns every subject on that day, ordered by kind, key, subject (LIMIT 30 kept). Each subject is its own figure: key 'kind.key[subject]' and title 'kind: key (subject)', with the subject scrubbed and cut to 80 chars. Rows with an empty subject keep the old 'kind.key' form. I chose listing over sum() because keys such as pagespeed scores or positions cannot be added. Regression test: test_the_fact_pack_lists_each_subject_of_a_connection_figure in apps/api/tests/test_p21_council.py.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/creative_overlay.py:400` · A failed overlay render leaks the committed original and the scratch thumbnail and agent copies → **fixed**
  - creative_overlay.store_result renders before it commits anything. agent_bytes, brand, font and render_png run first, then the original and the PNG are committed. A finally block always unlinks img.original/thumb/agent, which are no-ops after a commit has moved them, and the png scratch file. A failed render now leaves no storage object without a files row and no scratch files. Regression test: test_a_failed_overlay_render_stores_nothing_and_leaves_no_scratch in apps/api/tests/test_p12a_substitutes.py. It monkeypatches render_png to raise Refused, then checks that nothing was committed and that no scratch path is left.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/deploy_diff.py:387` · Whole-file patch of a renamed file shows the whole file as added → **fixed**
  - deploy_diff.file_patch now runs the whole `git diff --numstat -z -M base head` with no pathspec and looks up the row by path, so -M sees the rename and patch_of receives old_path. I fixed it this way, rather than storing old_path in the card rows, so cards stored before the fix are corrected too and the card's diff shape stays the same. The route signature and docstring are unchanged. I confirmed in a scratch git repo that with both paths the patch is only the changed line. Regression test: test_a_renamed_files_whole_patch_is_its_rename_diff in apps/api/tests/test_p12b_cards.py.
- **high** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/desk.py:368` · Any short support message containing the word «لغو» or "stop" permanently opts the customer out and is never answered → **fixed**
  - desk.is_opt_out now treats a message as an opt-out only when, after punctuation is removed and the text is normalised, it is under 40 chars and consists only of OPT_OUT phrases plus a small filler set: please/لطفا, اشتراک, پیام, me, my, from this list, and similar. The substring path through _has(t, OPT_OUT[:4]) is gone. I checked it against the real normalisers: all the false positives in the finding now return False ('چطور برداشت رو لغو کنم؟', 'سفارشم لغو نشد', 'لغو سفارش', "app won't stop crashing", 'Stop loss کار نمیکنه', and so on). Bare لغو, stop, unsubscribe, 'لطفاً لغو اشتراک', 'stop please', 'unsubscribe me', نمیخواهم and 'دیگه پیام نده' still opt out. I kept the early return for a 
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/desk.py:458` · Outbound answers are passed through the customer PII mask before storage and are sent masked → **fixed**
  - hq_common.customer_mask.mask gains a keyword `personal: bool = True`. With False, spans of kind 'personal' (e-mail, phone, IBAN, national code) are dropped, and secrets, keys and cards are still masked. desk.insert_out stores outbound rows with mask(..., personal=False), so the contact details in an approved answer reach the customer as written. The default is unchanged for all other callers. This touches services/hq-common, which the api imports; the deploy must pick up the new hq-common (rebuild or reinstall if it is not a path install). Regression test: test_an_outbound_answer_keeps_its_contact_details_but_never_a_secret in services/hq-common/tests/test_customer_mask.py. I ran it locally 
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/desk.py:751` · Safety line sent twice in the same message after a customer shares a secret → **fixed**
  - auto_message takes a new optional `with_safety: bool | None = None` (None keeps the old behaviour of adding the line on the first answer). desk.process step 3 passes with_safety=False, because its body is the safety line itself. safety_sent_at is still set by step 3's own UPDATE. Regression test: test_a_shared_secret_gets_the_safety_line_once in apps/api/tests/test_p10_desk.py. It sends a Luhn-valid card number and checks that the safety text appears exactly once across the outbound rows.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/desk.py:772` · Live channel with auto_send 'off': a strong FAQ match is recorded as a shadow draft and the customer is never answered or queued → **fixed**
  - desk.process step 4: a strong FAQ match on a live, non-mystery channel with auto_send 'off' still records the draft (or blocked) row. It then calls handoff(tx, hub, conv, 'low_confidence', or 'blocked' when the guard refused, note='auto_send_off'), which moves the conversation to waiting_owner and sends the holding reply. It returns Outcome('handoff', ..., 'auto_send_off', queued=...). Channels that are not live still return the silent 'shadow' outcome. Regression test: test_a_live_channel_with_auto_send_off_hands_a_faq_match_to_the_owner in apps/api/tests/test_p10_desk.py.

### g04 · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain` · branch `audit/fix-g04`

- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/dispatch.py:196` · Any number before an output word becomes the count; a count over 100 makes the order preview and order creation fail with 500 → **fixed**
  - dispatch.py: added MAX_COUNT = 100 and used it in Route.count (le=MAX_COUNT, same value, so the OpenAPI schema does not change). output_of now keeps a count only when 0 < count <= MAX_COUNT and gives None otherwise. This also covers council.py's use of output_of. Added a regression test in test_p21_secretary.py: «سال ۱۴۰۵ برنامه…» gives (plan, None), «برای ۱۲۰ پست…» gives (post, None), «۱۰۰ پست» gives (post, 100). I could not run it because pydantic is not installed outside docker.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/dispatch.py:439` · A remembered correction is applied without checking it against what exists now; carry_out then crashes with KeyError → **fixed**
  - dispatch.py: apply_memory(dr, to, d, online) now drops remembered companies and collaborators that are not in d['companies'], and keeps the rules' companies when none are left. A remembered kind 'kit' with no kit falls back to the rules' kind. A kind 'server' (remembered, or kept after a remembered company swap) falls back to the rules' kind, or to 'question' if that was 'server', unless companies[0] has a server online. by_rules passes d and online, and its only caller (routes/orders.py) already passes online. No route signature changes. Added a pure regression test test_a_remembered_route_holds_only_what_exists_now in test_p21_secretary.py.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/erase.py:171` · The erase preview never finds archived copies of an output: it queries source_kind = 'deliverable', which the CHECK forbids → **needs_migration**
  - Python half fixed. erase.py _output_keeps now counts archive.records WHERE source_ref = 'deliverable:<ref>' AND state <> 'disposed', which is the shape archive/deliverables.py files under. The source_kind filter is gone because the CHECK never allows 'deliverable'. Added test_an_output_filed_in_the_archive_is_named_in_its_erase_preview in test_w8_erase.py: file the output, then the preview names the archived copy. The session erase's 'filed' count lives in owner.erase_order_run from migration 0055 (never redefined since). It has the same wrong predicate and needs a migration (see migration_spec). Until then, the session erase preview still leaves out the 'archived copies stay' line.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/errands.py:367` · on_decided appends a step without the 200-step cap, so the owner's decision fails on a full errand → **fixed**
  - errands.py on_decided: when the errand already has MAX_STEPS steps, it appends an empty array instead of the note, so the status still changes to 'working' and the owner's decision no longer breaks the steps <= 200 CHECK. Added test_the_owners_decision_on_a_full_errand_adds_no_201st_step in test_inquiries_errands.py. The test inserts a 200-step waiting_owner errand with decision_id NULL and calls E.on_decided directly with d['id'] = None.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/feedback.py:188` · Threshold task is not opened on the signal that reaches the threshold (that signal is not linked yet) → **fixed**
  - feedback.py: file() no longer calls threshold(). ingest() and tag() now call threshold() after they link the signal to the item (UPDATE or INSERT with item_id), so the signal that reaches the threshold is counted. batch_company relies on its existing sweep, which runs after the loop and re-reads the items. test_p10_growth.py now asserts that threshold_work_id is set right after the 5th rule-tagged ingest, before FB.batch runs.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/feedback.py:338` · feedback.tag on an NPS wave (or a conversation with no inbound message) adds a new signal and +1 count on every call → **fixed**
  - feedback.py tag(): with no message_id (an NPS wave, or a conversation with no inbound message), it looks up an earlier signal by company_id, source_ref, message_id IS NULL, tagged_by='agent', kind and area. If that signal has an item, the call is answered as 'already' and nothing is counted. A mystery row found this way is not inserted again. Added to the feedback.tag test in test_p10_growth.py: an NPS wave tagged 3 times keeps count 1 and has one signal row.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/files.py:294` · PDF text containing NUL characters cannot be stored; the upload request returns 500 and the file stays 'processing' → **fixed**
  - NUL characters are now stripped in two places: files.document_done, which covers every extract_text caller (routes/files.py, internal.py, legal.py, servers.py and the worker), and media.pdf_text_job. Added test_a_nul_in_extracted_text_is_dropped_not_a_failed_write in test_files_media.py: upload a PDF, set its processing row back to 'processing', call document_done with 'a\x00b', and the stored text is 'ab'. As the verifier noted, the API answered 422 rather than 500, but the defect was real.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/gov_calendar.py:458` · A deploy freeze longer than 400 days breaks the nightly calendar sync and every deploy card that has a window → **fixed**
  - gov_calendar.py freeze_events: the event now ends at min(lifted_at or until, created_at + FAR) with FAR = 400 days, so it always meets the calendar_events CHECK. The freeze row itself is unchanged. The existing lifted-freeze UPDATE still only shortens the event. Added test_a_freeze_longer_than_400_days_is_a_capped_event_not_a_failed_sync in test_p11b_risks_calendar_batch.py: a 600-day freeze gives one event spanning exactly FAR. The test lifts the freeze in a finally block.
  - (schema part: in migration `0062_audit_fixes.py`)

### g05 · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain` · branch `audit/fix-g05`

- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/gov_common.py:21` · gov_common.number() reads a thousands comma as a decimal point and stops at the Persian thousands separator → **fixed**
  - _DIGITS now translates U+066C to ','. _NUMBER first tries comma-grouped thousands (-?\d{1,3}(?:,\d{3})+(?!\d)(?:\.\d+)?) and strips the commas. Otherwise the old form applies, so a lone comma between digits ('1,5') is still a decimal. Checked by hand: '10,000 کاربر فعال' gives 10000, '۱۰٬۰۰۰' gives 10000, '1,250,000' gives 1250000, '1,250,000.5' gives 1250000.5, '۱٫۵' and '1,5' give 1.5, '-2,000' gives -2000. Regression test added: test_p11b_cards_okr.py::test_a_number_in_free_text_reads_thousands_separators.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/gov_common.py:95` · number() reads a thousands separator as a decimal point, so free-text targets come out 1000x too small → **fixed**
  - Duplicate of #0, fixed by the same commit c92e7dd0. As the verifier found, the OKR path already receives floats through KeyResultChange validation.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/gov_kpis.py:470` · seed_company check-then-insert race: concurrent first reads of a new company fail → **fixed**
  - seed_company now inserts the kpi_sets row with INSERT ... ON CONFLICT (company_id) DO NOTHING RETURNING company_id. When no row comes back it returns 0 before inserting any KPI rows, so a concurrent first seed waits for the winner and then does nothing instead of raising 23505. No regression test: the race cannot be reproduced deterministically in the suite.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/gov_kpis.py:516` · The 'append only when changed or hourly' rule fails for values with more than 6 decimals → **fixed**
  - _store rounds value to 6 decimals (value = round(value, 6)) before comparing and storing, to match kpi_values.value numeric(20,6). Regression test: test_p11b_cards_okr.py::test_a_value_beyond_six_decimals_is_appended_once (two records of 123.45678912 ten minutes apart append one row).
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/gov_kpis.py:943` · Holding-only queries (e.g. treasury.agents_cost_month_usd) can be added to an operating company's KPI set and show every company's spend on it → **fixed**
  - Query.holding is now enforced in three places. put_set returns 422 (fields kpis.N.source: 'a holding-only query') for a holding query on a non-holding company. seed_company skips such a seed entry. collect treats such a pair as no_source and never runs the query, which also covers rows that already exist. The test in test_a_query_outside_the_registry_is_422 is extended. The PUT /api/v1/kpis docstring and signature are unchanged; only a new 422 field message was added.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/gov_okr.py:351` · An owner amendment that names an objective without title_fa renames the objective to the literal text 'None' → **fixed**
  - gov_okr.on_decided skips an objective amendment that has no title_fa, or an empty one, instead of writing str(None). The test test_an_okr_is_active_only_with_the_owners_seal now also sends objective_changes [{ref}] and asserts the title is unchanged.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/gov_outcomes.py:211` · A decision's outcome review is decided by code, permanently, from a KPI value that may be older than the decision → **fixed**
  - _kpi_value now takes a lower bound, the seal time, computed as due_at - day days (on_sealed sets due_at = now() + day). Only values with at in [seal, now] count. An older value falls through to 'unmeasurable', which goes to the proposer's note and the overseer. I did not add a freshness_hours check, because the existing test records a value once and then measures 31 days later. New test: test_p11b_outcomes_authority.py::test_a_value_from_before_the_seal_never_decides_a_review.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/gov_risks.py:323` · Updating an overdue risk sets its next review to 'now', so the risk owner gets a new review task every day → **fixed**
  - In gov_risks.update, a review_at that is already past (<= now) is treated as unset, so clamp_review returns now + max_review(score) and the next review moves forward. A future review_at keeps the old clamping behaviour. The risk sweep test is extended: after the sweep, the owner (the CEO) calls risk.update, the new review_at is more than 13 days ahead, and a sweep one day later creates no second review work. The route signature is unchanged.

### g06 · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain` · branch `audit/fix-g06`

- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/hiring.py:204` · The month envelope is checked over the Jalali month, but the router enforces the same budget over the UTC Gregorian month → **fixed**
  - hiring.envelopes() used_month now reads llm-router's own counter: llm.usage with scope company/c.code, period 'month' and period_start = date_trunc('month', now() AT TIME ZONE 'UTC')::date. That is the same key the router enforces tokens_month over, and usage.py/advisor.py/reviews.py already read it the same way. llm.usage.tokens is the same total as before (input+output+cache_read+cache_write, from pricing.Usage.total). hires_month stays on the owner's Jalali month. work.budget (order_report.py) and the runner top-up now decide on the router's window without any code change of their own. Docstring and Envelope field comment updated; there is no Field description, so the openapi is unchanged
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/hr.py:454` · Promotion/demotion envelope check assumes committed_month already holds the latest estimate, but envelopes() only sums hire-card estimates → **fixed**
  - envelopes() committed_month now sums each active employee's estimate in force: the latest approved evaluation's change.quota_estimate_month, else the hire card's estimate, else 0. This is the same COALESCE as hr._current_estimate. Both now read COALESCE(amendments->'change', payload->'change'), so an owner-amended change counts the way apply() uses it. hr.check_approval and hiring.check_approval are consistent again, and a demotion frees room for hires. Test: test_p12b_people.py::test_an_approved_promotion_counts_in_the_envelope (two 600-token promotions under a 1000 envelope: the second is now 409).
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/hr.py:485` · Applying an HR promote/demote card that changes title_fa fails: hq_worker/hq_api have no UPDATE grant on core.employees.title_fa → **needs_migration**
  - Confirmed: no migration grants UPDATE (title_fa) on core.employees to hq_api/hq_worker, and title_fa is not in employees_immutable. The fix is a grant only, with no code change. A regression test (approve a promote card with change.title_fa, then D.apply_effect, then assert title_fa changed) should be added together with the migration. I did not add it, because it would fail until the grant exists.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/hub_ops.py:648` · A dispatcher allow-list refusal of a profile job is reported as 'no memory room' → **fixed**
  - result_fa(j, state, refused=None) now returns the no-room text for a profile job only when the refusal was not the dispatcher's own (i.e. hq.sh exit 3). A body.refused (e.g. not_allowed) gets the generic 'اجراکننده‌ی هاب این کار را نپذیرفت'. finish() passes body.refused. Test: test_w8_hubops.py::test_only_the_over_budget_exit_says_there_is_no_room.

The runs query keys on the line id, so it is scoped through the line. Test: test_p24_sales.py::test_a_line_caps_impact_reads_the_cards_own_company_only.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/kb.py:179` · Support KB search: the Python normaliser behind the tsquery does not match core.fa_normalize behind the stored tsvector → **fixed**
  - kb._FA/_DROP now equal core.fa_normalize v2 (0007): آ/أ/إ/ٱ→ا, ؤ→و, ZWNJ→space (no longer dropped); ZWJ, tatweel and diacritics are still dropped. Checked by hand: «ثبت‌نام چطوری؟» gives 'ثبت نام چطوری؟' and «آدرس آموزش آنلاین» gives 'ادرس اموزش انلاین', matching SQL. Other callers (desk._has, shadow.topic_of/select, similarity) normalise both sides, so they stay consistent. test_schema_rules.py::test_persian_normalisation now also asserts kb.fa_normalize(raw) equals the SQL result for every case.
- **high** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/knowledge.py:356` · Owner's new version of an approved knowledge entry always fails (unique index on approved ref) → **fixed**
  - knowledge.create(): when the new version will be approved (owner) and ref is given, _retire_older(tx, ref, version) runs before the INSERT. The retire after the INSERT is removed. Regression test in test_p11_charter_knowledge.py::test_a_new_version_of_an_approved_entry_retires_the_old_one_first (owner PUT v2 returns 201 approved, and v1 is retired).
- **high** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/knowledge.py:551` · Approving a later version of a knowledge entry violates knowledge_entries_one_approved; renewal flow is broken → **fixed**
  - knowledge._approved() now calls _retire_older before the UPDATE that sets status='approved'. It serves both approve() (the CEO) and apply_card() (the owner's seal of a holding entry). The status trigger already allows approved/in_review→retired. The same test also covers an agent v3: propose with ref, overseer pass, own CEO approve → 200, and the final rows are v1 retired, v2 retired, v3 approved. Route signatures and docstrings are unchanged.

It does not call set_status('open') and does not wake HQ-LGL. Regression test: test_an_out_of_office_reply_is_kept_but_neither_reopens_nor_wakes. The _reply helper in the test file gained an `auto` kwarg.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/lifecycle.py:1021` · An agent's lifecycle_rule card is not type-checked, and a bad trigger.sample crashes the whole support sweep for every company → **fixed**
  - Added a domain model, LifecycleTriggerValues, with the same types and bounds as routes.LifecycleTrigger. validate_trigger now validates against it, so the agent card path is covered too, and a ValidationError maps to 422 validation_failed with trigger.* fields. nps_due also falls back to NPS_SAMPLE when the stored sample is not an int, and clamps size to max(0, ...). Domain code only; no route signature changed. Test: test_a_rules_trigger_is_typed_as_the_owners_route_types_it.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/lifecycle.py:706` · Sends held overnight are not counted against the rule's daily cap when they are retried, so the cap is bypassed → **fixed**
  - The daily-cap count now joins support.messages and counts sends whose message was made today in Tehran time (m.at >= day start). A bound of s.at > start - 8 days keeps the growth_sends_rule index useful; retries only cover 7 days. Test: test_a_send_held_yesterday_counts_against_todays_cap_when_it_goes_out.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/lifecycle.py:750` · retry_queued always takes the 200 oldest held sends; rows that can never go out block newer ones of other companies for up to 7 days → **fixed**
  - retry_queued now leaves out companies with a lock (control.lock_state(company) IS NULL). It orders by row_number() per company, then at, so companies take turns in the 200-row batch. When a held send's rule is no longer active, send_template marks the prior queued row skipped with reason no_active_rule, so it leaves the queue. Test: test_a_held_send_of_a_rule_no_longer_active_leaves_the_retry_queue.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/lifecycle.py:804` · The quarter's NPS wave is created and used up even when the company is locked, and nothing is sent or retried → **fixed**
  - nps_due checks gate.automation_lock(company) before inserting the wave and skips the company while it is locked. The rrule's 7-day window lets the wave open after the release. Test: test_a_locked_companys_nps_wave_waits_for_its_release.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/lifecycle.py:884` · An NPS question that was never sent (held, message_id NULL) still makes the customer's next numeric message an NPS answer and closes the conversation → **fixed**
  - The take_answer pending-NPS query now requires s.message_id IS NOT NULL, so a question held by the daily cap or quiet hours no longer captures the customer's next numeric message. A question that was made but not yet delivered still counts. Status 'sent' is not required, which keeps the existing wave test valid. Test: test_an_nps_question_never_made_takes_no_answer.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/lifecycle.py:969` · A CSAT question held for quiet hours or the daily cap never sets csat_asked_at, so the customer's 1..5 reply is not taken as CSAT → **fixed**
  - send_template sets csat_asked_at = now() on the conversation named by on_closed's dedupe key ('csat:<ref>', scoped to the company) whenever it creates the CSAT message. This covers both the immediate send and a later retry_queued send, and it uses the original closed conversation, not the customer's latest one, without needing a schema change. The duplicate UPDATE in on_closed was removed. Test: test_a_csat_question_held_overnight_still_takes_the_score.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/links.py:172` · The canvas counts hidden sessions as «فرمان شما در جریان» → **fixed**
  - links.canvas now counts sessions with o.closed_at IS NULL AND o.hidden_at IS NULL, matching live.SESSIONS_SQL. test_w7_links now hides the session and asserts open_sessions == 0 and no «فرمان شما» in line_fa. No route signature changed.

  - meeting_flow.spent_today now sums agents.runner.EFFECTIVE_SQL. The import is local because runner imports meeting_flow. run_tokens now reads the four columns and returns work.effective_tokens (cache read at CACHE_READ_SHARE), so meetings.used and meetings.turns.tokens count what the runner caps. Because reserve, forecast and the war-room URGENT check go through spent_today, they now agree with the daily cap. Regression test test_a_turns_cache_reads_count_at_a_tenth_on_the_meeting added: a cached turn costs 13500, not 103500, and the meeting is not closed for 'budget'.

  - sweep_unanswered now excludes already-told threads in SQL with NOT EXISTS (a notify.notifications row with the same company, link_kind 'thread' and link_ref, created_at >= the message), so told threads no longer fill the LIMIT. The per-row _told_since check was redundant and is removed, along with the unused typing.Any import. Threads skipped while a run is 'running' are not excluded in SQL; that state is transient. Test test_told_threads_do_not_fill_the_sweeps_limit added (limit=1, two sweeps tell two threads).
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/notes.py:177` · before_expiry reminder text hard-codes 6 hours though the threshold is configurable → **fixed**
  - notes._body takes before_expiry_h (default 6) and formats it in Persian digits, e.g. «کمتر از ۲۴ ساعت». remind() passes cfg['before_expiry_h']. Pure test test_before_expiry_text_follows_the_owners_threshold added to tests/test_p12b_cards.py.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/notify.py:686` · Away summary per-company counts include handler-only notifications the owner never sees → **fixed**
  - notify.away_summary: the per-company decisions_new subquery now filters d.owner_visible and notifications_new filters n.owner_visible, matching the totals and top items. Test test_away_summary_companies_count_only_what_the_owner_sees added to tests/test_notifications.py. The response shape is unchanged.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/office.py:60` · Office «this month» money is cut at the UTC month start, not the owner's → **fixed**
  - office.money now cuts the month at the first of the month in the owner's zone, using gov_common.owner_tz/zone and converting to UTC, as the treasury's default range does. Test test_the_offices_month_starts_in_the_owners_zone added to tests/test_w7_office.py: it inserts two 2020 llm.calls rows around the owner-zone month boundary (they land in the default partition).
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/order_turns.py:253` · 11th+ ask in one open turn is dropped: its asker is never woken by the reply → **fixed**
  - order_turns.ask now uses the new keep_asks() with MAX_ASKS=10 in place of asks[:10]. It always keeps the first ask (turn.by) and the newest one, and drops an asker's repeated ask before anyone's only ask, so the current asker is always stored and woken by reply(). The session-activity line key still uses the uncapped count, as before. Pure test test_an_open_turn_keeps_the_first_and_the_newest_ask added to tests/test_w6_order_turns.py.

### g09 · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain` · branch `audit/fix-g09`

  - reply() now calls plan_from_owner with the original text (capped at 2*OWNER_WORD_MAX), not the whitespace-collapsed step text. The collapsed `words` is still used for the «مدیر کل» step. owner_word() passes the raw text to reply(), which collapses and caps it itself, and passes the raw text to plan_from_owner. The route's clean() keeps newlines. No route signature or docstring changed.

  - org.dismiss now falls back to ceo_of() when manager_of() returns None, and also when the employee has no department, not only when the manager is the dismissed employee. Moved work and the knowledge-transfer work now go to the CEO, as W.create does. Test: test_agents_more.py::test_dismissal_with_no_manager_left_and_a_note_of_ten_tags dismisses TECH-MGR first, then the employee, and asserts the open work and the KT work go to the CEO.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/org.py:366` · Dismissal fails when a memory note already has 10 tags (CHECK cardinality(tags) <= 10) → **fixed**
  - The memory copy now uses `CASE WHEN 'transfer' = ANY(tags) THEN tags ELSE tags[1:9] || ARRAY['transfer'] END`, so the result always passes CHECK cardinality(tags) <= 10 and never gets a second 'transfer' tag. Covered by the same test: a 10-tag note is copied as t0..t8 plus 'transfer'. No schema change.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/oversight_checks.py:318` · PR-050 opens a new finding every time the set of overdue work changes; the old ones stay open → **fixed**
  - PR-050 now uses one stable key per company, "overdue" (as PR-052 uses 'late-findings'). The refs stay in body_md and evidence, and _found_again refreshes the evidence. Findings already open under old 'overdue:W-...' keys are not closed by this change and need a one-off close by the owner or overseer after deploy. Test: test_oversight.py::test_pr050_keeps_one_finding_and_pr060_reads_a_last_day_certificate, which uses faked tx rows.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/oversight_checks.py:546` · PR-060 skips a failing site whose TLS certificate has 0 days left → **fixed**
  - PR-060 now skips a site only when tls_days_left is None or >= 14, so 0 days left is no longer read as 99. Covered by the same faked-rows test: 0 days gives 'TLS 0 days left', and None is still skipped while an incident is open.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/oversight.py:1132` · issue_challenge returns a 500 when due_at has no time zone → **fixed**
  - issue_challenge now reads a due_at with no time zone as the hub's local time (ZoneInfo(tz), the tz the route already passes) before comparing it with now(UTC). It no longer raises TypeError (500). ChallengeIn is unchanged, so the OpenAPI is not affected. Test: in test_challenges_and_compliance, a naive past due_at gets 422 with the due_at field.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/oversight.py:1307` · A `tool` hold whose ref has no ':' makes every gate call of that employee crash with IndexError (500) → **fixed**
  - target_company() now refuses kind 'tool' with 422 (fields target.ref: 'EMP:tool') unless the ref has a non-empty employee part and a non-empty tool part. holds_for_run() uses partition, and reads any malformed tool hold already stored as a hold on the whole employee (its hold ref) instead of raising IndexError. Test: test_a_held_agent_only_reads_and_a_held_tool_never_runs asserts that _hold('tool', mgr) gets 422.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/oversight.py:583` · A protocol version made from a `protocol` card silently switches a Jalali protocol to the Gregorian calendar → **fixed**
  - new_version(): when a ref is given and the input has no calendar (or calendar is None), it reads the latest version's calendar and keeps it. ProtocolPayload (decisions.py) gains an optional `calendar: Literal['gregorian','jalali'] | None = None`, which changes the decision payload schema and the overseer's propose section. The owner route _seal_protocol now passes body.model_dump(exclude_unset=True), so a PUT that leaves out calendar keeps the current one. Route signatures and docstrings are unchanged, but the ProtocolPayload schema changed, so the OpenAPI, if it includes card payloads, would differ when regenerated (not regenerated here).
