### g10 · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain` · branch `audit/fix-g10`

- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/payments_reconcile.py:133` · One on-chain transfer can be counted as `matched` for any number of ledger rows → **fixed**
  - payments_reconcile.reconcile now loads the window's ledger rows ordered by (paid_at, ext_id, id) and treats each (network, tx_hash) as a pool. The pool is seeded with the amounts of same-hash ledger rows older than the window. Each row is judged against seen.amount minus the amount already claimed, so a second row naming an already-used hash becomes amount_mismatch (chain_amount = what is left, possibly 0) with a dedicated Persian note, and it raises the usual alert. No new status, so no schema change. Regression test test_one_transfer_pays_one_ledger_row is in tests/test_p24_money.py.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/payments.py:559` · A ledger batch with has_more=true and no new cursor queues the same page over and over → **fixed**
  - validate_batch now refuses has_more=true with next_cursor null (LedgerBreak). collect() stops paging when has_more is set and the resulting cursor equals the one that was sent: rows are still ingested (idempotent), the source is marked error with a Persian reason that points to `paid_at > :since`, and has_more is reported false, so on_job_finished/read_ledger do not requeue. Tests in test_p24_money.py: a batch-shape case and test_a_next_page_that_does_not_move_the_cursor_stops_paging, which calls P.collect directly.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/personas.py:419` · A model or router outage is reported as 422, so the line step `persona_test` never retries → **fixed**
  - I took the alternative fix so the owner's route contract stays as it is (journey.py documents and tests 422, never 5xx, for a model that cannot answer). personas.py adds `class ModelDown(ApiError)`, and run_test raises it (still 422, validation_failed, DOWN_FA) when the model or router gave no answer. The persona_test adapter now retries when `exc.status == 502 or isinstance(exc, P.ModelDown)`. An answer that fails checked_results still fails at once. Route status, signature and docstring are unchanged, so no OpenAPI change. New test in test_p24_journey.py: with max_attempts=2, model down gives 2 calls and a bad answer gives 1.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/publish_desk.py:44` · Desk LIMIT 80 drops scheduled/done publications of older outputs → **fixed**
  - DESK_SQL now includes every output named in refs with no limit (refs is already bounded by JOBS=400). LIMIT :n applies only inside a subquery of recent attachment-carrying outputs (`d.id IN (SELECT ... ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT :n)`), and the outer query orders by created_at DESC with no limit. The regression in test_w22_look.py::test_an_output_he_took_back_is_ready_again monkeypatches PD.OUTPUTS=0 and checks that the cancelled 'done' card is still shown (adds an `import pytest`).
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/recurring.py:106` · work.schedules.next_at is overwritten with the occurrence just created, not the next one → **fixed**
  - create_run now also selects the schedule's rrule and timezone (LEFT JOIN work.schedules) and sets next_at with a new helper, _upcoming(). It keeps the occurrence just made while that is still in the future (activation creates the first run ahead of time). Otherwise it uses recurrence.next_at(rule, tz, now+1s), or None when that is past the escrow's expires_at or the rule has ended. Unit test test_the_schedule_shows_its_next_run_not_the_one_just_made is in test_org_work.py.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/rescue.py:200` · Linking a product-opened rescue case to a support conversation violates the immutable-column trigger → **needs_migration**
  - Confirmed: 0025 puts core.keep_immutable(..., 'conversation_id', ...) on growth.rescue_cases, and NULL -> uuid raises HQI01, so the product-case-then-support branch in rescue.apply_check (rescue.py ~200) can never succeed. No code change is needed once the trigger allows setting conversation_id once (NULL -> value). A regression test (product referral_failed case, then a referral.check 'not invitee' with a conversation, then assert conversation_id is set and support.conversations.rescue_case_id is linked) should be added together with the migration. Downgrade: drop growth_rescue_cases_conversation_once and growth.rescue_conversation_set_once(), then recreate growth_rescue_cases_immutable wit
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/revenue_board.py:263` · Revenue board 500s (IndexError) when every ledger period starts in a future month → **fixed**
  - revenue_board.build clamps `first = min(first, current)`, so months_d always holds at least the current month and _costs(at(months_d[0])) no longer raises IndexError. The regression is an extra assertion in test_the_revenue_board_on_fixed_data: build with now=2025-12-10 while all periods start in Jan 2026 returns one empty month '2025-12'.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/roi.py:183` · Company revenue in ROI is read one day early (UTC date of the owner's local midnight) → **fixed**
  - roi._figures now takes the local period days (start, end) and the owner's ZoneInfo instead of two UTC datetimes. It computes s/e = S.at(day, tz) for the time-based queries, and the revenue.days query binds the local days directly. Both callers in rows() are updated. The function is internal, so no route or OpenAPI change. test_p12b_treasury's ROI test now also inserts revenue on the month's last day (counted) and on the day before its first (not counted), and expects revenue 15 / roi 7.5 / net 13.
  - (schema part: in migration `0062_audit_fixes.py`)

### g11 · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain` · branch `audit/fix-g11`

- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/routines.py:48` · Substring "مرتب" in the owner's words switches an agent-proposed automation on without his seal → **fixed**
  - routines.asks_for_a_routine now matches whole words on orders.normalise text (ZWNJ/diacritics folded, Persian digits to ASCII) via orders.has. I dropped the bare «مرتب», which also means 'tidy' and sits inside «مرتبط»/«مرتبه», and added «مرتباً», «به طور مرتب» and «بطور مرتب». _EVERY is anchored with (?<!\w) so «شهر … روز» does not match. I added the no-cases «یک گزارش مرتبط با فروش هفته بده», «مرتبه‌ی», «مرتب کن» and «شهر تهران روز» to test_w20_order_gaps, plus new yes-cases. I checked the existing yes/no lists offline. One side effect: a word glued to a suffix without ZWNJ (for example «روالی») no longer matches.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/seo.py:188` · Competitor 'new pages' counts every URL whose lastmod is today, so dynamic sitemaps raise a false jump every day → **fixed**
  - seo.new_pages: when 90% or more of the sitemap's URLs carry today's or yesterday's lastmod (new constant STAMPED_PCT=90), the sitemap was stamped wholesale and only its growth since yesterday counts. The rule max(growth, fresh) is unchanged otherwise. Unit asserts are added in test_p22_growth. The integration test's FakeEgress stamps all 12 URLs with today's date, so its expected new_pages changed from 12 to 9 (growth from yesterday's 3). The jump still fires at 9 because the threshold is 5.
- **high** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/server_classify.py:173` · Destructive-command classifier is bypassed by common wrappers, so the owner's seal is skipped → **fixed**
  - server_classify._segments now unwraps nested commands recursively, up to depth 6: shells with -c (bash, sh, dash, zsh, ksh, mksh, ash, fish, including -lc and -xc forms), su -c/--command, eval, flock <file> -c or flock <file> cmd, busybox, docker/podman exec, docker compose / docker-compose exec (global and exec flags skipped) and kubectl exec -- cmd. It also: removes su's -c from the value flags; fixes value flags for flock (-x/-s wrongly took a value), timeout (duration popped after flags), xargs, env, stdbuf and sudo long options; strips quotes from words when shlex fails; handles `systemctl --now disable ssh` (verb found past flags), `service ssh stop` and `/etc/init.d/ssh stop`; adds --
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/servers.py:1070` · Refusing a destructive command inside a running Claude session leaves the session in 'waiting_approval' → **fixed**
  - In servers.on_decided, both the approved branch and the refused branch for a dispatched claude job now run UPDATE workshop.sessions SET state='working' WHERE state='waiting_approval' and emit the session state (_session_emit) when a row changed. The cancel branch skips this, since cancel() ends the session itself. test_owner_session_on_a_company_server now rejects the card and asserts that the session is working again and the job is running.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/servers.py:1335` · A NUL byte in runner output or in a read file makes the event and result inserts fail (500), so the job never finishes → **fixed**
  - Added servers.nul_free(), which recursively replaces U+0000 with U+FFFD in strings and dict keys. add_events applies it to the cleaned event data. finish() applies it to summary_fa, error, usage and data before anything is stored, which covers a read's inline content, exec results and publish data. Added test_a_nul_in_output_or_a_result_is_kept_as_a_replacement_character to test_p17_runners. Not done: runner-side base64 of NUL reads (services/company-runner) is not changed; the hub-side fix is enough for the job to end.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/servers.py:1336` · A NUL character in output or file content breaks jsonb inserts: events are dropped and read results never arrive → **fixed**
  - Duplicate of #4; the same nul_free fix covers events (the EventSink no longer dies on a 422) and results.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/servers.py:1444` · A claude job that ends while one of its commands awaits a seal leaves a stale pending server_destructive card → **fixed**
  - New servers._withdraw_seal(tx, j): for a job in awaiting_seal with a decision_id, it sets the pending server_destructive card to 'withdrawn' (decided_by service/system, note SEAL_MOOT_FA), unless another awaiting_seal job shares it, and emits decision.decided. It is called from finish(), cancel() and sweep(). sweep() now also fails dispatched jobs stuck in 'awaiting_seal' past timeout_s + grace; before, a claude job whose runner died while waiting for a seal never ended. This uses the existing 'withdrawn' status, so no migration is needed. test_owner_session_on_a_company_server raises a second card (dropdb prod), finishes the job and asserts the card is withdrawn.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/servers.py:1444` · A publish result that arrives after the hub swept the job as timed_out is discarded, though the post is already on Instagram → **fixed**
  - finish() now accepts a late good result (exit 0, no error) for a publish job that the sweep failed with error_code 'timed_out'. It applies _apply/_apply_publish and sets state done; a late failure is still ignored. A retry then sees 'done' and returns early, so the change is idempotent. The route's SPB.after_job runs again and now reports the publish. Not done: the runner's timeout still does not count the download time, and the sweep grace is unchanged. test_publish_through_a_runner is now parametrized late=[False, True]; the late run ages dispatched_at, runs SV.sweep, checks failed/timed_out, then posts the result and asserts the item is published and the job done. No route signature or do

### g12 · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain` · branch `audit/fix-g12`

- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/servers.py:1457` · Files of a timed-out or failed workshop turn are uploaded but never attached (the 'unfinished output' never appears) → **fixed**
  - servers.py: the non-answer parts of a claude result (session_id, files, files_skipped, sandbox, effort, files_changed and so on) moved from _apply into a new _claude_parts(). finish() now stores them as the result for a failed claude job as well (`elif j['kind'] == 'claude': result = _claude_parts(data) or None`). made_files() and wall_of() therefore see the out/ files of a timed-out turn, and the 'خروجی ناتمام کارگاه' deliverable is now made. Regression test: test_w14_workshop's timeout step now uploads a file and posts exit 124 with data.files, then checks the failed job's result and the unfinished deliverable.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/servers.py:1457` · A workshop turn that fails or hits its 60-minute cap loses the files it sent from out/; the 'unfinished output' path never attaches them → **fixed**
  - Same defect as #0, fixed by the same commit (eafb21ac). Sandbox and effort are kept for failed turns as well, so wall_of() and made_with() work there and the workshop.sandbox_off audit row is written. The answer text (result_fa) is still not stored for a failed job.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/servers.py:1799` · A dispatched claude job left in 'awaiting_seal' is never swept when its runner dies → **fixed**
  - sweep() now times out dispatched jobs in state 'running' or 'awaiting_seal'. A live runner ends a job at its own timeout, so this only catches jobs whose runner died. For an awaiting_seal job, the new _withdraw_seal() sets the pending server_destructive card to 'withdrawn' and emits decision.decided. on_decided already ignores jobs that are no longer awaiting_seal. New test: test_p17_runners::test_a_sealed_command_of_a_dead_runner_is_swept.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/servers.py:1804` · A studio publish job that times out or is cancelled never reports back to its media item → **fixed**
  - For kind 'publish', sweep() (code timed_out) and cancel() (its code: company_locked, server_detached, seal_refused and others) now call _apply_publish(tx, j, {}, False, code). The media item gets its error and publish_log row at once, and a publish_failed card when nothing is pending. runner_published returns early for jobs with no args.item, such as session publishes. New test: test_a_publish_job_that_never_ends_tells_its_item. The item setup of test_publish_through_a_runner moved into a runner_item() helper.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/servers.py:2118` · Oversight PR-130 keeps reporting links that are no longer measured (detached targets, the dropped port 22) → **fixed**
  - The network_links_hold query now keeps only links that are still measured: (to_key='hub' AND port=NETCHECK_HUB_PORT) OR (port = ANY(NETCHECK_SERVER_PORTS) AND the target row exists and is not revoked). test_pr130 was extended with a port-22 link and a link to a revoked target, which are not reported, and a 443 link to a live target, which is.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/servers.py:695` · A detached server comes back online when its approved but unclaimed pairing is claimed later → **fixed**
  - No schema change. claim_token() now refuses an approved pairing with 410 expired_token in three cases: it is past expires_at + CLAIM_SLACK_S (60 s); its server is 'revoked'; or a newer approved or claimed pairing of the same server exists (approved_at later). This covers both detach-then-claim and the re-attach variant. detach() itself was not changed: a revoked server's pairing is refused at claim time. New test: test_an_approved_code_claimed_late_does_not_undo_a_detach.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/servers.py:768` · Rotation is accepted with the previous token: a lost rotate answer followed by more than 10 minutes of hub downtime leaves the runner permanently stopped → **fixed**
  - No schema change: prev_valid_until is already granted. rotate() now sets prev_valid_until = now() + ROTATE_PREV_MAX_S (7 days). runner_by_token() cuts it to now() + ROTATE_GRACE_S the first time the current token is used. A rotate called with the previous token (current False) keeps prev_sha256 and only replaces the token the runner never received, so repeating it is safe. The route docstring of POST /runners/rotate was not changed; it still says 'ten minutes', which remains roughly right (counted from the first use), so the OpenAPI text is unchanged. The rotation part of test_pairing_codes_step_up_and_token_once was extended.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/session_memory.py:187` · Memory fold overwrites lines that remember() appended while the model was folding → **fixed**
  - _fold's write now re-reads the row FOR UPDATE. It returns {folded: False, why: 'folded_meanwhile'} if memory_upto moved. Otherwise it writes merged(old, current, model_text): any line in the current memory that was not in the memory sent to the model is appended after the model's text, then _fit trims to size. The race was previously a silent no-op that still returned folded: True. New unit asserts on merged() in test_w10_playbooks.

### g13 · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain` · branch `audit/fix-g13`

- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/session_publish.py:1426` · Publications scheduled more than ~8 days ahead are never checked, so the hub never learns they went out → **fixed**
  - FOLLOW_SQL now reads scheduled_at through a guarded LATERAL CAST (a regex check comes first, so a malformed value cannot break the query). It keeps a job while GREATEST(created_at, scheduled_at) > now()-8 days. It also drops rows not yet due (scheduled_at > :now, where now is the follow_up argument), so far-ahead posts cannot take up the LIMIT 50 batch before their time. Regression test in test_w19_publish_time.py: created_at is backdated 9 days and the post is still asked about at its time.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/session_publish.py:1461` · follow_up polls an unscheduled pending publication every 3 minutes forever: the 6-hour window keeps sliding → **fixed**
  - follow_up now writes meta.first_at into every check job it creates. The value is taken from the existing meta, or failing that from this job's finished_at/created_at, so older chains get a fixed anchor once. cancel() copies meta, so cancel jobs carry first_at too. The stop test is now now > (scheduled_at or first_at) + 6h. Regression test: a publication sent for now with no url stops being asked after 6h from its first answer, however recent the last check is. meta.first_at reaches the runner inside HQ_PUBLISH_META as one extra JSON key.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/session_publish.py:186` · A follow-up check job cancelled by a kill switch marks a held publication as failed, and it is never checked again → **fixed**
  - New HELD = (company_locked, server_detached). _state treats a check job that is 'cancelled' with a HELD code as waiting. FOLLOW_SQL selects cancelled check jobs and cancelled cancel jobs with HELD codes, and follow_up asks them again: the cancel is re-sent as a check, the same way a refused cancel already is. While the kill switch is still engaged, SV.create_job refuses and the existing per-row except only logs it. Covered in the same new w19 test, which checks that the state is not failed and that the publication is asked again.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/session_publish.py:997` · Standing-word publish has no row lock: after_delivered and the GroupsTick sweep can both send the same package to Instagram → **fixed**
  - Added lock_publication(), which runs pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended('publish:'+publication_key)). start() takes it before last_of. publish_run takes an advisory lock on 'publish-run:'+run_id before the 'sent' check, then the publication locks for each kind before TP.of_package. That is the same lock order a tap uses (publication, then topic), so the two paths cannot deadlock each other. Sweep holds the locks until its tick transaction commits. No test added: a race test needs two concurrent transactions.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/sessions.py:538` · GET /sessions second page crashes: the cursor timestamp is bound as a str to CAST(:la AS timestamptz) → **fixed**
  - cards() now converts the cursor with int(after[0]) and datetime.fromisoformat(str(after[1])) and binds id as a str. A bad cursor gets 422 validation_failed on fields.cursor. The route signature is unchanged. New test test_the_sessions_list_pages_on_with_its_cursor in test_w7_sessions.py pages through with limit=1 and checks that a bad cursor returns 422.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/shadow.py:91` · Support shadow report sends `pass_`, but web and Android read `pass`, so every shadow test shows as failed → **fixed**
  - ShadowReport.pass_ is now Field(serialization_alias='pass'), with model_config serialize_by_alias=True (already used elsewhere in the repo). as_payload uses model_dump_json(by_alias=True); the old pop('pass_') would now raise KeyError. Python code keeps using .pass_. The response shape of GET /support/shadow and POST /support/shadow/{company}/run changes from pass_ to pass, so apps/api/openapi/hq-api.v1.json must be regenerated (not done here). Test in test_p10_kb_lifecycle.py checks that GET /support/shadow returns 'pass' and not 'pass_'.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/skills.py:404` · New skill refs use lpad(nextval, 3), which truncates and repeats refs once the sequence passes 999 → **fixed**
  - skills.write_version now uses core.pad_ref('SK-', nextval('knowledge.skill_ref_seq'), 3). The function comes from migration 0046 and hq_api already has EXECUTE on it; the CHECK allows 3 to 6 digits. No test: reaching sequence 1000 is not practical. The same lpad truncation pattern is still in knowledge.py:360 (K-), kb.py:340 (KB-), lifecycle.py:240 (TM-), lifecycle.py:402 (LR-) and routes/orders.py:650 (F-). Those are outside this finding and were left unchanged.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/studio_checks.py:333` · The carousel slide-count check double-counts slides when two targets share a placement (for example an IG account plus a runner target) → **fixed**
  - platform() now checks each distinct placement once. It counts frames after removing duplicate outputs that share the same (name, file_id); outputs where both are missing are kept separately. Duplicate problems and repeated 'seen' entries are gone too. New pure test in test_p13a_media.py: with an IG target plus a runner target, 6 slides pass and 1 slide fails with (1).

### g14 · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain` · branch `audit/fix-g14`

- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/studio_checks.py:39` · The trading detector's «طلا» matches inside «اطلاعات», so non-trading scripts need a risk warning → **fixed**
  - studio_checks.TRADING: «طلا» now needs a word start and is not followed by ق, written as (?<![؀-ۿ])طلا(?!ق). «طلای»/«طلاها» still match, while «اطلاعات», «اطلاع‌رسانی», «اطلاعیه» and «طلاق» no longer do. I gave «شورت» the same left boundary, because it was hitting inside «مشورت». I checked these with re directly; a regression test is in test_p13a_media.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/studio_checks.py:46` · Script part headings are matched anywhere in the text, so a mid-sentence «کپشن» or «بدنه» splits the script and pollutes the published caption → **fixed**
  - PART is now (?m) line-anchored and allows an optional list mark (-, *, •, #, 1.) or **. I did not make the colon mandatory, because the existing test SCRIPT and the scriptwriter's format put «قلاب» alone on its own line. A heading must end with a colon (:/：, optionally followed by **) or at the end of the line. On the finding's scenario, body is now «… لینک ثبت‌نام در کپشن است و همین حالا بزنید.» and کپشن is «ثبت‌نام رایگان #آموزش». The old test SCRIPT still parses the same way. The part keys did not change. A regression test is in test_p13a_media.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/studio_publish.py:541` · A runner publish job that times out leaves the item waiting 7 days, then everything is published again → **fixed**
  - Three changes. (a) New studio_publish.after_job(hub, job_id): routes/servers.loop calls it, in its own try block, for every job the sweep failed, and it records a timed-out studio publish job through runner_published. (b) The wait for runners is now RUNNER_WAIT_S = 2 h instead of 7 days. (c) When publish_due picks up an item that still has pending runner entries, it settles them with the new _settle_runners and does not call _publish_one. A FINAL job is recorded as failed (its error_code). A job that never started (queued/awaiting_seal) gets SV.cancel(..., 'timed_out') and is recorded as failed. A job that is still running, or row-locked, extends the wait by 30 minutes. runner_published then
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/studio_publish.py:562` · A platform_limit retry posts the placements that already went out again → **fixed**
  - New _sent(item) collects the item.publish entries that have an `at` and are not removed. _publish_one puts those into results as ok, keeping their at, external_id, url, job and message_ids. It does not send them again and does not write a second publish_log row. card() also carries those entries over instead of blanking item.publish, so approving a later card does not re-send them either. A sender that died before the end-of-pass transaction still records nothing; that was already the case before this change. No dedicated DB test, because it needs a mixed platform_limit fake.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/studio_publish.py:827` · Unpublishing a Telegram album deletes only its first message but reports it removed → **fixed**
  - _send keeps every sendMediaGroup id as `message_ids` (a list of str) on the result and the publish entry. external_id is still the first id, and the URL is unchanged. unpublish_due sends one deleteMessage per id and marks the entry 'removed' only when every delete succeeded; otherwise it marks it remove_by_hand. A regression test is in test_p13a_media; the fake answers [43, 44]. The item's publish list now also holds message_ids. The API exposes it as list[dict[str, Any]], so the OpenAPI schema does not change.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/studio.py:1841` · A final render deferred behind a claimed preview is lost when that preview fails, and the item stays in 'rendering' forever → **fixed**
  - _render_failed no longer returns early for a preview that fails for good. It loads the item FOR UPDATE, and if render_meta.final_wanted is set it drops the flag and calls queue_render(final, by=SERVICE.code), the same as render_done's preview branch. A retried preview still returns as before. Regression test in test_p13a_media: a claimed job is turned into a preview with final_wanted, then fails with a runtime_violation; the test expects a queued final job.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/substitutes.py:1294` · An agent's call answered within the route's wait is delivered twice (inline and as a reply work) → **fixed**
  - deliver_to_agent now skips a call that ends inside the route's wait (created_at + SUBSTITUTE_WAIT_S + INLINE_MARGIN_S of 4 s), because the route answers it inline and sets delivered_at. New deliver_late(tx, hub), run from the worker's players_sweep activity (output key substitute_replies), sends the reply work for such calls that the route did not take. It covers agent calls with kind 'call' and purpose NULL or caller_work that are final, have claimed_at set and delivered_at NULL, finished inside the window, and were created within the last day. With substitute_wait_s=0, which is the tests' bench default, nothing changes. Pure unit test of _inline_window added. No route change.
- **high** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/substitutes.py:2071` · A web call left in 'running' blocks that player's web queue forever → **fixed**
  - New _web_lost(tx, hub) runs at the start of web_jobs' picking transaction, after the holding-lock check, as claim() does for the CLI. It fails via='web' calls that are still 'running' past claimed_at + timeout_s + WEB_STALE_GRACE_S (2400 s, enough for two other players' jobs ahead of it in the same pass), with state_reason 'timeout' and input_md NULL. It also marks a creative purpose as failed and calls _applied, so councils, agents and players hear about it. apply_web now locks the call and drops a late answer for a call that is no longer 'running', so it is neither failed again nor delivered twice. I did not raise the activity's 15-minute limit or the schedule's 16-minute execution_timeout

### g15 · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain` · branch `audit/fix-g15`

- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/substitutes.py:2101` · A call's file_ids are never sent to the player: 'animate' makes a video without the picked image → **fixed**
  - Hub side: web_jobs now loads the call's file_ids, restricted to files of the call's own company, as named base64 files. It adds an extension from the mime type, because the profile browser types an attached file by its name. They go to egress through a new `files` argument on EgressWeb.web. If a file is gone or belongs to another company, the call fails with 'file_missing' instead of running on the text alone. The CLI runner takes text only, so seat_call now skips a call with files on a non-web seat (reason 'no_files'), and refusal() maps that to a 422 validation_failed on file_ids. Egress side (substitutes_web.py): a job that carries files for a recipe task with no `attach` step now answers
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/substitutes.py:2104` · Any web seat call longer than 20,000 characters always fails at egress → **fixed**
  - Raised egress WebIn.prompt max_length (new constant PROMPT_CHARS = 200_000) and the profile browser's JobIn.prompt in browser_web.py to 200 000. That matches the hub's TEXT_LIMIT and the substitutes.calls.input_md CHECK (<= 200000), and _retry_language already cuts to 200000. So the limit is now the same on both sides. A site that refuses a very long message will now fail as a recipe step, not as a 422 every time. Egress test added: a 60k-character prompt is sent whole, and PROMPT_CHARS+1 gets 422.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/substitutes.py:2113` · Web calls skipped because a profile is signed out or expired never reach the agent that asked → **fixed**
  - _skip_web now UPDATEs ... RETURNING * and calls _applied(tx, hub, skipped), as the failed path does. This covers web_jobs (profile not signed in) and apply_web's expired branch (the current call and every other queued call of that player) and its off branch. deliver_to_agent, players.on_call, talk player.on_call, advisor.apply_opinion and apply_cross_check all handle non-done states already. Test added in test_p12a_substitutes: an agent's queued GPT web call, then the profile expires, then the call is skipped and a reply work for HQ-PA says 'was skipped: expired', with delivered_at set.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/waste.py:151` · no_progress_runs counts every finished run without a work item (message/schedule/meeting/alert) as waste → **fixed**
  - classify() now flags no_progress_runs only when run['work_id'] is not None. The module docstring is updated. A pure unit test was added to test_p12b_treasury. The fixed-data test's expected figures do not change, because all its no-progress runs have work items.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/watch.py:134` · The monthly holding proposal is claimed before it is made; any skip or failure loses it for the whole month → **fixed**
  - watch.sweep no longer claims holding_proposal:{month} up front. It checks that router is not None, that the owner zone is not spent, that the new dceo.proposal_on(tx) passes (enabled and model_predictions; holding_proposal uses it too), and that the month key is not already there. It then claims at most one try per owner day (key holding_proposal:{month}:try:{YYYY-MM-DD}, kind holding_proposal, which fits the key regex). The month key is written only after holding_proposal returns proposed=1. A skipped or failed month is retried the next day, and an unreadable reply does not use the zone every 5 minutes. No schema change. Test added in test_p19_watch, using a random far-future month because 
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/work.py:603` · uncap() on a child of a work that still has a token budget always fails with HQB01 (409) → **fixed**
  - uncap now walks the capped parents (FOR UPDATE, stopping at the first one without a budget) and sets them to NULL budget, root first, with an 'uncapped' work event on each ({budget_tokens: null, for: <child ref>}). Then it uncaps the work itself. The trigger no longer raises HQB01. Behaviour change worth noting: lifting a child's cap now also lifts its capped parents' caps, the same way add_budget raises them. Test added in test_w11_budgets with a three-level chain.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/workspace.py:277` · Lock-order inversion between hand_over (work then workspace) and after_job (workspace then work) can deadlock and drop a turn's result → **fixed**
  - after_job now finds the workspace (id, work_id) without a lock, locks the work with W.load(lock=True), then locks the workspace row FOR UPDATE with the same match on last_job_id/session_id and re-checks its state. That is the same order as hand_over, HANDS.start and stop. No regression test: a deadlock cannot be tested reliably without concurrent DB sessions.
- **high** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/workspace.py:306` · Workspace/workshop turn output is lost when the work's token budget is used up: add_usage runs before the budget bump and the HQB01 aborts after_job → **fixed**
  - New _book_turn. A non-hands work, or a work without a budget, gets add_usage as before. A hands work with a budget first has its capped parents raised by t, root first (as add_budget does), and then one UPDATE changes tokens_used and budget_tokens together. after_job runs this in tx.conn.begin_nested(), catches DBAPIError with sqlstate HQB01, and writes an Audit row (action workspace.usage_refused, A1, with the trigger message). The deliverable, the workspace state and the wake still commit. Other errors are re-raised. Test added in test_w14_workshop: a child that took all of its 100k-capped parent's remainder runs a workshop turn. The result posts 200, one deliverable is made, the workspace

### g16 · `apps/api/src/hq_api/routes` · branch `audit/fix-g16`

- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/routes/ads.py:139` · PUT ad-rule with an explicit null for min_spend_before_judging crashes with 500 → **fixed**
  - routes/ads.py edit_ad_rule now returns 422 validation_failed ({field: 'cannot be empty'}) when the body sets scope, window_days, min_spend_before_judging, action or state to an explicit null. This happens before the merge, the same way as campaigns.update_growth_campaign. The route docstring changed, so the OpenAPI description will differ (openapi json was not regenerated). Regression test added to test_p22_ops.py.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/routes/ads.py:142` · Loosening an ad rule's window or moving it to another campaign skips the ads.cap step-up → **fixed**
  - domain/ads.py loosens() now also counts two more changes as loosening. One is int(new.window_days) > int(old.window_days). The other is new.scope not in (old.scope, 'account'), meaning a campaign-scoped rule moved to another campaign; moving it to the whole account covers more and still needs no seal. Both now need the ads.cap step-up and are audited as 'loosened'. Unit test added (window, campaign-to-campaign, campaign-to-account), plus a route test that PUT window_days 7->30 without X-Step-Up gives 403. The edit_ad_rule docstring was updated (OpenAPI description changes).
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/routes/agent_tools.py:345` · company.state for holding roles returns other companies' department identity_md → **fixed**
  - routes/agent_tools.py: on the holding path, ctx.who.department is passed only for the caller's own company (c.id == ctx.who.company_id), so other companies' AIOPS etc. no longer expose identity_md/identity_version. Test added in test_small_fixes.py: HQ-AIOPS-MGR sees its HQ identity but not a new company's AIOPS identity.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/routes/archive.py:313` · Idempotent retry of POST /archive/records (and /versions) fails instead of replaying after owner uploads were taken over → **fixed**
  - Added idempotency.peek(tx, request, who). It is a read-only lookup of a completed key within 24h: it returns the stored JSONResponse replay, raises 409 like claim for a different body or a still-running key, and returns None for an unknown key. archive_create_record and archive_add_version call it in a short ctx.tx() before ST.intake/ST.ingest, so a retry with the same key replays the 201 instead of reading the destroyed upload (500). archive_import was left alone because it already ingests after its claim. Regression tests added in test_p14_archive.py: a new create-retry test, and a same-key retry of a new version inside the sealed-record test.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/routes/council.py:121` · Council «اجرا کن» fallback to a new order always fails with 409 idempotency_conflict → **fixed**
  - council._same_order now runs idempotency.claim and OT.reply inside tx.conn.begin_nested(). When OT.reply raises ApiError, a private _NoOpenWork exception rolls back the savepoint, which undoes the claim and any partial reply writes, and None is returned. place_order can then claim the same key for the new order. Claim conflicts (409) still propagate as before. Regression test added in test_w7_sessions.py: the order's root work is cancelled, the order is not ended, and «اجرا کن» gives 201 with a new F-ref linked to the council.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/routes/council.py:84` · run_plan crashes with AttributeError when place_order returns an idempotent replay → **fixed**
  - run_plan: when place_order returns a Response (an idempotent replay), the ref is read from the replayed JSON body and the council's order_ref UPDATE/emit still runs before the replay is returned unchanged. If the body has no ref, the replay is returned as is. No dedicated test, because the window is hard to reproduce in the suite.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/routes/decisions.py:140` · GET /decisions?kind= cannot filter erase cards → **fixed**
  - Added 'erase' to the kind Literal of GET /decisions (routes/decisions.py list_decisions). I kept it an explicit Literal rather than building it from D.KINDS dynamically, so mypy and OpenAPI stay static. A unit test in test_w8_erase.py asserts that the Literal's values equal set(D.KINDS), so drift is caught. The OpenAPI enum for the kind query parameter gains 'erase' (openapi json not regenerated).
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/routes/egress.py:549` · egress.recent returns a next_cursor that the endpoint cannot accept → **fixed**
  - The internal GET /internal/v1/egress/recent now takes `cursor: str | None = None` and passes it to _entries, so the returned next_cursor works (the route signature and docstring changed; the OpenAPI internal spec, if it covers this route, is not regenerated). The gateway's EgressRecentIn still has no cursor or limit field. Exposing them to agents would also need agents/tools.md (out of scope), so the tool itself still gets the newest 50 rows. Paging test added in test_egress.py.

### g17 · `apps/api/src/hq_api/routes` · branch `audit/fix-g17`

ValueError or TypeError from any of them raises ApiError(422, validation_failed, fields={cursor: invalid}). Added a test in tests/test_audit_g17_cursors.py. No change to the route signature or docstring.

  - routes/files.py transcribe now catches `except Exception` around workflows_client.transcribe. A WorkflowFailureError still maps by its failure type (TooLong is 413, SttBusy is 429). Anything else (RuntimeError 'no Temporal client', RPCError) becomes the 503 channel_not_configured, and that answer is stored on the Idempotency-Key, so a retry replays it instead of getting 409 'still running'. Added a regression test in tests/test_files_media.py. No change to the route signature or docstring.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/routes/governance.py:298` · Malformed risks cursor answers 500 instead of 422 → **fixed**

The parametrised test in tests/test_audit_g17_cursors.py covers it, including a time_id cursor from another endpoint. No change to the signature or docstring.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/routes/home.py:57` · «اجرا کن» retry treats create_order's idempotent replay as 'order not made' (409) → **fixed**
  - routes/home.py _make_order: when create_order returns a JSONResponse (an idempotent replay), the ref is read from the stored body if the replayed status is 2xx. Any other replayed status still gets the existing 409. Added test_a_run_retried_with_its_key_links_the_order_it_made in tests/test_p21_home.py.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/routes/home.py:98` · Two 'run' taps on one suggestion with different keys create two orders → **fixed**
  - No migration needed: the CHECK on state allows no 'running' value, so I did not add one. Instead, act_on_suggestion takes pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended('hq:suggestion:<id>', 0)) on its own connection (ctx.hub.db.raw()) and holds it for the whole act, via a new private _act helper. A concurrent second request waits, then sees state='run' with order_ref set and returns that row without making an order. The order and the link still use their own transactions, and the lock never touches rows, so there is no self-deadlock. Added test_two_runs_at_once_make_one_order (asyncio.gather) in tests/test_p21_home.py. The route signature and docstring are unchanged.

Its only commit is the send-back of a first unsourced attempt (attempts=1, state 'returned', 422). The word-limit 422 is still not counted as an attempt. A new letter_attempt(tx, work_id, body_md) is called inside the delivery's own transaction, after the attachment and require_citable checks. It increments attempts, keeps the unsourced figures and returns the mark, so a refused or failed delivery no longer uses up the send-back. routes/internal.py deliver_work was updated

Any value of 2**63 or more answers 422. Cursors already issued stay valid. ### g18 · `apps/api/src/hq_api/routes` · branch `audit/fix-g18`

- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/routes/messages.py:177` · An audit citation ref passes the isdigit() guard and then crashes CAST(:r AS bigint) → **fixed**
  - messages.validate_citations and domain/meeting_flow.check_claims now check an audit ref with AUDIT_SEQ = re.compile(r"[0-9]{1,18}").fullmatch() before CAST(:r AS bigint). Persian or superscript digits and refs of 19 or more digits are dropped and counted (or marked unsourced/not_found) instead of failing the whole post or turn. Unit regression test uses a fake Tx.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/routes/mgmt.py:626` · Promoting a whitespace-only note to an order crashes with 500 → **fixed**
  - promote_note with to='order' now raises ApiError(422, validation_failed, fields={text_md: 'blank note'}) when text_md.strip() is empty, before OrderIn is built. The work and errand paths are unchanged: work falls back to the ref as title, and errand accepts it.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/routes/money.py:156` · A chain read that fails leaves its Idempotency-Key 'still running' for 24 h → **fixed**
  - Added Idem.release(tx) in idempotency.py. The app roles have no DELETE on core.idempotency_keys, so release ages the unanswered claim: it sets created_at to now()-25h only WHERE status_code IS NULL. The next claim() then takes the row over through its ON CONFLICT ... WHERE created_at < now()-24h, and the worker purge removes it. read_payment_source's chain branch wraps read_now and the store in try/except Exception, releasing the key and re-raising. A 502 retry with the same key now reads again, and a success after that replays. Limitation: if the request task itself is cancelled, the key is not released. Regression test is in test_p24_money.py.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/routes/orders.py:790` · GET /orders crashes on every page after the first: the cursor timestamp is bound to timestamptz as a str → **fixed**
  - list_orders now uses pagination.time_id(cursor), so asyncpg gets a typed datetime and UUID, and a malformed cursor is a 422. I also fixed the same defect in routes/talk.py (talk_list_conversations), routes/workshop.py (list_sessions) and routes/servers.py (server_activity). servers.py's id is text, so there only the timestamp is parsed, with datetime.fromisoformat (422 on a bad cursor). No signatures or docstrings changed. Regression test pages GET /orders.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/routes/oversight.py:999` · deploy.freeze and challenge.issue crash with 500 when an agent sends a time without a zone → **fixed**
  - FreezeIn.until and ChallengeIn.due_at are now pydantic AwareDatetime in the api (routes/oversight.py) and in the gateway tool models (services/gateway/src/hq_gateway/oversight.py; its now-unused datetime import was removed). A zone-less time now gets a 422 instead of a TypeError 500. The JSON schema should be unchanged (string, format date-time), so the openapi file should not need regenerating; please confirm with the openapi test. Not changed: audit_control.py's /control/deploy-freeze FreezeIn compares in SQL, not in Python, so it does not raise this error.
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/routes/owner.py:386` · PUT /owner/presence with a zone-less `until` answers 500 instead of 422 → **fixed**
  - PresenceIn.until is now AwareDatetime | None, so a zone-less until is a 422. The schema shape is the same (date-time).
- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/routes/playbooks.py:84` · draft_playbook commits the Idempotency-Key claim before the model call, so any later failure locks the key for 24 h → **fixed**
  - draft_playbook now matches apply_playbook. The first tx only checks read_key(required=True) and the OR.ensure 404. claim() and the replay check moved into the final transaction that stores the draft, so a failure or cancellation after the model call leaves no unanswered claim. Trade-off: a retry after a success whose response was lost runs the model again before it replays, as apply_playbook already does. No route signature or docstring change. Regression test (store_draft fails once, then the same key gets 201, then a replay) is in test_w10_playbooks.py.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/routes/revenue.py:370` · revenue.read totals are silently truncated by days_list's 400-row cap → **fixed**
  - revenue_read counts the distinct accounts with day rows in the range. It passes days_list limit = max(1, accounts) * (days in range), so every row in from..to is summed. The by=day output keeps its existing last-120-days trim. The owner's GET /revenue/days keeps its documented 400-row cap. Regression test: two accounts over 250 days (500 rows) sum to 500.

### g20 · `apps/api/src/hq_api/routes` · branch `audit/fix-g20`

  - Fixed in two steps. First, ResolveIn gets a new field, `defer: bool = False`. With defer=true, _a3 only names the approved card and does not insert the 'approved' mcp_calls row. Second, a new internal route POST /internal/v1/tools/mcp/consume takes {server, tool, args_sha256, decision}. It repeats resolve's checks through the new shared helper _callable (server, company, seal, status, runnable), then _caps, then the CARDS_SQL card lookup and ensure_unlocked. It writes the card's single 'approved' row atomically and returns 200 {decision}. If the card is used, stale or does not match, it returns 403 policy_denied; a tool that is not A3 gets 422. The gateway now sends defer=true and calls /con
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/routes/work.py:531` · Cancelling a recurring order does not cancel its open runs → **fixed**
  - cancel_work's recursive CTE now starts from `id = :id OR recurrence_of = :id`, so the template's open runs and their sub-works are cancelled too. The schedule update also sets next_at = NULL. Docstring changed (this affects OpenAPI descriptions). The escrow stays 'active' as before; runs can no longer be created because the schedule is ended and the template is closed. Regression test: test_closing_a_recurring_order_closes_its_runs_and_schedule in tests/test_org_work.py.
- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/routes/work.py:576` · Completing a recurring order leaves its schedule active: the planner keeps showing phantom occurrences and a run can still be spawned → **fixed**
  - complete_work now calls recurring.end(tx, id), which sets the schedule to 'ended' with next_at NULL. As backstops, recurring.create_run now refuses a template whose status is 'done' (it already refused cancelled and failed), and the planner's template query adds `w.status NOT IN ('done','cancelled','failed')`. Open runs are not cancelled on complete; a run is the owner's separate item. The same test as #1 checks that the schedule is 'ended' and that the planner has no 'scheduled' occurrences of the template.

The same bug existed in three more routes and is fixed there too: orders.list_orders (time_id), talk conversations list (time_id), and servers.server_activity, which uses a new pagination.time_key helper (datetime + text key, because x.id is text). A bad cursor now returns 422. No signature or docstring changes. Regression test: limit=1 paging over four sessions, scoped to the test's workspace, added to test_one_writing_session_per_area_the_others_queue in tests/test_workshop.py.

### g21 · `apps/api/src/hq_api/__main__.py` · branch `audit/fix-g21`

- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/__main__.py:86` · API connects to Temporal only once at startup and never retries → **fixed**
  - apps/api/src/hq_api/__main__.py: the single connect in build() is now connect_temporal(hub). It makes one attempt, wrapped in asyncio.wait_for with TEMPORAL_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_S=20s so a connect that hangs cannot stall startup. On failure it logs a warning with error_type and returns False. When hub.temporal is still None, lifespan starts a background task, temporal_reconnect(hub). That task retries with backoff, starting at TEMPORAL_RETRY_FIRST_S=2s and doubling up to TEMPORAL_RETRY_MAX_S=60s, until it sets hub.temporal. It then logs 'temporal connected after start' and exits, and it is cancelled with the other tasks on shutdown. Because hub.temporal itself is set, every caller now gets the cl

### g23 · `apps/api/src/hq_api/archive` · branch `audit/fix-g23`

- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/archive/pdf.py:230` · Table layout loops forever when a header is taller than a page → **fixed**

### g30 · `apps/api/src/hq_api/workshop` · branch `audit/fix-g30`

- **low** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/workshop/commands.py:100` · Workshop command gate is bypassed by a newline, a single '&' or process substitution, so any command is auto-allowed, even under a read-only grant → **fixed**
  - The fix is in apps/api/src/hq_api/workshop/commands.py. SHELL_CHAINS is replaced by a SPLIT regex that cuts the text at every place a new command can start: &&, ||, |, |&, ;, newline, CR, a lone & (not the redirections >&, <& or &>), (, ), <(, >(, $( and backticks. classify() now rules every piece, so any deny wins over the rest. If every piece allows but the text contains a substitution (`, $(, <(, >(), it answers ask. needs() returns the union of what all the pieces need. A piece cut from inside a quoted string still gets ask from classify(). needs() reads such a piece word by word with the quotes stripped, so a split never loses an edit or a push. SECRET_HINT now also matches .env after a

- **high** · `apps/api/migrations/versions/0041_growth_channels.py:63` · 0041 adds kind 'newsletter' to growth.metrics_daily but not to owner.progress, so home.generate_progress violates the progress_kind check → **needs_migration**

- **low** · `apps/api/seed/web_residue.yaml:48` · Generic offer_starts cut a legitimate closing question from the middle of a sentence → **fixed**

### g36 · `services/company-runner/src` · branch `audit/fix-g36`

- **high** · `services/company-runner/src/hq_runner/claude_job.py:372` · A walled turn can switch off the wall for its next turn, and for every company for an hour → **fixed**
  - Fixed in sandbox.py and claude_job.py. prepare() now repairs the private area without following links: lstat, unlink, chmod then fd-based rmtree for folders the turn made read-only. If that repair fails, it renames the whole area aside and rebuilds it, so an OSError after that is the server's own fault and keeps the owner's fail-open behaviour. make_home and carry_session no longer write through a link the turn planted (copies use O_EXCL|O_NOFOLLOW and links in the way are removed). - **low** · `services/company-runner/src/hq_runner/claude_job.py:558` · Unwalled claude job passes the brief as a positional argv; a brief starting with '-' is parsed as a CLI option → **fixed**

  - In events.py, each batch is now also capped at MAX_POST_BYTES = 4 MB of UTF-8 JSON (always at least one event). A 413 halves the per-batch event limit and re-queues the batch. Any other non-fatal HubError (4xx) drops only that batch with a warning, and the sink stays alive. Only JobGone, Unauthorized and Revoked set sink.dead (and only Unauthorized/Revoked call on_fatal), so execute() still posts the result. Regression test in tests/test_run.py using a stub hub that returns 413 for bodies over 8 MB.

The hub's events insert uses ON CONFLICT (job_id, seq) DO NOTHING (apps/api domain/servers.py:1337), so a resend is safe. The module docstring was updated. Covered by the same test_run.py test (outage, then recovery, all seqs delivered in order).
- **low** · `services/company-runner/src/hq_runner/hub.py:281` · Hub.download raises httpx.ResponseNotRead (not HubError) on any error status, so publish jobs report a generic internal error → **fixed**

- **low** · `services/company-runner/src/hq_runner/net.py:286` · Route swap closes the shared AsyncClient while other jobs' requests are in flight → **fixed**
  - Route._swap() no longer closes the old AsyncClient right away. The client is kept in _retired and closed RETIRED_CLOSE_AFTER_S (3600 s) later, checked on refresh() and _swap(), or by Route.aclose(). New requests use the new client at once. Regression test in tests/test_net.py.
- **medium** · `services/company-runner/src/hq_runner/outbox.py:170` · Runner follows symlinks the session planted when it writes into the workspace or the turn's home (writes cross the wall) → **fixed**
  - Hub.download (via a new _create helper) unlinks what is at dest and creates it with O_WRONLY|O_CREAT|O_EXCL|O_NOFOLLOW|O_CLOEXEC, mode 0600. A destination it cannot use raises HubError(0, 'bad_destination'), which outbox.fetch and run_publish already turn into a JobError. Downloaded files are now 0600 instead of following the umask; the same user reads them, so nothing breaks. The carry_session part was fixed in commit 96b12221: links in the dst path components or inside the session folder are removed before copying, and the .jsonl copy uses O_EXCL|O_NOFOLLOW. - **medium** · `services/egress/src/hq_egress/proxy.py:165` · Every proxied connection has a hard 180 s lifetime, shorter than the substitute and profile-browser job caps it carries → **fixed**

A read timeout re-checks that clock instead of closing dst, so the tunnel closes only when neither direction has moved for IDLE_S (or the deadline is reached). A completed drain also counts as activity. A new test, test_a_tunnel_streaming_one_way_is_not_idle, passed in a stub harness (all 8 chunks over 1.6 s with IDLE_S 0.5, then closed by idle). - **medium** · `services/hq-common/src/hq_common/outbound_guard.py:484` · asks_secret is skipped when any negation word appears anywhere in the clause that holds the request → **fixed**
  - _asks_secret no longer drops a clause because a negation appears somewhere in it. A new _governed() decides whether a negation is about a given ask verb. English: a negation before the verb with only filler words between ('never send', 'nobody from our team will ever ask you to send'); a bare 'no' right before the verb is an interjection. Persian: a governing word (نباید, لازم/نیازی نیست, هرگز, نه, نمی‌توان...) before the verb with no break word (لطفا, و, پس, فقط, اگر...) between; a finite negative verb (نشد, نیست) governs nothing after it. In its own clause, a secret is decided by its own verb: the nearest English verb before it, or the nearest Persian verb after it. A negated Persian verb 
- **medium** · `services/hq-common/src/hq_common/outbound_guard.py:501` · profit_promise is cancelled by any negation word anywhere in the clause → **fixed**
  - _profit now calls _claim_negated(clause, start, end) instead of searching the whole clause for a negation. A claim counts as negated only by: an English negation right before it over filler words ('there is no such thing as', 'no exchange can guarantee', 'is never'); «هیچ» or «نه» immediately before it; or, within 5 words after it with no break word, an English negation over filler words that is not followed by a hype word ('don't miss', 'won't lose', 'not a scam'), or a Persian negation after Persian marketing idioms are removed (از دست ندهید, شک نکنید, دروغ نیست, جا نمانید...). ### g51 · `infra/provisioning/templates` · branch `audit/fix-g51`

- **medium** · `infra/provisioning/templates/_header.sh:64` · REBOOT_RC=100 collides with apt-get's error exit code: a failed apt step is marked done and the server reboots → **fixed**
  - Removed REBOOT_RC=100 from infra/provisioning/templates/_header.sh. A step now asks for the reboot with a marker file, REBOOT_MARK=$STATE/reboot-requested. run_step clears the marker before each step. Any non-zero exit, 100 included, is now a failure: the marker is cleared, the result is ok=0 and $STATE/failed is written. run_step reboots only when the step exits 0 and the marker exists, and it removes the marker before rebooting. system-upgrade.sh now touches $REBOOT_MARK and returns 0 instead of returning $REBOOT_RC. The regression test is test_cloudinit::test_a_step_fails_when_any_command_in_it_fails in services/provisioner/tests. It now sets REBOOT_MARK, and step_boot touches the marker.

### g52 · `infra/openbao/provision.sh` · branch `audit/fix-g52`

- **medium** · `infra/openbao/provision.sh:251` · kv_exists treats any non-200 (5xx, 429, curl 000) as 'absent' and regenerates and overwrites live config, wiping every service-token digest → **fixed**
  - kv_exists in infra/openbao/provision.sh now has three outcomes, like kv-once.sh: 200 means the path exists and 404 means it is absent. Any other answer stops the run with die and nothing is written. That covers 5xx, 429 and a lost connection (curl prints 000). On a 404 it records the version that a check-and-set write must name in KV_CAS[path]: 0 for a path that was never written, or N from the metadata of a soft-deleted path. A new helper, kv_create, writes with options.cas set to that version. If OpenBao refuses the check-and-set, the run stops and nothing is overwritten. Every create-if-absent write now uses kv_create: llm-router/callers, llm-connections/*, hq-api/config, hq-api/webpush, 

### g53 · `infra/openbao/policies` · branch `audit/fix-g53`

- **low** · `infra/openbao/policies/hq-admin.hcl:12` · The 'not root' admin policy can grant itself anything (write any ACL policy plus mint an AppRole), contrary to its stated limits → **fixed**
  - Fixed with the finding's fallback option: the comment is now accurate. The policy itself is unchanged. Narrowing it is not workable. provision.sh logs in with the admin token on every `make up` and PUTs every policy file under infra/openbao/policies, hq-admin.hcl included. It also writes all the AppRoles. So the token must keep write access to its own policy, and limiting it to named policy and role paths would not close the gap: any named policy (for example hq-api) can be rewritten to `path "*"` with sudo, and its AppRole's secret-id can then be read. Moving policy writes to the root token only would break the routine provisioning flow, and the root token is kept by owner decision anyway.

- **medium** · `apps/api/src/hq_api/fresh_start.py:411` · The fresh start deletes the owner's session files that kept talk.turns still list (array references are not protected) → **fixed**
  - In the $files$ DO block of fresh_start.body(), right after the kept-FK pass, a new loop goes over every uuid[] column (pg_attribute atttypid = 'uuid[]', not dropped, regular or partitioned top-level tables) of every table in KEEP+ASK. This protects talk.turns.files, lines.assets.files and knowledge.skill_versions.resources, and also any kept uuid[] column a later migration adds.
