## Part 2: apps and agents (309): to fix in the consult session


### apps/android

- **high** · ui · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/core/net/Models21.kt:466` · Owner's server terminal glues all output lines together (the runner sends lines without '\n'), and blank lines or errors print raw JSON
  - What: The runner emits each stdout/stderr line without its newline (services/company-runner/src/hq_runner/proc.py:46 `on_line(pending[:nl]...)`, jobs.py:90 `{"text": line}`). The hub forwards that as `event: stdout data: {"text": "..."}` (routes/servers.py:1069). `TerminalBuffer.out` only ends a line on '\n', so every line is appended to `pending` and the whole output becomes one line, which is only flushed at exit. In addition, `termEventOf.text()` uses `o?.str("text")`, which drops blank values (`isNotBlank`), so an empty output line falls back to `m.data` and the literal `{"text":""}` is printed. The hub's `error` event carries `message` (servers.py:1080, 1090), not `message_fa`/`detail`, so failures also print the raw JSON body.
  - Fix: In termEventOf, read `text` without the blank filter (`(o?.get("text") as? JsonPrimitive)?.contentOrNull`), and append "\n" for stdout/stderr events, because each event is one line. Read `o.str("message")` in the error branch.
- **high** · correctness · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/knowledge/Knowledge.kt:157` · Knowledge list crashes with duplicate LazyColumn key once any entry has two versions
  - What: `for (e in items) item(key = e.ref) { EntryCard(e) ... }`. The server keeps one row per version in knowledge.entries (same `ref`, version+1, see domain/knowledge.py INSERT with COALESCE(:ref, ...) and `_retire_older`). GET /knowledge (routes/knowledge.py list_knowledge) selects every row with no latest-version filter, and it only hides `expired`, not `retired`. So an entry with v1 retired and v2 approved (or v1 approved and v2 in_review) comes back twice with the same ref.
  - Fix: Use a key that is unique per row, such as `item(key = "${e.ref}@${e.version}")`. Better still, show only the current version per ref by deduplicating client-side (`items.distinctBy { it.ref }`) or filtering server-side.
- **high** · correctness · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/support/Support.kt:707` · The first shadow test can never be started: «اجرای آزمون» is disabled until a report exists
  - What: `HqButton("اجرای آزمون", …, enabled = r != null, …)`. `r` comes from `GET /support/shadow`, which returns `SELECT DISTINCT ON (company_id) * FROM support.shadow_runs` (routes/support.py:624). A company that only imported its history has a suite but no shadow run, so `r == null` and the button stays disabled. The upload toast at line 643 even tells the owner «حالا اجرا کن».
  - Fix: Enable the button once the company has a suite: track a successful `supportHistory` (for example a `remember` flag set in `send`, or the HistoryOut.suite), or simply drop the `r != null` condition and let the server's 4xx answer for a company with no suite.
- **high** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/v30/Lines.kt:123` · Template gallery can never load: outputs_fa is a list on the hub, a String in the app
  - What: rememberTemplates() calls Api.lineTemplates(), which decodes each item into LineTemplate (core/net/Models30.kt:38 `val outputsFa: String = ""`). The hub's LineTemplate (apps/api/src/hq_api/lines/templates.py:84) declares `outputs_fa: list[str]` and its validator turns even a plain string into a list, so GET /line-templates always sends a JSON array. Decoding a JsonArray into a String throws a JsonDecodingException. Api.decoded() turns that into an ApiException. Lines.kt:158 then uses `t.outputsFa.isNotBlank()`. The demo (DemoV30.kt:281) and V30Test send a string, so the tests never catch this.
  - Fix: Change LineTemplate.outputsFa to `List<String> = emptyList()` (or use a lenient serializer that accepts both shapes). Render it at Lines.kt:158 as `t.outputsFa.joinToString("، ")`, and update the demo fixture and V30Test to send an array.
- **high** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/v31/Kits.kt:62` · «شرکت آماده» list can never decode: kit groups/kpis/connections are objects
  - What: kits() decodes /kits into Kit (Models31.kt:38-48), whose `groups`, `kpis` and `connections` are List<String>. The hub's route returns list[K.KitSummary] (routes/kits.py:72). In KitSummary (domain/kits.py:206-218) these fields are list[KitGroup{key,title_fa}], list[KitKpi{key,name_fa,card,unit,manual}] and list[KitConnection{kind,label_fa}], all objects. The seed kits have non-empty groups, kpis and connections (seed/kits/content_brand.yaml:17-44), so decoding always throws.
  - Fix: Model the hub's shape: `groups: List<KitGroup>`, `kpis: List<KitKpi>`, `connections: List<KitConnection>` (each with defaults). In KitCard and KitPreviewBody, show `titleFa`, `nameFa` and `labelFa`.
- **medium** · correctness · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/core/AppGraph.kt:210` · Home-screen widget refresh is cancelled by the next stream event (collectLatest + early return)
  - What: `stream.events.collectLatest { e -> if (!e.type.startsWith("decision.")) return@collectLatest; delay(3_000); runCatching { api.inbox() }... CartableWidget.update(...) }`. collectLatest cancels the running block on EVERY new emission, including the non-decision events that return immediately. The hub emits `decision.created` and then `notification.created` in the same transaction (apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/decisions.py:1324 then :1453), and both topics are on the alert lane, so the notification arrives milliseconds later and kills the pending 3 s delay. No widget update happens. With the app closed this is the widget's only update path (Shell.kt:386 only runs while the app is open). An urgent notification on its own never updates the widget either, because only `decision.` events are considered.
  - Fix: Filter before debouncing so that unrelated events cannot cancel the wait: `stream.events.filter { it.type.startsWith("decision.") || it.type.startsWith("notification.") }.collectLatest { delay(3_000); ... }`. Each matching event then restarts the 3 s window, which is the intended burst debounce.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/core/data/V30.kt:250` · Clearing a line's schedule or a cap is silently not applied (schedule null means 'unchanged' on the hub; null caps fall back to hub defaults)
  - What: `lineBody` writes `out["schedule"] = schedule?.let { JsonPrimitive(it) } ?: JsonNull`. LineScreen.kt:208 passes `schedule.trim().ifBlank { null }`. In the hub's PUT /lines/{ref} (routes/lines.py:327-330), a null `schedule` means 'unchanged'; removing a schedule needs `clear_schedule: true` (LinePut, lines.py:84), which the app never sends. Caps have a related problem: `out["caps"] = Api.json.encodeToJsonElement(LineCaps.serializer(), caps)` uses `explicitNulls=false`, so an emptied CapField (LineCaps field = null, which the app treats as 'no limit') is left out of the body. The hub's `Caps` model then fills its defaults (runs_per_day 1, tokens_per_run 200000, opus_per_day 1). Meanwhile `capsRaised` treats null over a number as a rise and asks for the passkey.
  - Fix: In lineBody: when schedule is null, put `clear_schedule: true` (and leave `schedule` out). For caps, either do not allow null in CapsEditor (the hub has no 'no limit'), or encode with every field written and reject a blank field before saving. Make capsRaised match the hub's caps_rise.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/core/net/Api.kt:1260` · unlink decodes an ArchiveRecord from DELETE /archive/records/{ref}/links, which answers 204 with no body: a successful unlink is always reported as an error
  - What: `suspend fun unlink(...): ArchiveRecord = call(Request.Builder()...delete().build(), serializer())`. The hub route is `@router.delete("/archive/records/{ref}/links", status_code=204)` (routes/archive.py:581), and OpenAPI lists no content. `call()` runs `json.decodeFromString(s, "")`, which throws, and `mismatch()` turns it into `ApiException(200, "contract_mismatch", ...)` plus an error-log entry.
  - Fix: Treat the call as a Unit: `execute(req).use { check(it) }`. Then re-read the record with `record(ref)` (or return Unit and let the caller refresh).
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/core/net/Api.kt:1467` · Editing a rule always fails with 422: both clients send `text`, which PUT /rules/{ref} forbids
  - What: The route at apps/api/src/hq_api/routes/rules.py:128 takes `RuleEdit(Model)` with `extra="forbid"` (schemas.py:18) and only the fields `text_md` and `applies_to`. Android putRule sends `{"text": ..., "text_md": ...}`, and `text` is an extra key, so it gets 422. Web apps/web/src/lib/api/v29.ts:243 sends only `{ text: v.text }`: the extra key gives 422, and without text_md or applies_to the model validator also fails.
  - Fix: Android: `buildJsonObject { put("text_md", text) }`. Web: `call<Rule>('PUT', ..., { text_md: v.text }, ...)`. Or accept `text` as an alias in RuleEdit.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/core/net/Api.kt:1469` · putRule sends a `text` field that PUT /rules/{ref} forbids, so every rule edit fails with 422 after the passkey
  - What: `val body = json.encodeToString(JsonElement.serializer(), buildJsonObject { put("text", text); put("text_md", text) })`. The hub's `RuleEdit(Model)` (apps/api/src/hq_api/routes/rules.py:43, `Model` = `ConfigDict(extra="forbid")`, OpenAPI `additionalProperties: false`) has only `text_md` and `applies_to`. The extra `text` key is rejected by validation.
  - Fix: Send only `text_md`: `buildJsonObject { put("text_md", text) }`.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/core/net/Api.kt:1620` · newNewsletter sends `body_md`/`list`/`subject_b`, but the hub requires `body` (and `list_id`), so creating a campaign always fails
  - What: `post("/companies/${seg(code)}/newsletter/campaigns", buildJsonObject { put("list", list); put("subject", subject); if (!subjectB.isNullOrBlank()) put("subject_b", subjectB); put("body_md", bodyMd) })`. The hub's `NewsletterCampaignIn` (apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/newsletter.py:88) is `subject: str` (required), `body: str` (required, min_length 1), `content_type`, `list_id: UUID | None`. Pydantic's default `extra=ignore` drops `list`, `subject_b` and `body_md`, and the required `body` is missing.
  - Fix: Send `body` (with `content_type: "markdown"`) and `list_id` (the list's UUID). Drop `subject_b`, or add A/B support on the hub first.
- **medium** · error-handling · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/core/net/Api.kt:2054` · An IOException while reading a response body escapes as a raw IOException, not ApiException, and crashes UI coroutines that catch only ApiException
  - What: In `call()`, `val text = r.body.string()` sits outside any IOException handling. `execute()` only wraps failures that happen before the headers arrive. The same applies to `writeLoose`, `request`, `recordFile` and `transcript`. More than 60 UI sites use `scope.launch { try { env.api.x() } catch (e: ApiException) { ... } }` with rememberCoroutineScope, for example Notifications.kt:233, OrderSession.kt:1015/1029, Canvas.kt:207 and Outputs.kt:416. Those coroutines have no handler.
  - Fix: In `call()` (and the other body readers), wrap the body read: `val text = try { r.body.string() } catch (e: IOException) { hub?.transient(); throw ApiException.offline().also { it.logged = failures?.record(ApiFailure("network", ...)) } }`.
- **medium** · correctness · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/core/net/HubGaps.kt:111` · routeMissing treats the hub's own detail-less 404 `not_found` as a missing route, hiding whole features for the rest of the app start
  - What: `status == 404 && (detail == null || detail == "Not Found")`. The hub's generic not-found is `tx.one(..., not_found="not_found")` → `ApiError(404, "not_found")` with `detail=None` (apps/api/src/hq_api/db.py:49), and `problem_response` uses `model_dump(exclude_none=True)`, so the Problem has no `detail`. The app's own `workOne` (Api.kt:364) also throws `ApiException(404, "not_found", ...)` with no detail. `gated()` then calls `gaps.mark(f)`.
  - Fix: Count only the framework's 404 as a missing route: `status == 405 || (status == 404 && detail?.trim().equals("Not Found", true))`. Or compare against a known route-missing signal (no `code`, or code `not_found` with detail "Not Found"). Never treat a null detail as a missing route.
- **medium** · data-loss · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/core/net/Models15.kt:190` · Saving the site list silently resets intervals to 300 s and wipes each site's custom `expect`; the 30-second option is always refused
  - What: `SiteIn.intervalS` defaults to 60. putSites encodes with `json`, which has encodeDefaults=false, so a 60 value is left out of the body and the hub applies its own default of 300 (routes/runs.py:326 `interval_s: int = Field(default=300, ge=60)`). Neither `Site` nor `SiteIn` carries `expect`, so every PUT sends no expect and the hub writes DEFAULT_EXPECT into every listed row (runs.py:325, 427-436). Sites.kt:184 offers a 30-second chip, but the hub requires ge=60.
  - Fix: Encode the body with `jsonBody` (encodeDefaults) or give `intervalS` no default. Add `expect: JsonObject` to Site and SiteIn and carry it through. Remove the 30 s option, or clamp it to ≥ 60.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/core/net/Models16.kt:270` · ShadowReport.pass is never set: the hub sends `pass_`, so every shadow test shows as failed
  - What: The hub's model is `pass_: bool` (domain/shadow.py:91), with no alias. GET /support/shadow and POST /support/shadow/{co}/run use `response_model=shadow.ShadowReport` (routes/support.py:623, 640), so the wire key is `pass_`. Only `as_payload()` renames it, and that is not used for these routes. The app reads `pass` and defaults it to false.
  - Fix: Map the field with `@SerialName("pass_") val pass: Boolean = false` (or `@JsonNames("pass","pass_")`), or fix the hub to serialize it as `pass`.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/core/net/Models19.kt:122` · SeriesTarget drops `server`/`command`, so editing a series that publishes through a company runner fails
  - What: The hub's SeriesTarget (routes/media.py:113-123) carries `server` and `command` for `channel: "runner"` targets (§26.1b). The Kotlin SeriesTarget has only channel/account/placement. Series.kt passes `s.targets` straight into SeriesIn for PUT /media/series/{ref}. check_series (domain/studio.py:252-257) then rejects a runner target with an empty server and command.
  - Fix: Add `val server: String? = null, val command: String? = null` to SeriesTarget (and make `account` nullable with a default), so the targets round-trip unchanged.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/core/net/Models29.kt:37` · OrderRoute29 drops `kit`/`kit_name_fa`, so a «new company» order sent through the secretary preview is refused
  - What: The hub's dispatch Route (domain/dispatch.py:96-98) has `kind: "kit"` with `kit` and `kit_name_fa`. OrderRoute29 has `kind` but no `kit` field, so ignoreUnknownKeys discards it on decode. VoiceOrder.kt:284 always sends the preview route back through `order(..., route)`. check_route (dispatch.py:644) then raises `422 route.kit unknown kit` because `r.kind == "kit"` and `r.kit` is None.
  - Fix: Add `val kit: String? = null` and `val kitNameFa: String? = null` to OrderRoute29, so the route round-trips unchanged.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/core/net/Models30.kt:90` · LineVersion.by is String but the hub sends an object, so the line version history never loads
  - What: `val by: String? = null`, but lines/store.py:80 has `by: By` (an object `{kind, code}`, which the spec also shows). Decoding an object into a String throws, so `lineVersions()` (Api.kt:1550 via `items`) always fails.
  - Fix: Use `val by: Actor? = null` (or JsonElement) and show `by.code` in LineScreen.kt:442.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/core/net/Models30.kt:91` · Android LineVersion.by is String?, but the API sends an object {kind, code}, so the line versions sheet fails to decode
  - What: apps/api/src/hq_api/lines/store.py:80 declares `by: By` (kind, code). Android `data class LineVersion(... val by: String? = null ...)`, and kotlinx cannot decode a JSON object into String. The web types it correctly as Actor.
  - Fix: `val by: Actor? = null` (Actor already exists in Models.kt) and show `by.displayFa ?: by.code`.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/core/net/Models31.kt:38` · Kit / KitPreview / KitResult do not match the hub's KitSummary / KitPreview / KitCreated: the kit screen cannot load
  - What: `Kit.groups`, `Kit.kpis` and `Kit.connections` are `List<String>`. The hub's `KitSummary` (domain/kits.py:206) sends `groups: list[KitGroup]` ({key,title_fa}), `kpis: list[KitKpi]` and `connections: list[KitConnection]`, all of them objects. `Api.kits()` → `items("/kits", Kit.serializer())` therefore throws on the first object element. `KitPreview` (line 52) expects preview_fa/team_fa/groups/lines/kpis/connections/todo_fa, but routes/kits.py:44 returns {kit: KitSummary, code, name_fa, name_en, code_free, problems, nothing_written}, so every field the app reads stays empty. `KitResult.lines` (line 67) is `List<String>`, but `KitCreated.lines` is `list[KitLine]` objects.
  - Fix: Model the real shapes. Kit: `groups: List<KitGroup>`, `kpis: List<KitKpi>`, `connections: List<KitConnection>`, plus `team` and `todoFa`. KitPreview: `kit: Kit`, `code`, `nameFa`, `nameEn`, `codeFree`, `problems`. KitResult: `lines: List<KitLine>`, `linesPending: List<KitLine>`. Then update KitPreviewBody to read them.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/core/net/Models31.kt:153` · Ads: client models do not match the API's AdCampaign/AdDay (Android decode fails on float conversions; web and Android never see paused campaigns)
  - What: API AdDay/AdTotals have `conversions: float` (domain/ads.py:132,140) and AdCampaign is {platform, campaign_id, name, status: active|paused|removed, paused_by, totals, days}. Android AdDaily has `conversions: Long`, and pydantic writes floats such as `0.0` or `3.0`, which kotlinx cannot decode into Long. Android AdCampaign uses `id` (default ""), `titleFa` and `state` (default "on"). Web v31.ts:133 uses `id`, `title_fa`, `state`, `spend`, `revenue` and `roas`, none of which exist in the response.
  - Fix: Android: `conversions: Double`. Map `campaignId`, `name`, `status`, `pausedBy` and `totals` in AdCampaign and use `status == "paused"` in Ads.kt. Web: retype AdCampaign as {campaign_id, name, status, paused_by, totals:{spend,revenue,roas}, days} and use `c.campaign_id`, `c.name`, `c.status` and `c.totals.*` in company.tsx:218-224. Fix AdAccount (label_fa, state, secret) in both clients the same way.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/core/net/Models31.kt:155` · Ads models do not match domain/ads.py: integer `conversions` breaks on the hub's float, and campaign id/name/state are never read
  - What: `AdDaily.conversions: Long`, but `AdDay.conversions: float` (domain/ads.py:132). Python writes `0.0`/`3.0`, and parsing "3.0" as Long throws, so the whole `ads(code)` answer fails to decode. `AdCampaign` reads `id`, `titleFa` and `state` ("on"/"paused"), but the hub sends `campaign_id`, `name` and `status` ("active"/"paused"/"removed") (ads.py:145-154). `AdAccount` reads `accountId` and `company`, which do not exist.
  - Fix: Change `conversions` to Double. Rename the AdCampaign fields to `campaignId`, `name` and `status` (and map paused/active in the UI), and add `totals`. Align AdAccount with {platform, label_fa, state, secret, missing_fa, last_read_at, last_error_fa}.
- **medium** · crash · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/device/AppLock.kt:62` · App lock crashes on Android 9 (minSdk 28): API-29 method and no negative button
  - What: unlock() builds an android.hardware.biometrics.BiometricPrompt. On API < 30 it calls `builder.setDeviceCredentialAllowed(true)`, but that method only exists from API 29. On API 28 the call throws NoSuchMethodError. Even without that call, API 28's BiometricPrompt.Builder.build() throws IllegalArgumentException when no negative button is set. minSdk is 28 (app/build.gradle.kts). LockScreen (ui/owner/Device.kt) calls `open()` from `LaunchedEffect(Unit)` as soon as it is shown, and AppLock starts locked whenever the setting is on (`MutableStateFlow(enabled())`).
  - Fix: Use androidx.biometric.BiometricPrompt (it handles API 28 and device credentials). Otherwise, on API 28 call setNegativeButton(...) and skip setDeviceCredentialAllowed (or fall back to KeyguardManager.createConfirmDeviceCredentialIntent). Guard the API-29 call with `SDK_INT >= Q`.
- **medium** · security · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/HqRoot.kt:68` · Alert toasts draw on top of the app-lock screen and leak their content
  - What: The lock covers the Shell but does not remove it: `Box{ Shell(...) }; if (hide) Box(...){ LockScreen }`. The Shell keeps running its `env.alerts.inApp.collect` effect, which raises `toasts.decision/info/ok/error` with "${a.title}؛ ${a.text}" (ui/shell/Shell.kt ~355-366). `ToastHost(toasts)` is drawn after the lock overlay in the same Box, so the toasts sit above the LockScreen. Their action buttons can also be clicked and navigate the Shell underneath.
  - Fix: Do not render ToastHost (and suppress in-app toasts and the urgent banner) while `locked && !env.demo`: for example `if (!hide) ToastHost(toasts)`. Or collect alerts into a queue that is shown only after unlock.
- **medium** · correctness · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/archive/Intake.kt:122` · Intake secret refusal marks the wrong file as refused
  - What: The hub names the offending file by its id in the *value* of `fields`: archive/store.py:242 raises `fields={"file_ids": str(fid), "kinds": ...}`. The app instead matches file *names* against the field *keys*: `val names = e.fields.keys + e.fieldKeys` gives {"file_ids","kinds"}, and `it.name in names` never matches. It then falls back to `ups.firstOrNull { it.id == ids.first() }`, which is always the first file. The server has already destroyed the real offending upload, so the next `intake(ids)` that still includes it fails with another error, and the loop stops with `failure`.
  - Fix: Find the refused file by id: `val bad = e.fields["file_ids"]; val hit = ups.firstOrNull { !it.refused && it.id == bad }`. Fall back to name matching only if that fails, and never to `ids.first()`.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/archive/Records.kt:245` · «نزدیک‌ترین سررسیدها» never appears and every draft shows «0 فایل»: the list endpoint sends no reminders or versions
  - What: ExpiringStrip reads `r.reminders` from `records(RecordFilters(expiringWithinDays = 365))`. DraftsBlock reads `r.current?.files?.size` (line 305), and RecordRow reads `r.versions.size` (line 149). The list route renders each record with `full=False` (routes/archive.py:280). `render()` adds `versions`, `links` and `reminders` only when `full` is set (archive/records.py:233-249), so all three are empty lists in list responses.
  - Fix: Either have the hub include `reminders` and a file count in list rows (or add a `full` query flag), or build the strip from `dueReminders()` (`GET /archive/reminders`, which carries record, field, at and days_left). Remove the per-row file and version counts, or fetch them from the detail.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/archive/Settings.kt:304` · Saving an edited party always fails with 422: the PATCH body contains `kind`, which the server forbids
  - What: PartyContent.save() sends the whole PartyIn when it edits an existing party: `Api.jsonBody.encodeToJsonElement(PartyIn.serializer(), body()) as JsonObject` → `{kind, name_fa, name_en, identifiers, scopes}`. The server's `ArchivePartyPatch` (apps/api/src/hq_api/routes/archive.py:123) only allows name_fa, name_en, identifiers, scopes and status. Its base `Model` sets `extra="forbid"` (schemas.py:17), so the extra `kind` key fails request validation.
  - Fix: For a PATCH, build the body without `kind`, e.g. `buildJsonObject { put("name_fa", ...); put("name_en", ...); put("identifiers", ...); put("scopes", ...) }`, or drop the `kind` key from the encoded object. Keep the kind Select read-only for an existing party (the server cannot change it). The web client has the same bug in components/v20/parties.tsx:35.
- **medium** · rtl-persian · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/authority/Authority.kt:218` · Authority limit typed with Persian digits is silently stored as 0
  - What: `val clean = v.filter { it.isDigit() || it == '.' }` keeps Persian digits (`Char.isDigit` is Unicode-aware). `clean.toDoubleOrNull()` only parses ASCII digits, so it returns null and `?: 0.0` writes 0 into the draft: `set(JsonObject(limit + (n.first to numberOf(0.0))), actor)`. HqTextField uses the default text keyboard, so on the owner's Persian keyboard the digits are ۰-۹. Every other numeric input in the app (DateField, numOrRaw, Schema.latin) maps Persian digits; this one does not.
  - Fix: Map Persian and Arabic-Indic digits to ASCII before parsing, e.g. `val clean = v.map { ch -> "۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹".indexOf(ch).takeIf { it >= 0 }?.digitToChar() ?: ch }.filter { it in '0'..'9' || it == '.' }`. Don't turn an unparsable or empty value into 0: keep the previous limit or mark the row invalid.
- **medium** · ui · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/charter/Charter.kt:335` · Red-line pattern field eats every Enter at the end of the text: a second pattern cannot be typed
  - What: `lines[i] = r.copy(patterns = v.split('\n').let { xs -> xs.filterIndexed { k, x -> x.isNotBlank() || k < xs.size - 1 } })`. This drops exactly the trailing empty element. HqTextField is a controlled BasicTextField(String), so the value goes back to the text without the newline.
  - Fix: Keep the raw text in its own state, split and filter blanks only when saving (the save path at line 343 already filters blanks), or keep the trailing empty element (filter `k == xs.size - 1 || x.isNotBlank()`).
- **medium** · correctness · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/companies/Catalogue.kt:174` · Running catalogue crashes the page: section keys are not unique before the merge
  - What: `for (s in k.sections.filter { ... }) item(key = "cat-${s.key}") { SectionCard(s) }`. While a catalogue runs, domain/catalogue.py appends one section per finished job (one claude job per server and section, plus `map_sections` fallbacks and `_unread_section`). `merged()` folds them into one per key only in `finish()`. GET returns the unmerged list while state is `running`, so the same key (e.g. `products`) appears more than once.
  - Fix: Key by index or by key plus server, such as `itemsIndexed(...){ i, s -> key = "cat-${s.key}-$i" }`. Alternatively, merge sections by key on the client while `k.running`.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/feedback/Feedback.kt:220` · «جدا کن» (unlink from work) always fails with 422
  - What: `save(workRef = null, setWork = true, ...)`, then Api.patchFeedback sends `{"work_ref": null}` with no state. The server's patch_feedback raises `422 validation_failed` when `body.state is None and body.work_ref is None`. Even if that check were passed, the UPDATE uses `work_ref = COALESCE(:wr, work_ref)`, so a null can never clear the link.
  - Fix: Remove the button until the hub supports unlinking, or add explicit unlink support on the hub (e.g. an empty string or a `clear_work` flag handled without COALESCE) and send that.
- **medium** · correctness · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/founding/Founding.kt:220` · Founding's plan query freezes on the first founding snapshot, so the build, login card and activate button never appear
  - What: `rememberQuery("founding:plan:$p", "founding") { f.planDetail?.takeIf { it.ref == p } ?: plan(p) }`. QueryCache.get keeps the first fetch lambda for the life of the cache (`entries.getOrPut(k) { Query(..., fetch) }`). The lambda captures the `f` from the first composition, and the API always fills `plan_detail` (domain/founding.py:650). Every later refresh or invalidate therefore returns the same stale ServerPlan: `state=proposed`, `run=null`.
  - Fix: Fetch only by ref (`{ plan(p) }`), or put the founding's version or updatedAt in the key so a new snapshot gets a new entry. Seeding from `f.planDetail` should go through `q.set(...)` in a LaunchedEffect keyed on `f`, not through the fetch lambda.
- **medium** · ui · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/founding/Founding.kt:521` · PV plan screen redirects with a push on every entry: Back from the founding bounces forward forever
  - What: `LaunchedEffect(pl?.founding) { pl?.founding?.let { nav.go("founding/$it") } }`. NavImpl.go calls `nav.navigate(to)`, which pushes and does not pop `founding/PV-…`. When the owner presses Back, the PV entry is composed again, its plan is already cached, and the effect pushes the founding page again.
  - Fix: Replace the PV entry instead of pushing, e.g. `nav.back(); nav.go("founding/$it")` (as the wizard does) or navigate with popUpTo(current){inclusive=true}. Alternatively, render the founding inline without navigating.
- **medium** · lifecycle · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/media/Media.kt:208` · Video and voice-note players keep playing with sound after the app goes to the background
  - What: `rememberPlayer` builds `ExoPlayer.Builder(context)...build()` with no audio-focus handling (`setAudioAttributes(..., handleAudioFocus=true)` is never called) and only releases the player in `onDispose`. Nothing pauses it on ON_STOP: a grep of ui/ finds no `LifecycleEventObserver`, `LifecycleStartEffect` or pause-on-stop for any player. Every player in the app goes through this function: `VideoTile` (Media.kt:242), `AudioNote` (Media.kt:273), and `rememberFilm` in look/Files.kt:217, which `FileViewer`, `HeroPlayer` and `InlineVideo` use.
  - Fix: In `rememberPlayer`, add a lifecycle observer (e.g. `LifecycleStartEffect(player) { onStopOrDispose { player.pause() } }`). Build the player with `.setAudioAttributes(AudioAttributes.DEFAULT, /* handleAudioFocus = */ true)` and `.setHandleAudioBecomingNoisy(true)`.
- **medium** · concurrency · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/messages/Messages.kt:297` · Cancelling the SSE source fires onFailure, which wipes the FAILED state and can kill the next reply's live watch
  - What: OkHttp's RealEventSource (okhttp-sse 5.5.0) always calls listener.onFailure(IOException("canceled")) after `cancel()`. The listener's onFailure (lines 301-308) does `if (got) return finish()` (finish = live.end(): cancels `live.source` and every job in `live.jobs`), otherwise `live.state = LiveState.IDLE` and starts seven refetches. The listener writes to the shared `live` object, not to its own source. Three paths cancel the source: (1) "reply.failed" at line 297 runs `eventSource.cancel(); live.state = LiveState.FAILED`, and the onFailure that follows sets IDLE right away, or calls finish() if deltas came. (2) startLive for a second message calls `live.end(THINKING)` (line 255), which cancels the old source. The old listener's onFailure then runs on the OkHttp thread after the new source has been set. With the old `got==true` it calls finish(), which cancels the NEW source and its watchdog. With `got==false` it forces the new thinking bubble to IDLE. (3) reply.done or LaunchedEffect(lastAgent) runs live.end(), which starts 2 minutes of needless polling when no delta came.
  - Fix: In the listener, ignore callbacks from a source that is no longer current: `if (live.source !== eventSource) return` at the top of onEvent and onFailure. Also, when the failure comes after a self-cancel (state FAILED, or the IOException message is "canceled"), return without touching the state. Keep `got`, `finish` and the job list per source, not on the shared LiveReply.
- **medium** · correctness · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/owner/TreasuryTools.kt:192` · Clearing a budget's fields never removes the cap, but the app reports success
  - What: The sheet's subtitle says «خالی یعنی بی‌سقف» (empty means no cap). The save code keeps only scopes with a non-blank field: `scopes.filter { s -> tok[key(s)].orEmpty().isNotBlank() || usdV[key(s)].orEmpty().isNotBlank() }`. The server's PUT /budgets (apps/api/src/hq_api/routes/treasury.py put_budgets) is an upsert per scope that never deletes or disables a row it is not sent, and BudgetIn requires either token_limit or usd_limit. A scope whose fields were all emptied is therefore not sent at all, and its old cap stays enforced. If every field is emptied, `rows` is empty and the hub rejects the body (`min_length=1`). The day row has no USD field, so emptying a company's day token field always leaves the day cap in place.
  - Fix: For a scope that had a budget and now has both fields blank, send a row with `enabled = false` and the previous limit, or add a delete route. Never filter that scope out silently.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/risks/Risks.kt:320` · Editing an accepted risk always fails with 422 because the sheet sends status "accepted"
  - What: RiskSheet sets `var status by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(r?.status ?: "open") }` (line 283), and the PATCH body always carries `put("status", status)` (line 320). RiskCard offers «ویرایش» for every status except closed (line 257), so accepted risks get the button. The server's RiskPatch (apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/gov_risks.py:80) declares `status: Literal["open", "mitigating", "closed"] | None`. "accepted" can only come from a sealed card, so pydantic rejects the body. The status chips (line 305) offer only open/mitigating, so for an accepted risk neither chip is selected and nothing in the UI shows why the save fails.
  - Fix: Send `status` only when the owner changed it (`if (r == null || status != r.status) put("status", status)`), or leave it out whenever `status == "accepted"`.
- **medium** · rtl-persian · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/scorecard/Scorecard.kt:255` · Android KPI target typed with Persian digits clears the target
  - What: `val clean = v.filter { it.isDigit() || it == '.' }` keeps Persian digits, because Kotlin isDigit is Unicode-aware. Then `put(p.copy(target = clean.toDoubleOrNull()))`: toDoubleOrNull (Java Double.parseDouble) accepts only ASCII digits, so it returns null. HqTextField with figure=true sets no keyboard type, so a Persian layout types ۰-۹. putKpis sends the body with `jsonBody` (explicitNulls = true, Api.kt:2190-2194, "a cleared KPI target"), so the null is sent and clears the target.
  - Fix: Map ۰-۹ / ٠-٩ to ASCII and ٫ to '.' before toDoubleOrNull, as parseMillions in Hiring.kt:265-268 already does. Or reject the input instead of sending null.
- **medium** · rtl-persian · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/scorecard/Scorecard.kt:257` · A KPI target typed with Persian digits is saved as "no target"
  - What: The target field keeps `v.filter { it.isDigit() || it == '.' }`, and Char.isDigit accepts Persian digits. It then stores `p.copy(target = clean.toDoubleOrNull())`, but String.toDoubleOrNull returns null for "۵۰". HqTextField sets no keyboard type, so on the owner's Persian keyboard digits arrive as ۰-۹. The field keeps showing "۵۰" (from `text[k.key]`), while the patch has target=null. putKpis uses jsonBody with explicitNulls=true, so the null is sent and the hub clears the target.
  - Fix: Normalise Persian and Arabic-Indic digits and the decimal mark (٫) to ASCII before calling toDoubleOrNull, as parseMillions already does, or reject non-ASCII digits visibly.
- **medium** · correctness · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/servers/Bits.kt:435` · Three server-activity queries share one cache key but fetch different limits
  - What: QueryCache.get (core/data/QueryCache.kt:100) keeps the fetch of whichever caller creates the key first. ServersControlCard uses `rememberQuery("server:activity:${s.id}::") { serverActivity(s.id, limit = 3) }`. ServerScreen's ActivityTab (Server.kt:213) uses the same key `server:activity:<id>::` with the default limit of 50. Terminal (Terminal.kt:127) uses `server:activity:<id>:owner:` with limit=20, which is the same key ActivityTab builds for «فقط من» (default 50).
  - Fix: Put the limit in the key, for example `server:activity:${s.id}::3` for the control card and `server:activity:${s.id}:owner::20` for the terminal, so that each different fetch has its own key.
- **medium** · correctness · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/session/OrderSession.kt:424` · Decision-bar close reuses one Idempotency-Key for the life of the screen, so a second close after reopening is replayed instead of performed
  - What: `val closeKey = remember(ref) { UUID.randomUUID().toString() }` is never renewed after a successful `closeNow()` (unlike `nudgeKey`, which is replaced after success). `closeNow()` always sends `env.api.closeSession(ref, "ok", null, closeKey)` with the same body `{"verdict":"ok"}`. On the hub, `idempotency.claim` (apps/api/src/hq_api/idempotency.py) replays the stored response for 24 hours when the principal, key, method, path and body hash all match (test_w7_sessions.py asserts `Idempotent-Replay: true`).
  - Fix: Make the key a `var closeKey by remember(ref) { mutableStateOf(UUID...) }` and set it to a fresh UUID after a successful close (keep it only across retries of a failed press), the same way `nudgeKey` is handled.
- **medium** · correctness · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/studio/Series.kt:463` · Series saved silently (no voice) while the editor shows «صدای فعال هاب» selected
  - What: A new series defaults to `voice = "active"`, and `voiceId = if (voice == "active") active?.id else voice`, where `active = voices.data?.firstOrNull { it.status == "active" }`. When the hub has no active voice (TTS was over the memory budget), or the voices query has not loaded or failed, `voiceId` is null. The body then gets `SeriesFormat(..., voice = null, ...)` with no warning and the chip still selected.
  - Fix: When `voice == "active"` and `active == null`, either disable «بساز» with a line saying no voice is active, or send an explicit 'active voice' marker the hub resolves at render time. Never silently send null.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/support/Support.kt:655` · Shadow test result is always read as failed: the hub sends `pass_`, the app reads `pass`
  - What: The server's `shadow.ShadowReport` (apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/shadow.py:91) has a field `pass_: bool` with no alias. `GET /support/shadow` returns `list[shadow.ShadowReport]` through response_model, so the JSON key is `pass_`. The openapi lists `pass_` as required, and `as_payload()` renames it only for event payloads. The Kotlin `ShadowReport` (core/net/Models16.kt:270) declares `val pass: Boolean = false`, which SnakeCase maps to `pass`. The key is never present, so `pass` is always false. Support.kt reads `r.pass` at line 655 (pill), `report?.pass` at line 383 (channel card) and `sd.count { it.pass }` at line 157 (summary tile).
  - Fix: Add `@SerialName("pass_") val pass: Boolean = false` to the Kotlin model, or accept both keys with `@JsonNames("pass", "pass_")`. Better still, fix the server model with `Field(alias="pass")` and `serialize_by_alias` so the contract carries `pass`.
- **medium** · correctness · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/v18/Bits.kt:340` · SecretField drops any value over 500 characters, so a Google service-account JSON cannot be entered
  - What: `BasicTextField(value, { if (it.length <= 500) onChange(it) }, …)`. ConnectionBody (v29/Connections.kt:174) uses this field for `putConnectionSecret`. For search_console and ga4 that secret is the whole service-account JSON (domain/connections.py:903 `service_account()` asks for «کل فایل JSON»), which is about 2.3 KB because of the RSA private key. The server accepts up to 20,000 characters (`SecretIn.secret max_length=20_000`).
  - Fix: Add a `maxLength` parameter to SecretField (default 500) and pass 20_000 from ConnectionBody. Consider `singleLine = false` for JSON secrets.
- **medium** · crash · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/v29/LongVoice.kt:267` · «تمام» while the hub session is still starting crashes the app (`id!!`) and can leave the microphone open
  - What: `toHub()` sets `engine = "hub"` and then, inside `scope.launch`, awaits `sttStart` before setting `session` or `legacy` and starting the recorder. If `finish()` runs in that window: `cut(last=true)` returns because `rec == null`, and `legacy` is still false. The launched block then calls `env.api.sttFinish(id!!)` with `id == null`, which throws a NullPointerException. That is not caught by `catch (e: ApiException)` and escapes `rememberCoroutineScope`, so the app crashes. The `toHub` coroutine also keeps going: it then sets `session` and calls `startChunk()`, which opens a VoiceRecorder after finish already stopped the ticker, so the microphone stays recording until the screen is left.
  - Fix: In `finish()`, if `engine == "hub" && session == null && !legacy`, mark `stopping = true` and let `toHub` finish once the session is known, or cancel it. Keep the `toHub` Job and cancel it in `finish()` and `cancel()`. Replace `id!!` with a null check that falls back to the committed text. Catch `Exception` rather than only ApiException.
- **medium** · correctness · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/v29/Secretary.kt:72` · The secretary's route for the old text stays and is sent with the edited order
  - What: `rememberPreview` never clears `p.route` when `text` changes. The LaunchedEffect returns early for text under 8 characters, waits 650 ms before fetching, and keeps the old route on an ApiException. VoiceOrder.kt:284 sends `preview.route` with the order, and the order is «followed exactly».
  - Fix: At the start of the effect, if `!p.edited && preview0 == null`, set `p.route = null` before the early returns and the delay. Also clear it on failure. Alternatively, store the text the route was computed for and only use the route when it matches the current text.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/v30/LineScreen.kt:208` · Removing a line's schedule is silently ignored by the hub
  - What: «بدون برنامه» sets schedule to "", and save sends `lineBody(..., schedule.trim().ifBlank { null }, ...)`, which writes `"schedule": null` (V30.kt:250). The hub's LinePut treats `schedule: null` as 'unchanged' and only clears the schedule when `clear_schedule: true` is sent (routes/lines.py:83-84, 327-330). The app never sends clear_schedule.
  - Fix: When the edited schedule is blank and the line had one, send `"clear_schedule": true` (and omit `schedule`). Otherwise send the schedule string.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/v30/LineScreen.kt:429` · «نسخه‌ها» sheet always fails: LineVersion.by is an object and body has no ref
  - What: VersionsBody reads lineVersions(), which decodes each item into LineVersion (Models30.kt:79-86: `body: Line?`, `by: String?`). The hub (lines/store.py:76-82, 419-426) sends `by` as an object `{kind, code}` and `body` as body_of(): template, platform, channel, title_fa, schedule, caps, auto_after, steps and library, with no `ref`. Decoding an object into String? fails. Line.ref has no default, so decoding `body` into Line also fails with a MissingFieldException. Line 442 then uses `v.by?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }`. The demo (DemoV30.kt:295) sends `by` as a string, which hides the mismatch.
  - Fix: In LineVersion, make `by` a small data class `By(kind: String = "", code: String = "")` (or a JsonElement) and make `body` a JsonObject. In VersionsBody, show who made the change from `by.kind/by.code`.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/v31/Ads.kt:68` · Ads tab fails to decode and mis-maps campaigns; «ادامه» cannot work
  - What: Ads (Models31.kt:139-171) does not match the hub's domain/ads.py:128-182. First, AdDay.conversions is a float (pydantic sends `3.0`) but AdDaily.conversions is Long, so any campaign with at least one day makes the whole /ads decode throw. Second, the hub's AdCampaign has `campaign_id`, `name` and `status` (active|paused|removed), but the app reads `id`, `titleFa` and `state` (default "on"), so every campaign has id "", no title and shows «روشن». The «ادامه» branch (`k.state == "paused"`, line 137) is never reached. AdAccount has no `account_id` (line 102 shows blank). Third, resumeAd signs target "$platform/$id" while the hub consumes `f"{platform}:{campaign_id}"` (routes/ads.py:220), so resume would fail with step_up_invalid even with the right id.
  - Fix: Align the model: `@SerialName("campaign_id") id`, `name`, `status` (map active→on), `conversions: Double`, account `labelFa`. Show «ادامه» for `status == "paused"`, and sign `"$platform:$id"` in resumeAd.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/v31/Ads.kt:180` · Deleting or loosening an ad rule is sent without the step-up the hub requires
  - What: `env.api.deleteAdRule(code, id)` sends a plain DELETE. The hub always calls `stepup.consume(..., "ads.cap", rule_id)` on delete (routes/ads.py:185). Edits need the same step-up whenever the rule 'lets more money through' (routes/ads.py:128-144): a lower ROAS floor, a larger min spend, notify instead of pause, or switched off. The app signs only when the daily cap rises (line 230), and the on/off switch (line 174) never signs.
  - Fix: Sign `ads.cap` on the rule id for every delete. For edits, either always sign or mirror the hub's `loosens()` (off, notify, lower min_roas, higher min_spend, higher cap, wider scope) before sending.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/v31/Ads.kt:226` · New ad rule is always rejected: required `platform` is never sent
  - What: RuleForm builds the body with scope, daily_cap, window_days, min_roas, min_spend_before_judging, action and state, then POSTs it via saveAdRule(code, null, body). The hub's AdRuleIn (domain/ads.py:74-87) requires `platform: Platform`, and Model is `extra="forbid"`. Without `platform` the hub returns 422.
  - Fix: Add a platform choice to RuleForm (from the company's ad accounts, or from the chosen campaign's platform) and `put("platform", …)` in the create body. Leave it out of the PUT body (AdRuleEdit has no platform).
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/v31/CampaignCalendar.kt:267` · Editing a campaign always fails: PUT body carries `company`, which CampaignPut forbids
  - What: CampaignForm sends the same body for create and edit, including `put("company", co)`, through saveCampaign(key, body), which is a PUT /campaigns/{id} when key != null. The hub's CampaignPut (domain/campaigns.py:79-86) has no `company` field, and Model is `ConfigDict(extra="forbid")` (schemas.py:17-18), so the hub answers 422.
  - Fix: Omit `company` when `camp != null` (build the PUT body without it).
- **medium** · security-contract · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/v31/Kits.kt:146` · Company-from-kit seal signs the wrong target, and the success response would not decode
  - What: createFromKit (Api.kt:1614) signs `stepUp("company.create", code)`. The hub consumes the step-up with `await stepup.body_target(request)`, which is the SHA-256 hex of the exact request body (routes/kits.py create_company_from_kit; security/stepup.py:80-84). consume() compares `row["target"] != target` and raises 403 step_up_invalid. Even if the signature matched, KitCreated.lines is list[KitLine] objects while KitResult.lines is List<String> (Models31.kt:66), so decoding would fail after the transaction had committed. The preview also ignores `code_free`/`problems` from the hub, so a taken code is only found at creation.
  - Fix: Serialize the body once, sign `sha256(text)` and send that exact text (as putBudgets/putSites do with `stepUp(sha256(text))`). Make KitResult.lines a `List<KitLine>`. In the preview stage, read `code_free`/`problems` and disable the seal when the code is taken.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/v31/Newsletter.kt:154` · «خبرنامه‌ی تازه» always 422: app sends list/body_md, hub requires body (and list_id)
  - What: newNewsletter (Api.kt:1618-1621) posts `{list, subject, subject_b, body_md}`. The hub's NewsletterCampaignIn (domain/newsletter.py:88-92) requires `body` (min_length 1) and takes the list as `list_id`. The extra keys are ignored but `body` is missing, so validation fails.
  - Fix: Send `put("body", bodyMd); put("content_type", "markdown"); put("list_id", list)`, and drop `subject_b` (the hub has no A/B field).
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/v31/Newsletter.kt:173` · List size limit and campaign's list never read: over-limit schedule goes unsigned and fails; send time never shown
  - What: The hub sends `size_limit` once at the top of NewsletterOut (domain/newsletter.py:79-85). NewsletterList has no size_limit, and CampaignOut has `list_id` (int) and `send_at`. The app reads `NlList.sizeLimit`, `NlCampaign.list` and `NlCampaign.scheduledAt` (Models31.kt:68-82), which are always null. As a result: (1) `over` at line 173 is always false, so scheduleNewsletter is called without the `newsletter.send` passkey; (2) the meter and warning at lines 103-108 never show; (3) `k.scheduledAt?.let` at line 118 never shows the send time or the sent time.
  - Fix: Read `sizeLimit` from Newsletter (top level), map `sendAt` to scheduledAt, and decide `over` from `list.subscribers > newsletter.sizeLimit` (or always offer the passkey when the hub answers step_up_required).
- **medium** · correctness · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/v32/TalkSession.kt:292` · 90-second 'no news' watchdog is never reset, so long answers are cut while still streaming
  - What: follow() starts `watchdog = scope.launch { delay(NO_NEWS_MS); if (phase != Phase.IDLE && !speaking) { streamJob?.cancel(); finishStream(ref) } }` once per answer. apply() never cancels or re-arms it when Text/Card/Tool events arrive. So it fires 90 s after the turn started, whatever is still streaming, despite its comment ('Ninety seconds of no news').
  - Fix: Restart the watchdog on every event in apply() (cancel and relaunch the delay), so it really measures 90 s without news.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/v33/Sales.kt:207` · Lead forms send values that break LeadIn/LeadPatch rules (pseudonym pattern, note length, value decimals)
  - What: (1) Pseudonym: `HqTextField(contact, ..., maxLength = 80)` takes any text. `LeadIn.contact` must match `^[A-Za-z0-9_:.-]{8,128}$`. The save button is enabled with only a contact, `enabled = valueOk && (name.isNotBlank() || contact.isNotBlank())`. (2) Note: `maxLength = 1000` on lines 215 and 403, but `LeadIn.note_fa`/`LeadPatch.note_fa` maxLength is 500. (3) Value: `usdtInput` accepts any number of decimals, but the value_usdt pattern allows at most 4.
  - Fix: Check the pseudonym on the client against `^[A-Za-z0-9_:.-]{8,128}$` and show a hint; set both note fields to `maxLength = 500`. Make `usdtInput` reject (or round) values with more than 4 decimals.
- **medium** · error-handling · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/workshop/Session.kt:139` · A failed first load of workshop events blocks the session feed for good: no retry, no live link, approvals never show
  - What: In `rememberSessionFeed`, a failed history fetch runs `catch (e: ApiException) { feed.error = e; return@launch }`. Neither SSE nor polling starts after that. `feed.error` is never set back to null, and `feed` is remembered per (env, ref). In `SessionScreen`, `feed.error != null -> item { ErrorBlock(feed.error, null) }` comes first in the `when` and passes `onRetry = null`, so there is no retry button. When the app goes to the background and back, `onScreen` toggles, the effect runs again and may load events, but the stale error still wins the `when`, so the events are never drawn.
  - Fix: Set `feed.error = null` at the start of each effect run and after a successful fetch. Pass a retry to the ErrorBlock (for example, bump a retry key used in the DisposableEffect keys). On a failed history fetch, fall back to `poll(4_000)` instead of returning.
- **low** · correctness · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/MainActivity.kt:27` · Spent enrolment token is re-applied when the activity is recreated (process death), covering the signed-in shell with EnrolScreen
  - What: `enrolToken = tokenOf(intent)` runs on every onCreate, including a restore with savedInstanceState != null. The `launch` extra is guarded by the savedInstanceState check (line 30) and removed from the intent, but the enrol URI stays in `intent.data` and is never cleared after use. HqRoot.kt:54 gives `enrolToken != null` priority over both the signed-in shell and the app lock.
  - Fix: Read the token only when `savedInstanceState == null`, and in onEnrolConsumed (and after reading it) clear it from the intent, for example `intent.data = null` / `setIntent(Intent(intent).setData(null))`.
- **low** · security · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/MainActivity.kt:37` · FLAG_SECURE on Android 9-12 follows the lock setting only at activity creation
  - What: `else if (graph.lock.enabled()) window.addFlags(FLAG_SECURE)` is evaluated once in onCreate, and nothing adds or clears the flag when the owner changes the app lock in settings (no other FLAG_SECURE reference in the app). The activity handles every configuration change itself and is singleTask, so it normally lives until the process dies.
  - Fix: Observe `prefs.appLock` (and the device-secure state) in the activity and call `window.addFlags` / `clearFlags(FLAG_SECURE)` on each change for SDK < 33.
- **low** · correctness · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/core/data/LiveStore.kt:381` · Server 'busy' count drops to 0 while a Claude job is still running (per-command server.call events treated as job ends)
  - What: In reduceServers, `"server.call"` adds 1 to `busy` on `running` and subtracts 1 on any done or failed state. The hub emits one `server.call` with state `running` when a job is dispatched (domain/servers.py:1171). Inside a `claude` job it also emits one `server.call` per command, with state done or failed (servers.py:1393), and then another at the job's real end (servers.py:1508). The first command's 'done' therefore brings busy back to 0 (it is clamped at 0), while the job (a workshop or direct turn of up to 60 minutes) is still running. reduceFlows also draws a `job_done` particle for each command.
  - Fix: Track running jobs by `data.job` (a set per server): add on `running`. Treat a done or failed event that carries a per-command `cmd_masked` inside a `claude` job as a step, not an end. Alternatively, have the hub tag per-command events (for example `step: true`) and ignore them in both reduceServers and reduceFlows.
- **low** · correctness · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/core/data/LookGroup.kt:192` · nextRun shows the wrong next run for valid hub schedules (ignores INTERVAL and multi-value BYHOUR/BYMINUTE/BYMONTHDAY)
  - What: nextRun takes only the first value of BYHOUR and BYMINUTE (`split(',').first()`). It reads BYMONTHDAY with `toIntOrNull()`, so "1,15" becomes null and falls back to day 1. It ignores INTERVAL for HOURLY. The hub's recurrence.parse (domain/recurrence.py:86-103) accepts all of these, and playbook and group routines are validated through it (playbooks.py:261, groups.py:226).
  - Fix: Expand every BYHOUR × BYMINUTE (and every BYMONTHDAY or BYDAY) over the search window and take the earliest one after now. For HOURLY with INTERVAL, step from local midnight by INTERVAL hours.
- **low** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/core/data/V30.kt:251` · Saving a line rewrites any step timeout of 120 s to 300 s (client default differs from the hub's)
  - What: `out["steps"] = Api.json.encodeToJsonElement(ListSerializer(StepDef.serializer()), steps)` is encoded with encodeDefaults=false. StepDef in Models30.kt:26 declares `timeoutS: Int = 120`, but the hub's StepDef default for `timeout_s` is 300 (openapi StepDef). A step whose timeout is exactly 120 is equal to the client default, so it is left out of the PUT body, and the hub stores 300.
  - Fix: Set the client default to the contract's (`timeoutS: Int = 300`) or encode steps with `Api.jsonBody` (encodeDefaults=true), as playbookPatch already does for seats and routines.
- **low** · correctness · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/core/data/V32.kt:237` · parseCap maps ',' to a decimal point, so a thousands-separated cap like "1,000" becomes $1
  - What: `'٫', ',', '،' -> '.'` turns "1,000" into "1.000", and `toDouble` gives 1.0, which passes the `> 0 && <= 100_000` check. The cap range goes up to 100000, so thousands separators are plausible. The Persian thousands mark '٬' is not handled either (it is rejected).
  - Fix: Treat ',' and '٬' as thousands separators (remove them) and keep only '٫' and '.' as the decimal mark. Or reject ambiguous input.
- **low** · rtl-persian · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/core/data/V33.kt:294` · usdtInput rejects the Persian decimal separator '٫', so values typed on a Persian keyboard are refused
  - What: `usdtInput` converts Persian and Arabic digits and removes ',' and '٬', but leaves '٫' (U+066B), which the Persian keyboard produces for a decimal point. `toBigDecimalOrNull()` then returns null. parseCap in V32.kt handles '٫', so the two money inputs behave differently.
  - Fix: Map '٫' to '.' before parsing: `.map { ch -> when { ch == '٫' -> '.'; ch in '۰'..'۹' -> ...; ... } }`.
- **low** · data-loss · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/core/diag/ErrorLog.kt:259` · Out-of-order async writes can overwrite a newer log, including the crash entry, with an older snapshot
  - What: `update()` computes `snapshot` under `synchronized(this)` but calls `io.execute { write(snapshot) }` outside the lock, so two threads can enqueue their snapshots in reverse order. `crash()` writes synchronously on the crashing thread, but a write already queued on the `hq-errorlog` executor (an older snapshot without the crash) can run after it, before the process is killed.
  - Fix: Have the writer always write the latest `state.value` (for example `io.execute { write(state.value) }` with coalescing), and in `crash()` shut down or drain the executor (`io.shutdownNow()`) before the synchronous write.
- **low** · correctness · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/core/link/LinkStore.kt:89` · ordersTold/ordersKnown are capped with takeLast on an unordered HashSet, so arbitrary (even recent) keys are evicted and order notices repeat
  - What: `putStringSet(K_ORDERS_TOLD, v.toList().takeLast(ORDERS_KEEP).toSet())`. `getStringSet` returns a HashSet with no insertion order. OrderWatcher loads it into `told` (`told += memory.ordersTold`) and appends new keys, so after the 1000-key cap, `takeLast` drops entries in hash order, not the oldest ones. ordersKnown has the same problem.
  - Fix: Persist an ordered list (for example a newline-joined string, as `K_TOLD` already does) and trim the oldest. Or store a timestamp per key and evict the oldest.
- **low** · crash · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/core/net/Api.kt:436` · upload(): a non-IOException thrown by `open()` inside RequestBody.writeTo is rethrown on OkHttp's dispatcher thread and kills the app
  - What: `writeTo` calls `open().use { ... }` on the OkHttp dispatcher thread (the call is `enqueue`d in `send()`). OkHttp's AsyncCall.run reports `IOException("canceled due to $t")` to onFailure and then rethrows `t` on its own thread. Callers' openers throw non-IO exceptions: ui/media/Composer.kt:143 `openInputStream(uri) ?: error("unreadable")` (IllegalStateException), and `ContentResolver.openInputStream` throws SecurityException when the picked URI's grant is gone (for example after the process was recreated).
  - Fix: In `writeTo`, convert every failure of `open()` and of the read into an IOException: `val input = try { open() } catch (e: IOException) { throw e } catch (e: Exception) { throw IOException("unreadable", e) }` (and the same around the read loop), so it reaches `onFailure` and becomes an ApiException.
- **low** · correctness · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/core/net/Api.kt:2065` · Any 401 clears the session, including the passkey registration 401 for an expired challenge while the owner is signed in

NotificationManagerService applies enqueue and cancel on its own handler, so the list read straight afterwards often does not include the notification just posted, or still includes the one just cancelled.

- **low** · correctness · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/archive/Archive.kt:91` · Archive list pagination: after a refresh, loaded pages go stale, rows go missing and «بیشتر» adds duplicates
  - What: `more` is keyed only on `f.key` (`remember(f.key) { mutableStateListOf() }`). `cursor` is keyed on `(f.key, list.data)`. When the first page refetches (pull-to-refresh, or any `env.cache.invalidate("archive")` after a filing or seal), `list.data` changes, so `cursor` resets to the new first page's `nextCursor`. But `more` keeps the pages loaded earlier. `rows = page1(new) + more(old)` is then shifted relative to the new first page.
  - Fix: Key `more` on the same thing as `cursor`: `remember(f.key, list.data) { mutableStateListOf() }`. Or clear `more` whenever the first page changes, e.g. `LaunchedEffect(list.data) { more.clear() }`.

  - What: DateField calls `onChange(x.ifBlank { null })` on every keystroke. In ArchiveScreen's Filters (Archive.kt:214-215) that sets `from`/`to` to partial strings like "2026-0". These go straight into `RecordFilters`, producing a new query key and a `GET /archive/records?from=2026-0`. The hub types `from`/`to` as `date` (routes/archive.py:252), so it answers 422. With no data for the new key, the list card renders `ErrorBlock(list.error)`; the search query fails the same way.
  - Fix: In the filter, pass a date to the filter state only when it matches `^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$`, and pass null otherwise. Or give DateField an `onValid` callback and keep partial text local.

While the value is empty (`r == null`) the choice is thrown away. The displayed kind falls back to `kinds.firstOrNull()`, and the ref text field then saves `kind` = that first kind.
  - Fix: Keep the chosen kind even without a ref, e.g. store `jobj("kind" to jstr(k), "ref" to JsonPrimitive(""))`, or hold the kind in a local `remember` state and use it when the ref is typed.

  - What: `val all = rememberQuery("archive:records:all", ...) { records(RecordFilters()).items }` fetches one page (`limit=50` in Api.records) and ignores `nextCursor`. `hit = all.filter { covers(m, s, it) }` drives «N سند فعلی را میگیرد» and «اکنون N سند را میگیرد».

- **low** · error-handling · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/archive/Record.kt:265` · Draft «ثبت کن»: if filing fails after the PATCH succeeds, every later save or file fails on a stale version
  - What: `file.run { val saved = env.api.patchRecord(r, body()); val out = env.api.fileDraft(saved); ...; onSaved(out) }`. patchRecord sends `If-Match: r.version` and bumps the row's version on the hub. If `fileDraft` then fails, `onSaved` is never called, so the screen keeps the old `r`. A common cause is `422 validation_failed` from check_files (`needs at least N file(s)`); the button never checks `cls.requiredFiles`. The next «ثبت کن» or «ذخیره‌ی پیش‌نویس» sends `If-Match` with the old version and is refused as stale.
  - Fix: Call `onSaved(saved)` right after the PATCH, before `fileDraft`, so the screen holds the new version even if filing fails. Also disable «ثبت کن» while `r.current?.files.size < cls.requiredFiles`, as IntakeContent does with `enough`.

The banner then reads «file_ids» رد شد…, and no row matches `refused == name`. VersionContent's file list (lines 591-597) also has no remove control. A refused file, or one whose upload failed (id stays null so `files.all { it.second != null }` is false), stays in the list forever.
  - Fix: Map the refusal to a row with `e.fields["file_ids"]` (the file id, stored as `Triple.second`) and show that row's name. Add an × per file row that removes it from `files`.

Removing step i shifts the later steps up, but each editor keeps the text of the step that used to be in its slot.

- **low** · correctness · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/live/HoldingMap.kt:332` · Second timed map state (green «back» link / red failed tick) is never expired when no event follows
  - What: `LaunchedEffect(live.serverStates) { val soon = …minOrNull(); delay(soon - nowMs + 50); tick++ }` is keyed only on `serverStates`. After the first `tick++`, `nowMs` is recomputed but the effect does not run again. The next pending expiry (e.g. a `failedAt + FAILED_TICK_MS` after a `backAt + RECONNECTED_MS`) is therefore never scheduled. `nowMs` otherwise moves only when `live.applied` changes, i.e. when another stream event arrives.
  - Fix: Key the effect on `tick` as well: `LaunchedEffect(live.serverStates, tick)`. Or loop inside the effect until no pending expiry is left.
- **low** · correctness · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/live/HoldingMap.kt:747` · Hub sheet «فرمان‌های امروز» counts by the UTC day, not the owner's day (Asia/Tehran)
  - What: `val today = LocalDate.ofInstant(Instant.ofEpochMilli(now), ZoneOffset.UTC)` and each flow's date are taken in UTC. The rest of the app uses `HqClock.today()` (OWNER_TZ = Asia/Tehran).
  - Fix: Use `ZoneId.of(OWNER_TZ)` (or `HqClock.today()`) for both `today` and each flow's date.
- **low** · ui · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/live/HoldingMap.kt:807` · Server sheet title omits the server name for multi-server companies the stream has not reported yet
  - What: `val title = serverLabel(s, mapServersOf(live, s.company) > 1, names)`. `mapServersOf` counts only `live.serverStates` entries (line 834), not the map's own server list built from `GET /infra/network` (`mapServers(net, live)`). Before the stream has said anything about those servers, the count is 0 or 1, so `many` is false and `serverLabel` returns just the company name. The count also includes revoked servers, which the map filters out. Elsewhere (HoldingTable line 912, CompanySheet) the count comes from the drawn `servers` list.
  - Fix: Pass the drawn server list (or a `many` flag computed as `servers.count { it.company == s.company } > 1`) into `ServerSheet` instead of `mapServersOf(live, …)`.
- **low** · rtl-persian · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/look/Limits.kt:148` · «90%» label of today's meter sits away from its mark in RTL
  - What: The 90 label box is placed at `x = at - box` with `align(TopStart).offset(x)`. Offset is direction-aware, so in RTL the box spans from (at-30dp) to at measured from the right edge, and the mark lies at the box's left physical edge. The text is aligned with absolute `TextAlign.Right`, which pushes it to the box's right edge, away from the mark. This is only correct in LTR. The 100 label uses `TextAlign.Left` and is correct in RTL only because it is pinned to the track end.
  - Fix: Use `TextAlign.End` for the 90 label (start/end resolve with the layout direction). Or compute the alignment from `LocalLayoutDirection`.
- **low** · state-restoration · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/media/Composer.kt:456` · A photo/video taken with the camera is silently dropped if the app process is recreated while the camera is open
  - What: `var capture by remember { mutableStateOf<Uri?>(null) }` holds the target Uri for `TakePicture`/`CaptureVideo`. The result callbacks do `capture?.let { if (ok) a.add(listOf(it)) }`. Because `capture` lives in plain `remember`, it is lost when the process is killed and the Activity restored, which is common while a camera app holds the foreground. The manifest's configChanges prevents recreation on rotation, but not on process death. The launcher result is delivered after restore with `capture == null`.
  - Fix: Keep the Uri in `rememberSaveable` (save it as a String), so the result callback can still read it after the process is restored.
- **low** · data-loss · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/media/Composer.kt:536` · Text typed while a message is sending is wiped when the send completes
  - What: `submit()` sends `text.trim()`. On success it sets `text = ""; voice = null; files.clear()`. The `BasicTextField` stays enabled while `sending` is true, so anything the owner types during the request is in `text` when it is reset.
  - Fix: Capture the sent body (`val sentBody = text`). On success, clear only if `text == sentBody` (or strip that prefix). Alternatively, make the field read-only while `sending`.

- **low** · correctness · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/meetings/Room.kt:264` · The time-box countdown of a later agenda item reads 00:00 before that item's first turn
  - What: `val begin = start ?: instant(m.startsAt)?.toEpochMilli() ?: now`. `start` is the earliest turn of the current item (RoundTable, line 276). For any item after the first, before its first turn, `start` is null, so the countdown counts from the meeting's start: `timeBoxMin*60_000 - (now - meetingStart)`. That value is already zero or negative, and mmss clamps it to 0. The code's own comment says the item's clock starts at its first turn.

  - What: `val spots = base + moved`: entries in `moved` always win over the hub's layout and are never removed. When putCanvasNode throws (line 207), the code shows a toast but leaves `moved[code] = p`. When the hub's canvas comes back with a different position (moved on the web, or rejected), the phone keeps drawing the local one. The comment at line 112 says the local spot should last only until the hub's canvas comes back.
  - Fix: On failure, remove `moved[code]`. Clear `moved` entries when `canvas.nodes` changes (for example with LaunchedEffect(canvas.nodes) { moved.clear() }), or once the hub's x/y matches the local spot.
- **low** · ui · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/office/Canvas.kt:135` · Canvas tap and drag handlers capture stale canvas width and height (w/h) after a resize; dragged nodes are saved to the wrong place
  - What: `w`/`h` are locals computed from BoxWithConstraints (lines 127-128). They are used inside `pointerInput(links, editable) { detectTapGestures { p -> linkAt(..., p.x / w, p.y / h) } }` and inside `pointerInput(n.code, editable) { detectDragGestures(onDrag = { ... amount.x / w ... }) }`. Neither key includes w or h, so the running gesture coroutines keep the values from their first composition. MainActivity declares configChanges=orientation|screenSize|screenLayout|smallestScreenSize (AndroidManifest.xml line 50), so rotation, split-screen or unfolding resizes the canvas without recreating the activity, and the handlers are not restarted.
  - Fix: Use the PointerInputScope's own `size` inside the handlers (`p.x / size.width`, `amount.x / size.width`), or add w and h to the pointerInput keys. For the node handlers, the canvas size is needed, so key them on w and h, or read w and h through rememberUpdatedState.

In the else branch, `for (o in list)` adds nothing, and the 'no active OKR' cards are shown only when `qtr == current`, which is false here.

- **low** · rtl-persian · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/outputs/Outputs.kt:449` · Android sorts producer names by UTF-16 code unit, so Persian alphabetical order is wrong
  - What: `.sortedBy { it.titleFa }` uses String.compareTo (code units). Persian-only letters (پ U+067E, چ, ژ, ک U+06A9, گ, ی U+06CC) sort after the Arabic-block letters, so «پ…» comes after «و…/ه…» and «ک…» after «ن…». The web version of the same list uses `localeCompare(b.label, 'fa')` (app/(app)/outputs/page.tsx:154).
  - Fix: Use `sortedWith(compareBy(java.text.Collator.getInstance(Locale("fa"))) { it.titleFa })`.

  - What: `dueAt = if (due.trim() != due0) dueIso else null`: when the owner empties the field, dueIso is null, so WorkPatch.dueAt is null. Api.json uses explicitNulls=false, so the null is dropped from the body. If nothing else changed, `patch == WorkPatch()` and no request is sent at all. Either way the code then toasts 'کار به‌روز شد.' The server supports clearing: routes/work.py:473 keeps an explicit `due_at: null` (`... or k in ("due_at",)`) and the UPDATE sets due_at = NULL.
  - Fix: Send an explicit null when the field was cleared, for example a separate `clearDue` flag that writes `"due_at": null` through a JsonObject body, or encode WorkPatch with explicitNulls=true for this call. Count a cleared due as a change, so the request is sent.

HqTextField uses the default text keyboard, so the Persian keyboard produces Persian digits.

- **low** · error-handling · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/owner/TreasuryTools.kt:239` · Every retry after a failed key save gets 409 because the sheet keeps the old connection version
  - What: The save runs `patchConnection(x, …)` with If-Match `x.version`, and then `connectionSecret(...)`. If the patch succeeds (the hub bumps the version) but the secret call fails, the sheet stays open with the same `x`. `changed` is still true, so the next tap sends the patch again with the stale If-Match version and the hub refuses it with a version conflict. The secret is never retried.
  - Fix: After a successful patch, keep the returned Connection in local state and use it for `changed` and If-Match, or skip the patch when it already went through.

Typing "0" or "0." parses to 0.0. That changes `value` (e.g. from 0.4), so `remember(value)` re-creates `text` as "0.0" and overwrites what was typed.
