- **low** · correctness · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/tools/Connection.kt:114` · Model prices are rounded to 2 decimals in the sealed connection body
  - What: `Price(String.format(Locale.US, "%.2f", priceIn[it]!!.trim().toDouble()), …)`. Prices are USD per million tokens, and cheap models are priced below one cent of precision (0.075, 0.028, 0.004). The server's ModelPrice accepts any decimal.
  - Fix: Send the owner's decimal as typed (trimmed and validated), or use enough digits, for example `BigDecimal(it).stripTrailingZeros().toPlainString()`.
- **low** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/tools/Tools.kt:110` · An egress request with no HTTP status is shown as a green «0»
  - What: In openapi `EgressEntry.status` is `integer | null`: null means no response, such as a timeout or connection error. The Kotlin `EgressEntry.status: Int = 0` coerces null to 0. Line 110 then renders `Fig(e.status.toString(), color = if (e.status < 400) c.positive else c.negative)`.
  - Fix: Make `status: Int? = null` and render «—» or a failure tone when it is null.
- **low** · correctness · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/treasury/Treasury.kt:311` · «امروز» is labelled «از نیمه‌شب تهران», but the hub's day starts at UTC midnight; the breakdowns use the Tehran day
  - What: The server's `period_start` (routes/treasury.py:231) is UTC midnight for `day` and UTC day 1 for `month`. The hero says «توکن امروز، از نیمه‌شب تهران». `periodStart()` (line 252) sends the Tehran date as `from` for the company and model breakdowns, which the server reads as the owner's day.
  - Fix: Either label the hero as the UTC day, or have the summary use the owner's zone. Use the same window (the hero's `period_start`) for the breakdown queries.
- **low** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/treasury/Waste.kt:104` · A missing 7-day comparison is shown as «+0%»
  - What: In openapi `WasteDay.vs_7d` is `number | null`. Kotlin `@SerialName("vs_7d") val vs7d: Double = 0.0` coerces null to 0.0, and line 104 then prints `"+" + change01(0.0)`, which is «+0%».
  - Fix: Make `vs7d: Double? = null` and show «—» when it is null.
- **low** · ui · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/v14/Backups.kt:280` · A restore test that never ran is shown as «رد» in red, and a failed backups query shows an endless skeleton
  - What: Line 280 reads `"${if (b.restoreTest.ok == true) "قبول" else "رد"}، ${ago(b.restoreTest.lastAt)}"` with a negative colour, so `ok == null` (no test yet) reads as a failed test. Line 255 reads `val b = q.data ?: return Skeleton(...)`, which has no error branch, so a 5xx or offline answer leaves a loading skeleton with no retry.
  - Fix: Show «هنوز اجرا نشده» in a neutral colour when `ok == null`, and handle `q.error` with ErrorBlock.
- **low** · correctness · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/v18/Bits.kt:295` · AudioPlayer keeps playing the first file after its fileId changes
  - What: `val player = rememberPlayer(media)` is remembered without a key, and `fun load() { if (player.mediaItemCount == 0) { player.setMediaItem(MediaItem.fromUri(media.url(fileId))) … } }` loads only once. When the same AudioPlayer call site recomposes with a different `fileId`, the old media item stays. `pos` and `dur` also stay. Call sites where the id changes in place: v30/Library.kt:220 (`voices.firstOrNull { it.name == speaker }?.sampleFile` changes when another speaker chip is tapped) and studio/Voice.kt:198 (the active voice's sample).
  - Fix: Key the player state on the file, for example `key(fileId) { … }` around the body, or `remember(fileId)` for pos and dur plus a `LaunchedEffect(fileId) { player.stop(); player.clearMediaItems(); pos = 0.0; dur = 0.0 }`.
- **low** · data-integrity · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/v30/LineScreen.kt:208` · Saving a line silently changes steps with a 120-second timeout to 300 seconds
  - What: lineBody encodes steps with `Api.json.encodeToJsonElement(ListSerializer(StepDef.serializer()), steps)` (V30.kt:251). Api.json has the default `encodeDefaults = false`, so fields equal to the Kotlin defaults are left out. StepDef's default is `timeoutS = 120` (Models30.kt:24), but the hub's StepDef default is `timeout_s = 300` (lines/templates.py:49). A step whose timeout is exactly 120 s is therefore sent without timeout_s and comes back as 300. Seed templates have such steps (seed/lines/instagram_reel.yaml:79,183; newsletter.yaml:49). The same applies to any cap field the owner clears: it is dropped and the hub's Caps default (1, 200000, 1) is applied.
  - Fix: Encode the PUT body with `Api.jsonBody` (encodeDefaults = true), or make the Kotlin StepDef defaults match the hub's (timeoutS = 300).
- **low** · data-integrity · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/v31/Automations.kt:128` · Retrying «مُهر و روشن کن» after a failed or cancelled passkey creates duplicate automations
  - What: seal.run first POSTs /automations (newAutomation) and then seals the new one. If the step-up is cancelled or sealAutomation fails, the automation already exists in state `proposed`. `compiled`/`sentence` are kept, and the cache is not invalidated, so the list does not show it.
  - Fix: Keep the created ref in state (e.g. `var made by remember`) and only seal it on retry. Invalidate "automations" right after creation.
- **low** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/v31/Automations.kt:184` · An automation's runs are never listed
  - What: The card reads `(detail.data ?: a).runList`, and GrowthAutomation.runList parses `runs` as a JSON array (Models31.kt:177-188). On both GET /automations and GET /automations/{ref}, the hub sends `runs` as an integer count and the list as `run_log` (AutomationDetail). Each run has `outcome`/`error_fa`, not `state`/`note_fa`. runList is therefore always empty.
  - Fix: Add `runLog: List<AutoRun>` to GrowthAutomation, mapping AutoRun.state from `outcome` and noteFa from `error_fa`, and read it in the expansion.
- **low** · ui · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/v31/CampaignCalendar.kt:151` · Calendar line items that already ran cannot be opened
  - What: routeOf only routes `kind == "line"` items whose ref starts with "LN-". For a line item that has a run, the hub sets `ref = run ref or line ref` (domain/campaigns.py:297), and run refs are `RN-…` (migration 0040_production_lines.py:162). For these items routeOf returns null, and the row's clickable is disabled (line 172).
  - Fix: Route `RN-` refs to `line-runs/$ref` and `LN-` refs to `lines/$ref`.
- **low** · correctness · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/v32/HandsFree.kt:148` · Hands-free on the hub path stops listening after a key is spoken
  - What: In the key branch of utterance(), the code calls `relisten()`, which does nothing in hub mode (`if (on && !speech.listening && !hubMode) listen()`). In hub mode, `rec` was already set to null in hubStep() before transcription, and nothing calls listenHub() again. The state stays ON/LISTENING with no recorder, so hubStep() returns early on every tick.
  - Fix: In the key branch (and the blank-text branch), call `if (hubMode) listenHub() else relisten()`.
- **low** · data-loss · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/v32/TalkComposer.kt:312` · Draft typed while the previous turn is sending is erased
  - What: send() takes `body = text.trim()`, launches session.send(...), and on success sets `text = ""` without checking that the field still holds that body. The text field stays editable while `sending` is true; only the send button is disabled.
  - Fix: Only clear when unchanged: `if (ok && text.trim() == body) text = ""` (or remove just the sent prefix).
- **low** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/v33/Money.kt:199` · Cost-per-output row prints "$null" when usd_each is null
  - What: `Fig("$" + String.format(java.util.Locale.US, "%.2f", x.usdEach), ...)`: `RevCostPer.usdEach` is `Double?`, and the OpenAPI `RevBoardOutputCost.usd_each` can be null. The hub (`revenue_board.py:418-425`, `each()` returns None when n == 0) always sends all three kinds (line_run, deliverable, order) and gives null for any kind whose count is 0. `String.format("%.2f", null)` does not throw; it returns the text "null".
  - Fix: Show a dash when the value is missing: `Fig(x.usdEach?.let { "$" + String.format(Locale.US, "%.2f", it) } ?: "—", ...)`. Or skip rows where `count == 0`.
- **low** · error-handling · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/v33/Money.kt:232` · Reconcile card says "no payments in these days" while loading or when the reconcile call fails
  - What: `ReconcileCard(rec.data.orEmpty(), days) { days = it }`: a null `rec.data` (still loading, or failed with `rec.error`) becomes an empty list. The card then shows «در این روزها پرداختی نبود.», and the error is never shown.
  - Fix: Pass the handle: show a Skeleton while `rec.data == null && rec.error == null`, and `ErrorBlock(rec.error, { rec.refresh() })` when the call failed.
- **low** · ui · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/v33/Parallel.kt:123` · Spacer in split-part tiles does not push «باز کن» to the bottom, so the buttons in the row do not line up
  - What: The tiles share the row height through `height(IntrinsicSize.Max)` and `fillMaxHeight()`. The spacer meant to push the button down is `Box(Modifier.weight(1f, fill = false))`. With `fill = false`, an empty Box measures at 0 height, so it takes no space and the button sits right after each tile's content.
  - Fix: Use `Spacer(Modifier.weight(1f))` (fill = true).
- **low** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/v33/Personas.kt:202` · Persona form allows input the hub always rejects with 422 (name > 100 characters, more than 8 pains or objections)
  - What: `HqTextField(name, ..., maxLength = 120)`, but the API's `PersonaIn`/`PersonaPatch.name_fa` has maxLength 100. Pains and objections are free multi-line fields (`maxLength = 2000`, "one per line") turned into lists by `lines()` with no cap, but the API has `pains_fa`/`objections_fa` maxItems 8. The form gives no hint of either limit.
  - Fix: Set name `maxLength = 100`. Limit the lists to 8 entries: block saving and show a hint when `lines(pains).size > 8` or `lines(objections).size > 8`, or cap them with `.take(8)` and a visible note.
- **low** · crash · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/v33/Revenue33.kt:162` · Affiliate-gaps list can crash on a duplicate LazyColumn key
  - What: `items(list.size, key = { "${list[it].account}:${list[it].month}" }) { ... }`. `account` is `revenue.accounts.service_account`, which is unique only per (company_id, exchange) (migration 0025). A tool account whose `companies` is NULL is allowed for every company (`check_service_account`, domain/revenue.py:135). So two companies, or two exchanges of one company, can share the same service_account, and `figures()` returns one row per account_id and month. `GapCard`'s `rememberSaveable("${g.account}:${g.month}")` uses the same colliding key.
  - Fix: Key on something unique per row, for example `"${g.company}:${g.exchange}:${g.account}:${g.month}"`, in both the `items` key and the `rememberSaveable` key.
- **low** · data-loss · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/v33/Sales.kt:338` · Lead edit and «از دست رفت» sheets close before the PATCH finishes, so a failure throws away what the owner typed
  - What: `LeadEditForm(cur, save.busy) { body -> patch(body, "ذخیره شد."); editing = false }` and `patch(...); losing = null` (line 345-346). `patch` only launches `save.run { ... }` and returns at once, so the sheet is closed before the request ends. If `patchLead` fails (a 409/412 because the lead's version changed through `If-Match`, a 422, or offline), the owner gets an error toast but the sheet and its fields are already gone.
  - Fix: Close the sheet only on success: move `editing = false` and `losing = null` inside the `save.run` block after `q.set(...)`, for example by giving `patch` an `onDone` callback.
- **low** · rtl-persian · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/v33/Usage.kt:161` · Usage report date range shows its exclusive end day, so a daily report reads as two days
  - What: `"${jDate(r.from)} تا ${jDate(r.to)}"` (also on line 146). The hub's `usage.window()` sets `end = today 00:00` in the owner's zone, an exclusive bound, so `to` is the day after the last day covered.
  - Fix: Show the inclusive end, for example `jDate(instant(r.to)?.minusSeconds(1)?.toString())`. For a daily report, show only `jDate(r.from)`.
- **low** · permission-handling · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/work/Orders.kt:192` · Notification card's «روشن کن» does nothing when the permission is already granted or permanently denied, and the card does not update after returning from Settings
  - What: `allowed` is `remember { AlertNotifier.allowed(ctx) }`. `allowed()` also requires `areNotificationsEnabled()`. On API 33+, the button always calls `ask.launch(POST_NOTIFICATIONS)`. If the runtime permission is already granted but notifications are turned off in system settings, or the owner has denied twice (permanent denial), the system returns at once with no dialog and `allowed` stays false. The button becomes a no-op. `ui/settings/Link.kt:85/207` handles this case with `deniedForGood()` and falls back to Settings; this card does not. On API < 33 the button opens app notification settings, but `allowed` is never re-checked on resume, so the card stays after the owner turns notifications on.
  - Fix: When the permission is already granted, or denied for good (`!shouldShowRequestPermissionRationale` after asking), open `Settings.ACTION_APP_NOTIFICATION_SETTINGS` instead of `ask.launch`. Re-evaluate `allowed` on ON_RESUME, for example with `LifecycleResumeEffect` or by keying on `rememberOnScreen()`.
- **low** · contract-mismatch · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/work/VoiceOrder.kt:93` · Order route kind "kit" shows as raw English in the route chip
  - What: The `KIND` map covers work, code, meeting and question. The API's `OrderRoute.kind` enum also has "kit". The fallback `KIND[r.kind] ?: (r.kind to DivanIcon.TASK)` shows the raw enum value.
  - Fix: Add a Persian label and icon for "kit" to `KIND`.
- **low** · ui · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/workshop/Sheets.kt:102` · Transcribing spinner in SpokenField draws a 40dp circle over a 14dp-tall slot
  - What: `CircularProgressIndicator(Modifier.height(14.dp), ...)` sets only the height. Material3 then applies `.size(40.dp)`, so the canvas is 40dp wide and 14dp tall. M3 draws the arc with `size.width` as its diameter, so the ring is about 38dp across and spills above and below the row onto the label and the text field. Other call sites use `Modifier.size(14.dp)`.
  - Fix: Use `Modifier.size(14.dp)`.
- **low** · correctness · `apps/android/app/src/main/java/com/coinepro/hq/ui/workshop/Workshop.kt:173` · «جلسه‌ی تازه» opens claude.ai in the browser while `meta` is still loading or has failed
  - What: `HqButton("جلسه‌ی تازه", { if (meta.data?.workshop?.runtime != null) onNewSession() else uri.openUri("https://claude.ai/code") })`. When `meta.data` is null (first load, or a failed `meta()`), this takes the "no in-hub runtime" branch. The info card below is correctly limited to `meta.data != null`.
  - Fix: Disable the button, or show loading, until `meta.data != null`. Only open claude.ai when `meta.data != null && meta.data.workshop?.runtime == null`.

### agents/charter

- **medium** · correctness · `agents/charter/HOLDING.md:10` · Red-line patterns match the honest risk disclaimers they are meant to protect
  - What: The no_guaranteed_profit rule patterns `سود\s*(?:ی\s*)?(?:قطعی|تضمینی|...)` (line 10) and `بدون\s+(?:هیچ\s+)?(?:ضرر|زیان|ریسک)` (line 11) have no negation handling. studio_checks.red_lines (apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/studio_checks.py:209-225) uses these exact file patterns as long as no charter is sealed (red_line_patterns falls back to agents/charter/HOLDING.md, and this file is still `status: proposed`). It runs them with a plain re.search over the script, voice, screen, frame and caption. The outbound guard's own profit check skips negated clauses («هیچ سود تضمینی وجود ندارد» is its documented allowed example, outbound_guard.py:34), but its red_line check has no such skip and is in NO_OVERRIDE. Verified: re.search returns True for «هیچ سود تضمینی وجود ندارد.», «هیچ معامله‌ای بدون ریسک نیست.» and «بازار رمزارز بدون ریسک نیست و ممکن است سرمایه از دست برود.».
  - Fix: Anchor the patterns so negated forms do not match. Add negative lookbehind and lookahead for «هیچ … » before the noun and «نیست/ندارد/وجود ندارد» after it, for example `(?<!هیچ\s)(?<!هیچ\s{2})سود\s*(?:ی\s*)?(?:قطعی|تضمینی…)(?![^.!؟\n]{0,20}(?:نیست|ندارد|وجود\s+ندارد))`. The same applies to the «بدون … ریسک» pattern. Alternatively, apply red-line patterns per clause with the same negation skip the guard's _profit uses. Add the four sentences above as negative test cases.

### agents/roles

- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `agents/roles/_common.md:15` · Shared rules still call deploys and restarts A3 'never do', against the owner's server decision
  - What: Line 15 (and line 13) tells every role: «Anything external or irreversible (A3: publishing, messaging customers, restarts, deploys, price changes) you never do — you `propose` it». The owner's decision of 2026-10-30 (CLAUDE.md rule 7, contract §26) is that only destructive actions need his seal. The technical charters say «Free: … commit and push, deploys and restarts» (ceo.md 121, cto.md 43, sre.md 32, devops_engineer.md). server.exec and server.write are A2 in tools.md and the gateway (servers.py). _common is the first block of every system prompt, so every technical role receives two opposite rules. The golden evals encode the stale rule: EV-005 c1 «proposes the deploy as A3» and EV-003 c2 «Critical finding (A3 without seal) … a config change is still a deploy».
  - Fix: Rewrite line 15 to match the server rule: on the company's own servers, through `server.*`, deploys, restarts and configuration are allowed. Only the destructive list (drop a DB, delete a volume or backup, wipe a disk, SSH, users or firewall, delete the server) waits for the seal. Remove «restarts, deploys» from the A3 list there and «publishing» from line 13 (see the publishing finding). Update the EV-005 c1 and EV-003 c2 rubrics to the same rule.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `agents/roles/ceo.md:11` · CEO holds media orders with no media director but has no speech.make, while direct sessions forbid delegating
  - What: routing.route sends the media domain to `media_director`, else `ceo` (apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/routing.py:361). The CEO's tool list (line 11) has no `speech.make`, and it is not in SHARED_TOOLS (charters.py), although `narrator.set` is shared. _common.md line 30 (direct sessions) tells the holder to do the work itself and not hand parts to others. A direct turn gets only the role's own tools (direct.py). _common line 28 and CLAUDE.md say a narrated reel is never made silent.
  - Fix: Add `speech.make` to ceo.md's tools (and to manager.md if managers can hold media orders), or add it to SHARED_TOOLS beside narrator.set. Bump `version`.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `agents/roles/cto.md:22` · CTO charter contradicts itself on restarts, deploys and pushes
  - What: Line 22: «SEV1: … propose any A3 action (restart, rollback, deploy) as a decision — you never execute it.» Line 30: «Code changes happen only on branches …, through CI and the owner's approval. No direct pushes to main or production, ever.» Line 43 of the same charter: «Free: reading, code, commit and push, deploys and restarts, configuration, packages…». The owner's decision (CLAUDE.md rule 7: only destructive actions need his seal) matches line 43. Lines 22 and 30 are left over from before prompt 17.
  - Fix: In line 22, replace «propose any A3 action (restart, rollback, deploy) as a decision — you never execute it» with «restart or roll back on the company's servers through server.* and say what you did; only destructive steps wait for the seal». Align line 30 with §26 (branches and PRs as practice, with deploys free outside a freeze). Bump `version`.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `agents/roles/motion_designer.md:69` · Motion designer is told «Voice is off» and given a package that has no caption.txt
  - What: Line 69 ends the owner's-session section with «Voice is off.» This directly contradicts the owner's final decision of 2026-11-10 (CLAUDE.md; _common.md line 28; media_director.md lines 99-105): the narrator of content is on, and «صدا خاموش» is never a reason for a silent video. Line 67 also defines the deliverable as «an MP4, plus a cover PNG». It omits `caption.txt` and `topic.txt`, which _common.md line 28/33 and session_publish.package_of require («Without caption.txt nothing goes out»). The role prompt is more specific than _common, so the model follows it. motion_designer runs on Opus and is the seat that makes reels.
  - Fix: Replace «Voice is off.» with: narration is on; make it with `speech.make` (the company's narrator) and pass the audio as `files` to `workshop.run`; never make a silent reel in place of a narrated one. Change the out/ list to «the .mp4, cover.jpg (JPEG), caption.txt with hashtags, and topic.txt», as in _common.md line 28/33. Bump `version`.
- **low** · contract-mismatch · `agents/roles/_common.md:25` · Shared rule tells every role to hand work over with work.request, which most roles do not have
  - What: Line 25: «Never say a capability or access is missing … do it, or hand it to who can with `work.request`». Line 26 also points to `work.delegate`/`work.request`. These tools are not in SHARED_TOOLS and are absent from most charters: sre, devops_engineer, lead_developer, scriptwriter, video_director, visual_designer, voice_producer, motion_designer, publisher, content_analyst, revenue_analyst, support_lead, legal_counsel, fact_checker, security_analyst, ai_safety, hub_platform_sre, inspector, overseer, assistant and advisor (checked by loading the charters with hq_common.charters).
  - Fix: Either add `work.request` to SHARED_TOOLS (with the policy deciding the target), or reword lines 25-26 so roles without it hand the need to their manager in their deliverable or with work.fail and a reason.
- **low** · correctness · `agents/roles/ceo.md:16` · CEO charter hard-codes eight departments and omits MEDIA
  - What: Line 16: «through your eight department managers (TECH, GROWTH, SALES, PRODUCT, FIN, QUANT, AIOPS, HR)». apps/api/seed/templates/standard.yaml defines nine departments, including MEDIA (line 274), whose head is media_director. The fixed list also contradicts the owner's rule that departments are data described by their mandate (manager.md line 20).
  - Fix: Replace the fixed list with «your department managers (company.state lists them, including MEDIA)». Bump `version`.
- **low** · contract-mismatch · `agents/roles/ceo.md:111` · Charters route removable publications to A3 propose cards, which need a passkey
  - What: Several charters tell the role to `propose` (A3) any publication outside a sealed series: ceo.md 111 «publishing is a proposal»; scriptwriter.md 77-78; publisher.md 46-47 («A one-off post … is the owner's: `propose` it (A3)»); visual_designer.md 60-61; video_director.md 67-68; voice_producer.md 56-57; motion_designer.md 73-74; and _common.md line 13 («publishing … You `propose` it»). An agent's `propose` of kind a3_action is A3, and decisions.needs_step_up asks for the passkey for every A3 card except TAP_KINDS (media_publish only, which agents cannot raise). The owner's decision of 2026-11-10 is that approval in the session is the seal for removable publications: no second card, no fingerprint. _common.md line 28 itself says «Never raise a card for it».
  - Fix: In each listed charter, replace «propose it as A3» for posts, reels and stories with the _common line 28 flow: put the package (file, cover.jpg, caption.txt) in the session and ask «تأیید و انتشار؟». Keep `propose` only for publications that cannot be taken back (newsletters, ads, emails) and for take-downs if wanted. Remove «publishing» from the A3 list in _common.md line 13/15.
- **low** · contract-mismatch · `agents/roles/content_analyst.md:11` · The content analyst's revenue.read and funnel.read always fail
  - What: content_analyst (employee, MEDIA department) lists `revenue.read` and `funnel.read`. support.rego grants these ops only to a same-company CEO, a manager of FIN or SALES, charter `revenue_analyst`, the support charters (funnel only), or holding advisor/overseer. A MEDIA-department employee matches none of these.
  - Fix: Add `"content_analyst"` to the funnel/revenue readers in support.rego (by `tv("charter")`), or remove the two tools from the charter.
- **low** · contract-mismatch · `agents/roles/content_analyst.md:29` · Analyst and publisher report Instagram as «وصل نیست», which the owner forbade
  - What: content_analyst.md line 29: «A channel that is not connected reads «وصل نیست», never zero», repeated at lines 39 and 48 with no Instagram exception. The morning table and weekly review reach the owner through the director. publisher.md line 41 gives the model the literal example «اتصال اینستاگرام IG-Hub قطع است» as the plain-Persian failure message for the owner's line; that example also names a service, which publisher.md line 51 forbids. No rule matches the overseer, so `work.delegate_not_permitted` / `part_not_delegable` comes back every time.

Otherwise it returns the latest catalogue or 409 owner_order_required. ceo.md lines 137-139 already describe the new rule; the overseer charter was not updated.

### agents/tools.md

The advisor's grants are only ['workshop.run'], and the advisor holds http.request.

This prose line is not rendered into agent prompts (only table rows are), so it does not change runtime behaviour. It is still the reference text for anyone writing charters or prompts from the catalog.
  - Fix: Reword: 'Publishing has no agent tool: a sealed series or a working group with the owner's standing permission is published by code, and a file in the owner's session goes out on his own «تأیید و انتشار» tap (no card). Only what is neither of these is proposed.'

A figure the connections give is never guessed. Any agent for its own company; the holding's readers for any.' Confirmed from the loaded catalog: the description ends with '`secret_state: set'.
  - Fix: Write the values without a pipe, e.g. 'only `secret_state` (`set` or `missing`)'.
- **low** · contract-mismatch · `agents/tools.md:202` · Stray '|' in the workshop.run row cuts off the second half of its description for every agent
  - What: The workshop.run row has an extra column separator: `... say so in one line and do the rest of the work. | ⟨2026-11-10⟩ In a step-by-step order («با من») `task` is required ...`. hq_common/charters.py parse_tools() splits each row on '|' and keeps only cells[3] as the description (`description=cells[3].strip()`), so cells[4] is silently dropped. Everything after the stray pipe never reaches a model: that `task` is required in a «با من» order and that `not_next_step` refuses any other task; what `files` is for (up to 10 ids of the company or this session, found in `in/`, to be explained in the brief); the §45 publish-package layout; and the `effort` default (`max`, «حتماً Opus 5.5 با بیشترین effort»; pass less only for a small mechanical fix). The truncated text is what agents/protocol.py tool_table(), direct.py catalog_fallback(), gateway app.py catalog_rows() (direct sessions) and talk/context.py show as the tool's description. Verified by loading the live catalog: lib.tools['workshop.run'].description ends at '...do the rest of the work.'
  - Fix: Remove the stray ' | ' before '⟨2026-11-10⟩ In a step-by-step order' so the row has exactly four cells, and add a sentence break before '⟨1.31⟩ `effort`'. Optionally make parse_tools join cells[3:] with ' | ' so a pipe inside the last cell is never lost.

### apps/web

- **high** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/app/(app)/tools/page.tsx:37` · Tools page reads GET /egress/summary as one object, but the API returns an array; the page always crashes
  - What: v14.ts:173-178 types the response as `EgressSummary` = {requests, blocked, needs_owner, bytes_in, by_company, by_tool}. The route at apps/api/src/hq_api/routes/egress.py:282 is `response_model=list[EgressDay]` (day, company, requests, blocked, bytes, browser_s, hosts). The page runs `s.requests.toLocaleString('en-US')` and `Object.entries(s.by_tool)` when `s` is an array.
  - Fix: Aggregate the array on the client: requests/blocked/bytes as sums, by_company grouped by company. Read `bytes`, not `bytes_in`. Drop `needs_owner` and `by_tool`, or add them to the API. Or make the API return the summary object that v14.ts expects.
- **high** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/components/v16/intake.tsx:181` · Registering a Telegram intake source always fails with 422 (no `channel` sent); the shown terminal command is rejected too
  - What: RegisterSheet lets the owner pick kind `telegram` but posts only `{company, kind, allowed_ips, kinds}`: IntakeSourceIn in lib/api/v16.ts has no `channel`. Server `put_source` (apps/api/src/hq_api/routes/intake.py:161-165) does `if body.kind == "telegram" and channel not in ("telegram_bot", "telegram_business"): raise ApiError(422 ...)`. The passkey ceremony runs first (`stepUp('intake.source', …)`), so the owner seals and then gets 422. Separately, the sheet (line 201) and IntakeCard (line 228) tell him to run `make intake-secret COMPANY=… SOURCE=telegram`, but infra/openbao/intake-secret.sh dies unless SOURCE is chatwoot or product. Telegram's secret lives at support/telegram/<CO>/<channel>. IntakeCard also keys rows by `${s.company}-${s.kind}`, so a company with both telegram_bot and telegram_business sources produces duplicate React keys.
  - Fix: When kind is telegram, add a channel picker (telegram_bot / telegram_business) and send `channel` in the body (add it to IntakeSourceIn). For telegram rows, show the telegram-bot-token command (`make telegram-bot-token COMPANY=… CHANNEL=…`). Key the rows by `${company}-${kind}-${channel ?? ''}`.
- **high** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/components/v16/support.tsx:315` · Shadow-test history upload always fails: JSON/CSV are not accepted by POST /files
  - What: ShadowCard's file picker only offers `.json,.csv,application/json,text/csv` (line 357), and `upload()` sends the file through `uploadFile(file, file.name, setPct)`. uploadFile (lib/api/v13.ts) sets `Content-Type: file.type || 'application/octet-stream'`. The server's `media.check_type` (apps/api/src/hq_api/media.py) only allows the types in `ALLOWED` (images, video, audio, pdf, fonts, text/plain, text/markdown, text/x-markdown, zip). For `application/octet-stream` it accepts only font or ZIP bytes. `application/json`, `text/csv`, `application/vnd.ms-excel` and an empty type are all refused with 415 "this type is not accepted". So `act.history.mutateAsync` (POST /support/history) is never reached.
  - Fix: Send the history as a type /files accepts: re-wrap the Blob as `new File([file], name, { type: 'text/plain' })` before uploadFile (JSON and CSV are UTF-8 text, so looks_text passes). Alternatively, add application/json and text/csv to the server's TEXT_TYPES.
- **high** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/components/v17/meetings.tsx:670` · Every meeting created from the web is rejected with 422 (extra field time_box_min; postmortem type not accepted)
  - What: NewMeetingSheet sends `items: items.filter(x => x.title_fa.trim())`, and each item is `{ title_fa, time_box_min }`. On the server, `MeetingOwnerItemIn` (apps/api/src/hq_api/routes/meetings.py:48) has only title_fa, brief_md, sources and companies, and its base `Model` sets `extra="forbid"` (schemas.py:18). `valid` requires at least one titled item, so every request includes `time_box_min` and fails validation. AD_HOC (line 657) also offers 'postmortem', but `MeetingCreateIn.type` is `Literal["board","company","strategy"]`.
  - Fix: Send items as `{ title_fa }` only (drop time_box_min, or add the field to the server contract first), and remove 'postmortem' from AD_HOC unless the server accepts it.
- **high** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/components/v17/meetings.tsx:718` · Saving meeting settings always fails: the whole GET row, including `calendar`, is PUT back
  - What: `run = () => save.mutateAsync(rows)` sends `{types: rows}`, and each row is the full GET `MeetingType` merged with the edits. The GET model (domain/meetings.py:52) includes `calendar: str = "gregorian"`, which is always serialised. `MeetingTypeIn` (domain/meetings.py:68) has no `calendar` field and is `Model` (extra=forbid), and the OpenAPI marks it additionalProperties:false.
  - Fix: Build the body from MeetingTypeIn fields only, e.g. `rows.map(({calendar, ...t}) => t)`. Better: send `{key, version, ...changed fields}` per edited type.
- **high** · correctness · `apps/web/src/components/v18/people.tsx:66` · HR card crashes for any employee without a score yet (HrEmployee.score is nullable)
  - What: `EmployeeHr` renders `faDecimal(e.score)` (which calls `n.toFixed`) and computes `e.score - e.score_prev`. The server sends `score: float | None` (routes/mgmt.py:87). domain/hr.py:152 sets it to None when the employee has no `core.employee_scores` row, for example a hire in their first month, who is still included in the round because they are active.
  - Fix: Type score as `number | null`. Render '—' when `e.score == null`, and compute `drop` only when both score and score_prev are non-null.
- **high** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/components/v30/lines.tsx:109` · LineTemplate.outputs_fa is list[str] in the API, but web and Android read it as a string (web crashes, Android gives contract_mismatch)
  - What: apps/api/src/hq_api/lines/templates.py:84 declares `outputs_fa: list[str]`, and its validator wraps the seed's string into `[str]`. Web v30.ts:46 types it `outputs_fa: string` and renders `<FaPara text={t.outputs_fa}>`. FaText then calls `bidiParts(text)` → `text.matchAll(...)` on an array. Android Models30.kt:38 has `val outputsFa: String = ""`. kotlinx cannot decode a JSON array into String (coerceInputValues only handles null and unknown enums).
  - Fix: Web: type it `outputs_fa: string[]` and render `t.outputs_fa.join('، ')` (also the mock). Android: change it to `val outputsFa: List<String> = emptyList()` and join it in Lines.kt:158.
- **high** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/components/v31/campaigns.tsx:146` · Campaign calendar always errors on a hub that serves /campaign-calendar
  - What: `useCampaignCalendar` (lib/api/v31.ts:111) expects the response to be either an array or `{days:[...]}` and does `r.days.flatMap(...)`. The hub's `CampaignCalendar` (apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/campaigns.py:61) is `{from, to, items: [...], holidays: [...]}`, with no `days` field.
  - Fix: In v31.ts handle the real shape: `if (r) return { items: Array.isArray(r) ? r : 'items' in r ? r.items : r.days.flatMap(...), lines_only: false }`, and type the response as `{from,to,items,holidays}`.
- **high** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/components/v31/kit.tsx:74` · Kit preview crashes: panel reads top-level lines/groups/kpis/todo_fa, but the hub nests them under `kit`
  - What: The hub's `KitPreview` (apps/api/src/hq_api/routes/kits.py:44) is `{kit: KitSummary, code, name_fa, name_en, code_free, problems, nothing_written}`. The panel's `KitPreview` type and KitFlow read `prev.lines.map(...)` (line 74), `prev.groups.length`, `prev.kpis.length`, `prev.connections.length` (line 76) and `prev.todo_fa.length` (line 80). All of these are undefined in the real response. Even under `prev.kit`, groups, kpis and connections are objects ({key,title_fa} / {kind,label_fa}), so `.join('، ')` would print «[object Object]».
  - Fix: Read the preview from `prev.kit` (`prev.kit.lines`, `prev.kit.groups.map(g => g.title_fa)`, `prev.kit.kpis.map(k => k.name_fa)`, `prev.kit.connections.map(c => c.label_fa)`, `prev.kit.todo_fa`) and correct the `KitPreview`/`Kit` types in lib/api/v31.ts to the hub schema.
- **high** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/components/v31/kit.tsx:74` · Company-from-kit flow is broken: the web preview crashes, and Android cannot decode the kit list
  - What: POST /companies/from-kit/preview returns `KitPreview{kit: KitSummary, code, name_fa, name_en, code_free, problems, nothing_written}` (routes/kits.py:44). Web v31.ts:47 expects top-level `lines/groups/kpis/connections/todo_fa`, and kit.tsx:74/76/80 calls `prev.lines.map`, `prev.groups.length` and `prev.todo_fa.length`, which are all undefined. GET /kits returns KitSummary whose `groups`, `kpis` and `connections` are objects (KitGroup{key,title_fa}, KitKpi, KitConnection; domain/kits.py:214-217). Android Kit (Models31.kt:38-48) declares them `List<String>`, and KitResult.lines (Models31.kt:67) is `List<String>`, but the API returns KitLine objects (routes/kits.py:63).
  - Fix: Web: read the preview from `prev.kit` (`prev.kit.lines`, `prev.kit.groups.map(g => g.title_fa)`, `prev.kit.kpis.map(k => k.name_fa)`, `prev.kit.connections.map(c => c.label_fa)`, `prev.kit.todo_fa`) and fix the Kit/KitPreview types. Android: model KitGroup/KitKpi/KitConnection as data classes (or List<JsonElement>), KitPreview as `{kit: Kit, code, codeFree, problems}`, and KitResult.lines as `List<KitLine>`.
- **high** · crash · `apps/web/src/components/work/voice-order.tsx:38` · Golden-button sheet crashes when a recent order has a «kit» route
  - What: `KIND` only maps 'work' | 'code' | 'meeting' | 'question', and `Route` reads `KIND[r.kind].icon` / `KIND[r.kind].fa` (lines 61-62) with no fallback. The hub's `OrderRoute.kind` enum is ['work','code','meeting','question','kit'] (apps/api/src/hq_api/routes/orders.py:102, and line 514 emits kind 'kit' for a «شرکت آماده» order). GET /orders returns these routes, and the sheet's «فرمان‌های اخیر» renders `o.routes.map((r) => <Route r={r} />)` (line 358).
