- **high** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/lib/api/mock-v31.ts:113` · Mock campaign calendar returns {days}, but the real hub returns {from,to,items,holidays}, and the client crashes on that
  - What: mock-v31 answers `GET /campaign-calendar` with `{ days: [{day, items}] }`. The OpenAPI `CampaignCalendar` schema (and apps/api tests/test_p22_growth.py) answers `{from, to, items, holidays}`, with no `days` key. The client in lib/api/v31.ts:111 does `Array.isArray(r) ? r : r.days.flatMap(...)`, which only works on the mock's shape. The e2e suite runs on this mock, so it passes and hides the crash.
  - Fix: In v31.ts useCampaignCalendar, read `r.items` first: `Array.isArray(r) ? r : Array.isArray(r.items) ? r.items : (r.days ?? []).flatMap(...)`. Change mock-v31 to return `{from, to, items, holidays: []}` so e2e tests the real shape.
- **high** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/lib/api/v14.ts:178` · Tools page crashes: GET /egress/summary returns an array of EgressDay, client expects one summary object
  - What: `useEgressSummary = () => useQuery({ queryFn: () => call<EgressSummary>('GET', '/egress/summary?period=day') })`. routes/egress.py:282 is `response_model=list[EgressDay]` (`{day, company, requests, blocked, bytes, browser_s, hosts}` per row). app/(app)/tools/page.tsx does `s.requests.toLocaleString(…)`, `s.bytes_in / 1e6`, `Object.entries(s.by_tool)`.
  - Fix: Type it as `EgressDay[]` and aggregate on the client (sum requests/blocked/bytes), or have the hub add the summary route; until then guard the page against the array shape.
- **high** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/lib/api/v29.ts:385` · Company connection secret is sealed on the wrong step-up target (slash instead of colon)
  - What: `secret: useMutation({ mutationFn: async (secret) => call('PUT', `/companies/${code}/connections/${kind}/secret`, { secret }, { 'X-Step-Up': await stepUp('connection.secret', target) }) })` with `const target = `${code}/${kind}`` (line 380). The hub (routes/connections.py:147) consumes `stepup.consume(..., "connection.secret", f"{code}:{kind}")` and `security/stepup.py consume` rejects any token whose stored target differs.
  - Fix: Use the hub's target for the secret: `stepUp('connection.secret', `${code}:${kind}`)` (keep `target` only where the hub uses it).
- **high** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/lib/api/v31.ts:111` · Campaign calendar crashes: hub returns `{from,to,items,holidays}`, client reads `r.days.flatMap`
  - What: `const r = await served(call<{ days: … } | CampaignItem[]>('GET', `/campaign-calendar?…`)); if (r) return { items: Array.isArray(r) ? r : r.days.flatMap(…), … }`. routes/campaigns.py returns `CP.CampaignCalendar` = `{from, to, items, holidays}` (no `days`).
  - Fix: Read `r.items` when present: `Array.isArray(r) ? r : 'items' in r ? r.items : r.days.flatMap(…)`, and type the response as the hub's CampaignCalendar.
- **medium** · rtl-persian · `apps/web/src/app/(app)/authority/page.tsx:50` · Limit input drops Persian digits and cannot take a decimal point
  - What: `Number(e.target.value.replace(/[^\d.]/g, '') || 0)`: JS `\d` matches only ASCII 0-9, so Persian (۰-۹) and Arabic-Indic digits are stripped. The controlled value is `String(num.value)`, so typing '2.' becomes Number('2.') = 2 and the dot vanishes at once.
  - Fix: Normalise Persian and Arabic digits to ASCII first. Keep the raw string in local state while editing and parse only on blur or save, so a trailing '.' is allowed.
- **medium** · correctness · `apps/web/src/app/(app)/cartable/page.tsx:43` · Cartable shows only the first 50 decisions and never pages; with more than 50 pending, the newest cards are invisible
  - What: `useDecisions({ status: tab, company, risk, sort })` sends no `limit` and no `cursor`, and the page never reads `next_cursor`. The API default is `limit=50` and the default order is oldest first (routes/decisions.py:181-188). The client-side sorts ('زودتر منقضی', 'تازه‌ترین') only reorder those 50 rows, and there is no 'بیشتر' button.
  - Fix: Use an infinite query with the cursor (or `limit: 200` plus a 'بیشتر' button driven by `next_cursor`). Show a count or notice when more exist.
- **medium** · state-bug · `apps/web/src/app/(app)/messages/page.tsx:129` · Chat is not keyed by thread: draft, voice note and live reply carry over to the next thread
  - What: `<Chat code={current.code} agentKind=... />` has no `key`. When the owner picks another thread on desktop (the list and the chat are both visible at lg), React reuses the same Chat instance. Composer state (`text`, `voice`, `files` in components/media/composer.tsx:86-89), `useLiveReply` state (`live`, plus the open EventSource for the old thread in chat.tsx:71-104) and `resolve` all stay. `useSend(code)` already points at the new thread.
  - Fix: Remount the chat for each thread: `<Chat key={current.code} code={current.code} ... />`.
- **medium** · data-loss · `apps/web/src/app/(app)/sites/page.tsx:48` · Saving the site list resets every site's custom `expect` to the default
  - What: EditSites builds rows as `{url, kind, method, interval_s, enabled}` and drops `s.expect`, although `Site` carries `expect`. `PUT /ops/sites` (routes/runs.py:321-325, 427-440) defaults `SiteIn.expect` to `site_seed.DEFAULT_EXPECT` and runs `UPDATE ops.sites SET ... expect = :expect` for every listed URL.
  - Fix: Carry `expect` through: map `expect: s.expect` into each row and send it back in the PUT body. New rows can omit it.
- **medium** · correctness · `apps/web/src/app/(app)/support/[ref]/page.tsx:132` · 'Send this draft' can send a customer the same message twice and wipes the owner's typed reply
  - What: `onSend={(x) => void send(x.body, crypto.randomUUID())}`: every click uses a new Idempotency-Key. The bubble's «همین را بفرست» button (line 44) has no `loading` or `disabled` while `act.reply` is pending. It also stays active while the main send is in flight. On success `send()` runs `setReply({ text: '', key: '' })` (line 84), which clears the textarea even though the owner did not send that text.
  - Fix: Pass `loading={act.reply.isPending}` (or `disabled`) to the bubble's send button. Derive the key per draft, e.g. `draft:${m.id}` hashed to a UUID or kept in a ref per message id, so a repeat is a replay. In `send`, clear `reply` only when the sent text is `reply.text`.
- **medium** · security · `apps/web/src/app/(auth)/login/page.tsx:23` · Open redirect after passkey sign-in via a backslash or tab in ?next=
  - What: `const target = next && next.startsWith('/') && !next.startsWith('//') ? next : '/'` then `router.replace(target)`. A value like `/\evil.com` (sent as `?next=/%5Cevil.com`) or `/%09/evil.com` passes both checks. The Next 16 app router resolves the href with `new URL(href, location.href)`. For https, the WHATWG parser treats `\` as `/` and drops tab characters, so the URL becomes `https://evil.com/`. The router sees an external origin and calls `location.replace` to it.
  - Fix: Accept only same-origin paths. Example: `const u = new URL(next, location.origin); target = u.origin === location.origin ? u.pathname + u.search + u.hash : '/'`. Or reject any `next` containing `\` or a control character, or not matching `^/[A-Za-z0-9_\-/?=&%.~]*$`.
- **medium** · correctness · `apps/web/src/components/canvas/office-canvas.tsx:75` · Canvas drag treats a slow drag as a click (position not saved) and a jittery tap as a move
  - What: `moved: d.moved || Math.hypot(p.px - d.px, p.py - d.py) > 0.012` compares each pointermove with the previous move, not with where the drag started (`setDrag` overwrites px/py on every move). `onNodeDown` also seeds px/py with the node's centre (`at(n.code)`), not the pointer position.
  - Fix: Keep the start point in the drag state (`sx, sy` from `point(e)` in onNodeDown) and the grab offset. Set `moved` when the distance from the start passes the threshold, and place the node at pointer minus offset.
- **medium** · state · `apps/web/src/components/look/session.tsx:225` · NowBar's `held` flag never resets when work resumes on its own, so a finished turn is shown as «کار نگه داشته شد»
  - What: `held` is set to true after «نگه دار» and only cleared in the onSuccess of this bar's own «ادامه بده» (line 241). `stopped = held && !live`, and the bar renders whenever `live || held`. If work restarts another way (the owner types in the composer, KnotCard's or IdleNudge's «ادامه بده», a ready answer), `live` turns true and the working view shows, but `held` stays true. When that work ends normally, `live` goes false.
  - Fix: Reset `held` when work goes live again, e.g. `useEffect(() => { if (live) setHeld(false); }, [live])`, or track the held work ref and clear it once activity shows that work running.
- **medium** · correctness · `apps/web/src/components/v15/treasury.tsx:95` · Budget editor cannot remove a cap: clearing a field is dropped from the PUT, yet the sheet says empty means no cap
  - What: `save` sends only `rows.filter((r) => r.tokens !== '' || r.usd !== '')`, always with `enabled: true`. PUT /budgets upserts per (scope_type, scope_ref, period) (`ON CONFLICT … DO UPDATE`, treasury.py:1041). Rows that are left out keep their stored limit, and the client never sends `enabled: false`. The sheet's description says «خالی یعنی بی‌سقف». If every field is empty, `items` is [] and the server answers 422 (BudgetsPut min_length=1).
  - Fix: For a row that had a stored budget and is now fully empty, send it with `enabled: false` (keep the old limits so the validator passes), or call the API's delete or disable path. Do not silently omit it. Disable Save when nothing would be sent.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/components/v16/support.tsx:316` · Failed history import is reported as success, and the case count reads a field the API does not return
  - What: POST /support/history returns 201 SupportHistoryOut `{id, company, state, format, pairs_found, cases_kept, topics, suite, error}`. When parsing fails, `shadow.import_history` stores state 'failed' with `error` (e.g. 'no question and answer pairs', domain/shadow.py:259-269) and still returns 201. The client types the answer as `{ suite?: string; cases?: number }` (v16.ts:194). It always toasts «مجموعه‌ی آزمون … ساخته شد … حالا اجرا کن.» and reads `out.cases`, which never exists (the field is `cases_kept`).
  - Fix: Type the response as SupportHistoryOut. If `state === 'failed'`, show an error toast with `error`. Otherwise show `cases_kept`.
- **medium** · ui · `apps/web/src/components/v17/cards.tsx:104` · Numeric inputs that re-parse on every keystroke swallow the decimal point
  - What: The KPI target input stores `Number(e.target.value.replace(/[^\d.]/g, ''))` and renders `value={k.target}`. Typing '1.' gives Number('1.') = 1, so the field shows '1' and the dot is gone. The minus sign is stripped too, so negative targets are impossible. The same pattern is in settings.tsx:72 (behind_ratio: `Number(e.target.value) || 0`, so '0.' becomes 0).
  - Fix: Keep the raw string in edit state (as PrepRoom's cap does) and convert to a number only on save, after validating it with a regex such as `/^-?\d+(\.\d+)?$/`.
- **medium** · ui · `apps/web/src/components/v17/charter.tsx:187` · Red-line pattern textarea swallows Enter at the end, so a second pattern cannot be typed
  - What: onChange runs `e.target.value.split('\n').filter((x, k, a) => x.trim() || k < a.length - 1)`. That keeps blank lines in the middle but drops a blank last line. Pressing Enter after the first pattern produces 'abc\n', so ['abc',''] is filtered to ['abc'], and the textarea re-renders as 'abc' without the newline. Blank middle lines are kept and then rejected by the server (domain/charter.py RedLine._compiles: 'a pattern is 1..300 characters').
  - Fix: Keep the raw textarea text in local state and derive `patterns` on save as `text.split('\n').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean)`.
- **medium** · correctness · `apps/web/src/components/v17/knowledge.tsx:28` · Knowledge page crashes on the employee layer (valid_until is null)
  - What: `Expiry` always calls `jDate(validIso(e.valid_until))`, where `validIso = d => `${d}T20:29:00Z``. For employee-layer entries (memory notes) the server sends `valid_until: null` (domain/knowledge.py render_memory: `valid_until=None`; the OpenAPI marks it nullable). That gives `new Date('nullT20:29:00Z')`, an Invalid Date, and `Intl.DateTimeFormat.format` throws RangeError: Invalid time value. EntryCard renders Expiry for every listed entry. The knowledge page offers the 'employee' layer filter (LAYERS includes it), and the server returns render_memory rows for `layer=employee`.
  - Fix: In Expiry, return null (or «بی‌انقضا») when `!e.valid_until`, and type valid_until as `string | null`. Make employee entries read-only cards that do not open EntrySheet, or show q.error in EntrySheet.
- **medium** · rtl-persian · `apps/web/src/components/v18/settings.tsx:48` · Persian digits typed into numeric fields silently become 0 or 1
  - What: Several inputs strip non-ASCII digits with `replace(/\D/g,'')` or `/[^\d.]/g` (JS \d is ASCII-only) and then fall back to a default. Affected: settings.tsx:48 and 53 (`|| 0`: auto-memo and proactive caps); meetings.tsx:739 (budget_tokens `|| 0`); substitutes.tsx:119-120 (per_day `|| 0`); series.tsx:321 (`|| 1`); cards.tsx:104 (target: '۵۰' becomes '', and Number('') is 0 because the trimmed raw text is non-empty) and cards.tsx:108 (`|| 1`); meetings.tsx:487 (cap: Persian digits are dropped). Other screens normalise ۰-۹ (v14/hiring.tsx:132, v20/schema.tsx:287, v21/control.tsx:554), but these do not.
  - Fix: Normalise ۰-۹ (and ٫) to ASCII before parsing, using one shared helper such as the one in v20/schema.tsx, and treat unparseable input as invalid instead of 0.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/components/v19/org.tsx:59` · Role-draft sheet crashes: `written_by` is a string code, but it is passed to ActorAvatar as an Actor object
  - What: RoleDraft.written_by is `str | None` (apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/role_drafts.py:160, set from `written_by_code`, line 220). Web v19.ts:67 types it `Actor`, and org.tsx:59 renders `<ActorAvatar a={d.written_by}>`. avatarKind (components/v17/bits.tsx:74-81) on a string reaches `a.code.endsWith('-CEO')` with `a.code === undefined`.
  - Fix: Type `written_by?: string | null` and render it as `<ActorAvatar a={{ kind: 'agent', code: d.written_by }} />` with `ACTOR_FA({ kind: 'agent', code: d.written_by })`, or return an Actor from the API.
- **medium** · data-loss · `apps/web/src/components/v19/series.tsx:211` · Editing a series with a schedule the simple parser cannot read silently replaces it with «Sat+Tue 18:30»
  - What: `rule0 = (s && parseRrule(s.schedule)) || { freq: 'WEEKLY', days: ['SA','TU'], hour: 18, minute: 30 }`, and submit always sends `schedule: buildRrule(rule)`. parseRrule returns null for anything other than DAILY or WEEKLY with a single BYHOUR and BYMINUTE. The server's recurrence parser accepts more: several BYHOUR values, MONTHLY/BYMONTHDAY, HOURLY with INTERVAL. SeriesCard shows such schedules raw, so they occur.
  - Fix: When parseRrule(s.schedule) is null, keep the original string untouched unless the owner explicitly edits the schedule. For example, hold `rawSchedule` and send it as-is, and show the raw rule with a «تغییر زمان‌بندی» control.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/components/v20/record.tsx:91` · DraftPanel sends a lower `level` without the passkey; the hub requires archive.declassify, so the whole save fails
  - What: The draft form shows the level select (RecordForm, hideLevel unset). RecordForm.setClass (records.tsx:119) also resets `level` to the new class's `default_level`. `patch()` then sends `level: d.level` whenever it differs from r.level, with no X-Step-Up. The hub's PATCH (routes/archive.py:396-404) consumes the `archive.declassify` step-up whenever the new rank is lower, even for a draft. SealPanel's level buttons, which do the passkey, are hidden for drafts (`r.state !== 'draft'`).
  - Fix: In DraftPanel, when `m.rank(d.level) < m.rank(r.level)`, either send the level through `act.declassify` (passkey) after the other fields, or don't let setClass/the select lower the level of an existing record (keep max(rank) as `apply()` already does).
- **medium** · error-handling · `apps/web/src/components/v20/record.tsx:564` · «ثبت کن» on a draft swallows the filing error: DraftPanel remounts on the patch's new version
  - What: `<DraftPanel key={r.version} r={r} />`. `file()` first awaits `patch()`, whose onSuccess (`done` in lib/api/v20.ts:221) awaits invalidation of ['archive']. TanStack v5 awaits that before mutateAsync resolves, so the record refetches with version+1 and the panel remounts. `act.file.mutateAsync(saved)` then runs from the old, unmounted instance, and its `catch` calls `setErrors`/`setFailure` on a dead component. DraftPanel also never checks `required_files`, so the hub's `422 file_ids: needs at least N file(s)` (records.py:388) is a normal outcome.
  - Fix: Don't key the panel by version (key by r.ref), or report the filing error through a toast or state that outlives the remount (e.g. `toast({tone:'error', text: faError(e)})` in the catch). Also check `c.required_files` against the current version's file count before enabling «ثبت کن».
- **medium** · correctness · `apps/web/src/components/v20/records.tsx:97` · Clearing a metadata field never reaches the hub: empty values are dropped and PATCH merges
  - What: `metaFor` returns `cleanMeta(...)`, which filters out every `emptyValue` (null, '', [], money without amount). MetadataPanel.save (record.tsx:145) and DraftPanel.patch (record.tsx:91) send that as `metadata`. The hub's patch (apps/api/src/hq_api/archive/records.py:730-732) does `merged = {**meta, **body.metadata}` and then drops None values. A key that is missing from the body therefore keeps its stored value. The UI has no way to send `null` for a cleared field.
  - Fix: For PATCH, send cleared keys explicitly as null: build the body from the class's fields, mapping empty values to `null` for keys that exist in `r.metadata`, e.g. `Object.fromEntries(keys.map(k => [k, emptyValue(d.metadata[k]) ? (k in r.metadata ? null : undefined) : d.metadata[k]]))` (dropping undefined). Keep `cleanMeta` only for create.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/components/v20/records.tsx:233` · Archive intake blames the wrong file for a secret refusal (reads file name from `fields`, hub sends `file_ids`)
  - What: On `422 secret_in_document` the sheet does `const names = Object.keys(e.fields ?? {}); const hit = list.find((u) => ... names.includes(u.file.name)) ?? list.find((u) => u.id === ids[0]);`. The real hub (apps/api/src/hq_api/archive/store.py:240-245) raises `fields={"file_ids": str(fid), "kinds": ...}`, so the keys are `file_ids`/`kinds`, never a file name (only mock-v20.ts:490 uses `{[name]: ...}`). The name lookup never matches, so the fallback always marks `ids[0]`, the first file, as refused. The id of the refused file is in `e.fields.file_ids`.
  - Fix: Pick the refused file by id: `const bad = e.fields?.file_ids; const hit = list.find((u) => u.id === bad && !u.refused);` (fall back to ids[0] only when `file_ids` is absent). Update mock-v20 to send the same `{file_ids, kinds}` shape.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/components/v29/rules.tsx:65` · Rule edit always fails with 422, after the owner has already given the passkey
  - What: «مُهر نسخه‌ی تازه» calls `act.edit.mutate({ ref, text })`. `useRuleActions().edit` (lib/api/v29.ts:244) first runs `stepUp('rule.seal', ref)` (the passkey ceremony) and then PUTs `{ text: v.text }`. The hub's `RuleEdit` (apps/api/src/hq_api/routes/rules.py:43) is `Model` (extra forbid) with fields `text_md` and `applies_to` only.
  - Fix: In v29.ts send `{ text_md: v.text }`.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/components/v30/lines.tsx:67` · Every line card is titled with the company's name instead of the line's name
  - What: LineCard shows `line.name_fa || line.title_fa`. In the hub, `Line.name_fa` is the company's name: `coalesce(c.name_fa, c.code) AS name_fa` (apps/api/src/hq_api/lines/store.py:188). It is always non-empty, so `title_fa` (the line's own name, which the owner edits on the line page) is never shown.
  - Fix: Show `line.title_fa` (and fix the misleading `name_fa` comment in lib/api/v30.ts).
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/components/v31/automations.tsx:86` · An automation's run history is always shown as «هنوز اجرا نشده»
  - What: `runs = Array.isArray(det.data.runs) ? det.data.runs : []`. The hub's `AutomationDetail` returns `runs` as an integer and the history in `run_log` (items `{key, trigger, outcome, steps, error_fa, at}`), see routes/automations.py:227. The rows also read `r.ok` / `r.note_fa`, but the hub sends `outcome` / `error_fa`.
  - Fix: Use `det.data.run_log ?? []` and render `outcome` ('ok'|'failed'|'skipped') and `error_fa`.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/components/v31/campaigns.tsx:219` · Editing a campaign always fails with 422 (sends id and company to an extra-forbid model)
  - What: `save()` calls `act.edit.mutate({ ...f, id: f.id })`. `useCampaignActions().edit` (lib/api/v31.ts:123) PUTs the whole object `b`, including `id` and `company`. The hub's `CampaignPut` (domain/campaigns.py:79) extends `Model` with `extra="forbid"` (hq_api/schemas.py:18) and has no `id` or `company` field.
  - Fix: Send only the editable fields: `const { id, company, ...rest } = b; call('PUT', `/campaigns/${enc(id)}`, rest)`.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/components/v31/company.tsx:67` · New newsletter campaign always 422, and scheduled time is never shown
  - What: The create body is `{list, subject, subject_b, body_md}`. The hub's `NewsletterCampaignIn` (apps/api/src/hq_api/domain/newsletter.py:88) requires `body` (min_length 1) and takes `list_id` and `content_type`. The fields `list`, `subject_b` and `body_md` are ignored and `body` is missing. The list also renders `c.scheduled_at` (line 78), but `CampaignOut` names the field `send_at`.
  - Fix: Send `{ list_id: form.list, subject, body: form.body_md, content_type: 'markdown' }` (drop subject_b or add it to the hub), and read `c.send_at`.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/components/v31/company.tsx:140` · Keyword switch, competitor edit and ad-rule switch/edit always 422 (whole row PUT to extra-forbid models)
  - What: `useCrud().edit` (lib/api/v31.ts:91) PUTs the whole object. The keyword switch sends `{...w, state}` (with id, company, day, position, source). Competitor edit sends `{...b, id}` (line 165). The ad-rule switch and edit send `{...x, state}` / `{...r, id}` (lines 246, 256), which carry id, company, platform, summary_fa, created_at and updated_at. `KeywordPut`, `CompetitorPut` (domain/seo.py:68,107) and `AdRuleEdit` (domain/ads.py:96) all extend `Model` with extra='forbid'.
  - Fix: In useCrud.edit strip non-editable keys and send only the editable fields (e.g. `{ state }` for the switches; the form fields without id for the edits).
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/components/v31/company.tsx:194` · Adding an ad rule always 422: `platform` missing and default daily_cap 0 violates gt=0
  - What: `RULE0` is `{scope, daily_cap: 0, window_days, min_roas, min_spend_before_judging, action, state}` and `act.add.mutate(r)` posts it as is. The hub's `AdRuleIn` (domain/ads.py:74) requires `platform` ('google_ads'|'meta_ads'), and `daily_cap` must be > 0 or null. The form has no platform picker, and `Num` coerces an empty field to 0, so a null cap cannot be sent either.
  - Fix: Add a platform choice (from the company's ad accounts) to the form and body, and send `daily_cap: null` when it is empty or 0.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/components/v31/company.tsx:211` · Ads tab reads fields the hub does not send: every campaign shows untitled and «متوقف», resume never offered
  - What: The hub's `AdCampaign` (domain/ads.py:145) is `{platform, campaign_id, name, status, paused_by, totals, days, ...}` and `AdAccount` has no `account_id`. The panel reads `c.id`, `c.title_fa`, `c.state`, `c.spend`, `c.roas`, `c.paused_by_rule` and `a.account_id`. In addition `useResumeCampaign` steps up with target `v.id`, while the hub consumes `ads.resume` with target `<platform>:<campaign_id>` (routes/ads.py:220).
  - Fix: Map to the hub's shape: `key={c.platform-c.campaign_id}`, `c.name`, `c.status`, `c.totals.spend/roas`, `c.paused_by === 'rule'`. Resume with `{platform, id: c.campaign_id}` and step-up target `${platform}:${campaign_id}`. Key accounts by platform and show `label_fa`.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/components/v32/talk.tsx:171` · New conversation after a manager talk is created as persona 'manager' without role/company → 422
  - What: `open(r, persona)` sets `face='manager'` for any 'role@CO' persona (line 179), and PlusSheet's onDone does `setFace('manager')`. «گفتگوی تازه» calls `open(null, face)`, then the first message runs `ensure()` → `act.create.mutateAsync({ persona: face })` = `{persona:'manager'}`. The hub's `TalkConversationIn` validator (routes/talk.py:190) rejects a manager without role and company.
  - Fix: In `open(null, …)` / onNew, reset a 'manager' face to 'secretary', or keep the role and company and pass them to create.
- **medium** · rtl-persian · `apps/web/src/components/v33/sales.tsx:95` · Web number inputs delete Persian digits (JS \d and \D are ASCII-only), so values are silently saved as 0 or null
  - What: Many owner inputs clean their text with `/\D/g` or `/[^\d.]/g`. In JavaScript `\d` matches only [0-9], so every Persian digit typed on a Persian layout (۰-۹) is removed. Examples: sales.tsx:95 `setValue(v.replace(/[^\d.]/g, ''))`, then line 96 `value_usdt: value ? Number(value) : null`; v17/cards.tsx:104 KPI target `Number(e.target.value.replace(/[^\d.]/g, ''))`; app/(app)/authority/page.tsx:50 `Number(... || 0)`; v18/settings.tsx:48 and 53 advisor caps `Number(e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, '') || 0)`; v17/meetings.tsx:739; v18/substitutes.tsx:119-120; v19/series.tsx:300 and 321; v18/creatives.tsx:146. Other parts of the same app convert Persian digits (v20/schema.tsx:512, v14/hiring.tsx:132, v21/control.tsx:554), so these inputs are the exceptions.
  - Fix: Map Persian and Arabic-Indic digits to ASCII before filtering (one shared helper, e.g. `s.replace(/[۰-۹]/g, d => String(d.charCodeAt(0) - 0x6f0)).replace(/[٠-٩]/g, d => String(d.charCodeAt(0) - 0x660)).replace(/٫/g, '.')`). Use it in every one of these onChange handlers. In sales.tsx, never send null when the field was emptied by stripping.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/lib/api/mock-v29.ts:573` · Mock group KPIs are a flat array; the real GroupKpis object is parsed as empty, so the KPI card never shows numbers
  - What: `GET /groups/{key}/kpis` in the mock returns `kpiRows(co)`, a flat array of `{company,key,title_fa,unit,series}`. The real route (apps/api routes/groups.py:417, schema GroupKpis) returns `{group, days, companies:[{code, name_fa, kpis:[{key,title_fa,unit,source,series}]}]}`. `parseKpis` in lib/api/v29.ts:315 calls `listOf(x)`, which returns `x.items ?? []`. The real object has no `items`, so the result is always `[]`.
  - Fix: In parseKpis, when `x` is an object with `companies`, flatten it: `x.companies.flatMap(c => c.kpis.map(k => ({company: c.code, ...k})))` before the current row loop. Make mock-v29 return `{group:'content', days, companies:[...]}`.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/lib/api/mock-v31.ts:83` · Newsletter mock accepts the client's {list, subject_b, body_md}; the real hub requires {subject, body, list_id} and sends `send_at`
  - What: The mock creates a campaign from any body (`b.list`, `b.subject_b`) and reports times as `scheduled_at`. The real `NewsletterCampaignIn` requires `subject` and `body` (optional `list_id`, `content_type`), and `CampaignOut` has `send_at`/`list_id`, not `scheduled_at`/`list`. The client in lib/api/v31.ts:73 sends `{list, subject, subject_b, body_md}` without `body`, and components/v31/company.tsx:78 shows `c.scheduled_at`.
  - Fix: In v31.ts send `{subject, body: body_md, list_id: list || null, content_type: 'markdown'}`. Map `send_at` to the shown time (and `list_id` to the list). Make mock-v31 check the same required fields and return `send_at`.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/lib/api/mock-v31.ts:125` · Ads mock uses invented field names; on the real hub campaign rows are blank and a paused campaign can't be resumed
  - What: The mock returns campaigns `{platform, id, title_fa, state, paused_by_rule, spend, revenue, roas}` and accounts `{company, platform, account_id, state}`. The real `Ads` schema has AdCampaign `{platform, campaign_id, name, status, paused_by, paused_at, paused_reason_fa, totals, days}` and AdAccount `{platform, label_fa, state, secret, missing_fa, ...}`. components/v31/company.tsx:211-223 reads `a.account_id`, `c.id`, `c.title_fa`, `c.spend`, `c.roas`, `c.state`, `c.paused_by_rule`.
  - Fix: Align the v31.ts types and company.tsx with the contract: `campaign_id`, `name`, `status`, `paused_by === 'rule'`, `totals.spend`/`totals.roas`, account `label_fa`. Make mock-v31 return the contract shape.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/lib/api/v14.ts:61` · Finding status mutation allows statuses the hub refuses (`open`, …)
  - What: `findingStatus: (v: { ref; status: FindingStatus; note }) => call('POST', `/oversight/findings/${v.ref}/status`, …)` with FindingStatus = open|acknowledged|fixing|fixed|verified|disputed|closed. routes/oversight.py:66 `FindingStatusIn.status: Literal['verified','closed','disputed']`. app/(app)/oversight/page.tsx:123 sends `set('open')` for «دوباره باز کن».
  - Fix: Restrict the mutation's status to 'verified' | 'closed' | 'disputed' and change the reopen button to send 'disputed'.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/lib/api/v16.ts:169` · Shadow test result read as `pass` but GET /support/shadow sends `pass_`
  - What: `ShadowReport = { …; pass: boolean; … }`; consumers (support/page.tsx:36, components/v16/support.tsx:156,325) read `r.pass`. domain/shadow.py:91 declares `pass_: bool` with no alias; only `as_payload()` (the decision card payload) renames it to `pass`. The GET route returns the model directly (openapi shows `pass_`).
  - Fix: Read `r.pass ?? r.pass_` (type both), or have the hub serialise the field with alias `pass`.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/lib/api/v17.ts:99` · Meeting settings save echoes GET rows (with `calendar`) into a PUT whose items forbid it
  - What: `useSaveMeetingSettings` sends `{ types }` where the caller passes `rows = q.data.map((t) => ({ ...t, ...edit[t.key] }))` (components/v17/meetings.tsx:715). GET MeetingType always includes `calendar` (default 'gregorian'); PUT items are `MeetingTypeIn` (additionalProperties false) without `calendar`.
  - Fix: Map each row to MeetingTypeIn's keys only (key, version, title_fa, schedule, members, max_rounds, max_speakers, budget_tokens, turn_cap_tokens, owner_presence, enabled, model_summary) before hashing and sending.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/lib/api/v20.ts:179` · Archive schema retire preview posts `?dry_run=1` to a route without dry-run, so it always 403s
  - What: `retirePreview: call('POST', `/archive/schema/${part}/${key}/retire?dry_run=1`, {part,key,version})` with no X-Step-Up. routes/archive.py:888 `archive_retire_schema` has no `dry_run` parameter and calls `stepup.consume` unconditionally → 403 step_up_required. components/v20/schema.tsx:665 only opens the confirm (and the real retire) after this preview succeeds.
  - Fix: Drop the preview call (show the impact from a PUT dry-run or from the row's `records`) and go straight to `retire` with the step-up, or add `dry_run` to the hub route.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/lib/api/v20.ts:179` · Archive schema «retire» preview sends `?dry_run=1`, which the retire route ignores, so the call fails with 403 and retiring is unreachable
  - What: POST /archive/schema/{part}/{key}/retire (routes/archive.py:888) has no `dry_run` parameter and always calls `stepup.consume`, which raises `403 step_up_required` when there is no X-Step-Up header (security/stepup.py:57). Only the PUT route supports dry_run (archive.py:831). Web retirePreview and Android schemaRetirePreview (Api.kt:1205) both send the preview without a step-up, and the confirm dialog that does the sealed retire opens only after the preview succeeds (web schema.tsx:665, Android Schema.kt:837).
  - Fix: Add `dry_run: bool = Query(False)` to archive_retire_schema and return the impact before `stepup.consume` when it is set, as the PUT route does. Or have the clients compute the preview through the PUT dry run or skip the preview.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/lib/api/v29.ts:243` · Rule edit sends `{text}`; hub's RuleEdit accepts only `text_md`/`applies_to` (extra forbidden)
  - What: `edit: useMutation({ mutationFn: async (v) => call<Rule>('PUT', `/rules/${enc(v.ref)}`, { text: v.text }, { 'X-Step-Up': await stepUp('rule.seal', v.ref) }) })`. routes/rules.py:43 `class RuleEdit(Model): text_md, applies_to` with a validator requiring one of them; Model forbids extra keys.
  - Fix: Send `{ text_md: v.text }`.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/lib/api/v29.ts:333` · Group KPI series are always empty: hub returns `{group, days, companies:[{code,kpis:[…]}]}`
  - What: `useGroupKpis` → `parseKpis(await call('GET', `/groups/${key}/kpis?…`))`; `parseKpis` starts with `listOf(x)` which for an object returns `x.items ?? []`. The hub's GroupKpis is `{companies: CompanyKpis[{code, name_fa, kpis: KpiSeries[]}], days, group}` — no `items`.
  - Fix: In parseKpis, when `x.companies` is an array flatten it: `x.companies.flatMap((c) => c.kpis.map((k) => ({ company: c.code, ...k })))` before grouping.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/lib/api/v29.ts:383` · Widening a company connection's config is sealed on `code/kind` but the hub binds it to the body hash
  - What: `config: withStepUp('connection.config', target, (h) => call('PUT', `/companies/${code}/connections/${kind}`, { config }, h))`. The hub (routes/connections.py:100-101) consumes `"connection.config", await stepup.body_target(request)` = SHA-256 of the exact body when the change widens what is read.
  - Fix: Compute the target from the exact body: `const body = { config }; withStepUp('connection.config', await bodyTarget(JSON.stringify(body)), (h) => call('PUT', …, body, h))`.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/lib/api/v31.ts:73` · Newsletter campaign create sends `list`/`body_md`; hub requires `body` (and reads `list_id`)
  - What: `create: (b: { list; subject; subject_b?; body_md }) => call('POST', `/companies/${code}/newsletter/campaigns`, b)`. domain/newsletter.py:88 NewsletterCampaignIn = `{subject (req), body (req, min 1), content_type, list_id}`; extra keys are ignored. The GET shape also differs (`list_id`, `send_at` vs client `list`, `scheduled_at`).
  - Fix: Send `{ list_id: b.list, subject, body: b.body_md, content_type: 'markdown' }` and type NlCampaign from the hub (`list_id`, `send_at`).
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/lib/api/v31.ts:94` · Keyword/competitor/ad-rule edits send `id` (and other read-only fields) in a PUT body the hub forbids
  - What: `edit: useMutation({ mutationFn: (b: Partial<T> & { id: string }) => call<T>('PUT', `${base}/${enc(b.id)}`, b) })` sends the whole object. KeywordPut/CompetitorPut/AdRuleEdit are `Model` with `extra="forbid"` (schemas.py:17); callers pass `{ ...w, state }`, `{ ...b, id }`, `{ ...x, state }` which carry `id`, `company`, `created_at`, `summary_fa`, `platform`…
  - Fix: Strip the id and read-only fields before sending: `mutationFn: ({ id, ...rest }) => call('PUT', `${base}/${enc(id)}`, pick(rest, allowedKeys))` with the PUT model's keys per resource.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/lib/api/v31.ts:111` · Campaign calendar always errors: GET /campaign-calendar returns {from,to,items,holidays}, but the web reads `r.days`
  - What: The schema CampaignCalendar has required `items`, `holidays`, `from` and `to`, and no `days`. useCampaignCalendar does `Array.isArray(r) ? r : r.days.flatMap(...)`. Android handles both shapes (Api.kt:1639 itemsOrSelf); the web does not.
  - Fix: `Array.isArray(r) ? r : 'items' in r ? r.items : r.days.flatMap(...)`. Also type the object shape as `{ from; to; items: CampaignItem[]; holidays: ... }`.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/lib/api/v31.ts:123` · Campaign edit PUTs the whole campaign including `id` and `company` to a forbid-extra model
  - What: `edit: useMutation({ mutationFn: (b: Campaign) => call<Campaign>('PUT', `/campaigns/${enc(b.id)}`, b) })`. CampaignPut (domain/campaigns.py:79, extra forbid) has only title_fa, goal_fa, starts, ends, channels, budget, state; components/v31/campaigns.tsx calls `act.edit.mutate({ ...f, id: f.id })`.
  - Fix: Send only CampaignPut fields: `({ id, company: _c, ...rest }) => call('PUT', `/campaigns/${enc(id)}`, rest)`.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/lib/api/v31.ts:130` · Ad rule creation never sends the required `platform`
  - What: `AdRule` has no `platform` field, and the create path `add: (b: Partial<T>) => call('POST', base, b)` is fed `RULE0 = { scope, daily_cap, window_days, min_roas, min_spend_before_judging, action, state }` (components/v31/company.tsx:194). The hub's AdRuleIn (domain/ads.py:74) requires `platform: 'google_ads'|'meta_ads'`.
  - Fix: Add `platform` to `AdRule`/the create payload and let the form choose it (default to the company's only ad account's platform).
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/lib/api/v31.ts:133` · Ads types do not match the hub's `Ads` response: campaigns render blank and resume is unreachable
  - What: Client `AdCampaign = { platform, id, title_fa, state: 'active'|'paused', paused_by_rule, spend, revenue, roas }`, `AdAccount = { company, platform, account_id, state }`. The hub's GET /companies/{code}/ads returns `AdCampaign{campaign_id, days, name, paused_at, paused_by, paused_reason_fa, platform, status, totals}` and `AdAccount{label_fa, last_error_fa, last_read_at, missing_fa, platform, secret, state}` (openapi). components/v31/company.tsx reads `c.title_fa`, `c.id`, `c.state`, `c.spend`, `a.account_id`.
  - Fix: Type `AdCampaign`/`AdAccount` from the hub's schema (`campaign_id`, `name`, `status`, `totals`, `paused_by`, `label_fa`…) and read those fields in the page.
- **medium** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/lib/api/v31.ts:141` · Ad campaign resume is sealed on the campaign id only; hub expects `<platform>:<campaign id>`
  - What: `withStepUp('ads.resume', v.id, (h) => call('POST', `/ads/campaigns/${platform}/${id}/resume`, {}, h))`. routes/ads.py:220 consumes `"ads.resume", f"{platform}:{campaign_id}"`.
  - Fix: `withStepUp('ads.resume', `${v.platform}:${v.id}`, …)`.
- **medium** · error-handling · `apps/web/src/lib/error-upload.ts:64` · Error reports are sent with keepalive in batches of up to 50, which can pass the 64 KiB keepalive limit and then block every later upload
  - What: `post()` builds `new Request(..., { method: 'POST', keepalive: true, body: JSON.stringify(body) })` and `flush()` sends `batch.slice(0, BATCH)` with `BATCH = 50` (line 17). The Fetch spec, and Chrome and Firefox in practice, reject a keepalive request whose body is over 64 KiB with a network error (TypeError). An ordinary http/warning entry carries up to 10 breadcrumbs of about 93 chars each, plus headers, a message, a title, a path and a request id. That is roughly 1.3 to 1.6 KB of JSON (more with Persian text, which is multi-byte UTF-8), and a crash entry carries a stack of up to 6000 chars. About 45 ordinary entries, or about 11 crashes, are enough to pass 64 KiB. When the fetch throws, `flush` takes the `catch { emit({ failing: true }); return; }` branch without marking anything sent. The next flush rebuilds the same oldest-first batch from `due(...)`, so it fails the same way every time.
  - Fix: Only use `keepalive` for the hidden-tab flush, and cap that body under 60 KiB. Better: build each batch by size, adding entries while `JSON.stringify(part).length` stays under about 60_000 bytes (and fewer than 50 entries), and drop keepalive on the regular timer flushes.
- **medium** · data-loss · `apps/web/src/lib/errorlog.ts:184` · After «پاک کن» and a reload, new log entries reuse old ids that the upload map already marks as sent, so they are never uploaded
  - What: `start()` sets `this.seq = this.entries.reduce((m, e) => Math.max(m, e.id), 0)`. `clear()` (line 241, called from app/(app)/errors/page.tsx:179) stores `entries: []`, so on the next load `seq` starts again at 0 and new entries get ids 1, 2, 3... The upload state in error-upload.ts (`hq.errors.sent`, keyed by `String(e.id)` and reset only when the app version changes) still holds the counts of the old entries with those ids. `due()` keeps an entry only when `(sent[String(e.id)] ?? 0) < e.count`. A new entry with count 1 and an id whose old entry was sent with count ≥ 1 is never due.
  - Fix: Store the sequence high-water mark next to the entries (for example `{ app, entries, seq }`) and restore `seq = max(stored.seq, max id)` in `start()`. Alternatively, `clear()` can also reset the `hq.errors.sent` map for this version.
- **low** · race-condition · `apps/web/src/app/(app)/archive/page.tsx:60` · Search box can be overwritten with an older query while the owner keeps typing
  - What: `if (q !== seenQ) { setSeenQ(q); if (q !== text.trim()) setText(q); }` treats every URL change as an outside link. The debounced `go()` calls `router.replace`, whose URL update arrives asynchronously after an RSC round trip. If the owner types again in that window, the arriving older `q` replaces the newer text.
  - Fix: Remember the last value this component pushed (a ref) and skip `setText` when `q` equals it, or only sync from the URL when the box is not focused.
- **low** · error-handling · `apps/web/src/app/(app)/assistant/page.tsx:26` · Cancelling an errand fails silently
  - What: `act.cancel.mutateAsync(e.ref).then(onClose)` has no `.catch`. A rejected mutation becomes an unhandled promise rejection with no toast.
  - Fix: Add `.catch((x) => toast({ tone: 'error', text: x instanceof ApiError ? x.messageFa : 'لغو نشد.' }))`.
- **low** · security · `apps/web/src/app/(app)/audit/page.tsx:18` · CSV export is open to formula injection (audit and resolutions 'Excel' buttons)
  - What: `downloadCsv` (lib/api/v15.ts:188) only quotes cells: `"${String(v).replaceAll('"','""')}"`. Excel and LibreOffice still evaluate a quoted cell that starts with `=`, `+`, `-` or `@`. The audit export writes `e.summary` and target refs; resolutions/page.tsx:31-32 writes `r.title`/`r.body`. Agents write that text (proposals become resolutions; summaries carry titles).
  - Fix: In downloadCsv, prefix any cell whose first character is one of `= + - @ \t \r` with a single quote (`'`) before quoting.
- **low** · ui · `apps/web/src/app/(app)/authority/page.tsx:112` · Confirm dialog says 'فقط با تو' for a row whose owner-only switch was turned back off
  - What: When the switch is toggled off, `set({ actor: r.actor })` stores `actor: r.actor` (the original non-owner actor) in the draft if the limit was also changed. The dialog renders `{c.actor ? 'فقط با تو' : null}` and treats any actor as owner-only.
  - Fix: Render the badge only when `c.actor === 'owner'`, and omit `actor` from the draft when it equals `r.actor`.
- **low** · date-time · `apps/web/src/app/(app)/calendar/page.tsx:114` · New event times are taken in the browser's time zone but shown in Tehran time
  - What: `new Date(from).toISOString()` reads the `datetime-local` value in the browser's local zone. Every displayed time uses OWNER_TZ Asia/Tehran (jTime/jDate, tehranDay).
  - Fix: Treat the input as Tehran wall time: append '+03:30' to the datetime-local string before parsing, or convert explicitly from Asia/Tehran.
- **low** · error-handling · `apps/web/src/app/(app)/cartable/page.tsx:116` · On a phone a decision that fails to load shows an endless skeleton
  - What: In the narrow layout the Sheet body is `detail.data ? <DecisionDetail/> : <Skeleton/>`, with no `detail.error` branch. The wide layout does handle errors.
  - Fix: Render `detail.error ? <ErrorState error={detail.error} onRetry={() => detail.refetch()} /> : ...` in the sheet, as in the wide layout.
- **low** · race-condition · `apps/web/src/app/(app)/founding/new/page.tsx:111` · Removing (or replacing) the logo during upload is undone when the upload finishes
  - What: `pick()` awaits `uploadFile` and then calls `set({ logoFileId: ref.id })` unconditionally. 'بردار' clears `preview` and `logoFileId` while the upload is still running, and a second pick starts a parallel upload. Whichever upload resolves last wins.
  - Fix: Track the current pick in a ref or counter and ignore results from superseded or cancelled picks. Revoke the object URL as well.
- **low** · ui · `apps/web/src/app/(app)/messages/page.tsx:131` · Phone layout gets stuck when `?thread=` names a thread that is not in the list
  - What: With `sel` set, the list card is `hidden lg:flex`. The back IconButton is rendered only inside the `current ? ...` branch. If no thread matches `sel` (still loading, a CEO not hired yet, a stale link), the right card shows the 'یک گفت‌وگو را انتخاب کن' empty state with no way back to the list.
  - Fix: Show the back button (router.replace('/messages')) in the empty branch too, or fall back to the list when `sel` matches no thread after loading.
- **low** · state-bug · `apps/web/src/app/(app)/okr/page.tsx:105` · Chosen quarter persists across a company change and leaves a blank page
  - What: `quarter` is local state kept when `picked` changes. If the new company has no objectives in that quarter, `list` is empty. `without` is non-empty, so the Empty state is skipped, but it renders only when `qtr === current`, so the grid is empty and no quarter chip is selected.
  - Fix: Reset `quarter` to null when `company` changes, or fall back to `current` when `quarter` is not in `quarters`.
- **low** · state-bug · `apps/web/src/app/(app)/oversight/page.tsx:115` · Finding note survives closing the sheet and is submitted with the next finding's status change
  - What: `<FindingSheet f={open} .../>` stays mounted and returns null when `f` is null, so `const [note, setNote] = useState('')` keeps its value across findings. `set()` sends `note: note.trim()` with whatever finding is open.
  - Fix: Key the sheet by the finding (`{open ? <FindingSheet key={open.ref} f={open} .../> : null}`) or reset `note` when `f?.ref` changes.
- **low** · date-time · `apps/web/src/app/(app)/planner/page.tsx:30` · Planner computes 'today' and the week from the UTC date, not the Tehran date
  - What: `weekStart(d)` uses `utc(d)` and `d.getUTCDay()`. `todayKey = key(new Date())` and `jMonth` use `utc(new Date())`. Between 00:00 and 03:30 Tehran the UTC date is still yesterday. Elsewhere the page formats in Asia/Tehran, and calendar/page.tsx uses a Tehran day.
  - Fix: Derive the current day from the Tehran calendar date, e.g. `new Date(Date.now() + 3.5*3600_000)` read with getUTC* (Iran has no DST), or format with Intl in Asia/Tehran. Use it for weekStart, jMonth and todayKey.
- **low** · rtl-persian · `apps/web/src/app/(app)/planner/page.tsx:76` · Web and Android take "today" from the UTC date, not the owner's Tehran day
  - What: planner/page.tsx:28 `key = d.toISOString().slice(0,10)`, :71 `weekStart(new Date())` on UTC days, :76 `todayKey = key(new Date())`. The API's /planner works in the owner's zone (routes/work.py:616-624). Others: components/v31/campaigns.tsx:20, 33, 52, 98 `iso(new Date())`; components/v33/money.tsx:165, 171-172, 227-228; Android ui/live/HoldingMap.kt:747-748 counts «فرمان‌های امروز» by `ZoneOffset.UTC`.
  - Fix: Derive the day from the owner's zone, e.g. `new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', {timeZone: OWNER_TZ}).format(d)` as v20/bits.tsx daysTo does, and `HqClock.today()` on Android.
- **low** · rtl-persian · `apps/web/src/app/(app)/planner/page.tsx:89` · Navigation tooltip/aria-label reads 'ماهی قبل' ('fish before') in month view
  - What: `label={`${unit}ی قبل`}` with unit 'ماه' gives 'ماهی قبل' / 'ماهی بعد' ('fish', not 'the month'). With 'هفته' it gives 'هفتهی قبل' without the ZWNJ/ی. IconButton puts the label in `title` and `aria-label`.
  - Fix: Use explicit labels: view === 'month' ? 'ماه قبل' / 'ماه بعد' : 'هفته‌ی قبل' / 'هفته‌ی بعد'.
- **low** · correctness · `apps/web/src/app/(app)/resolutions/page.tsx:28` · Resolutions ledger shows and exports only the first page
  - What: `useResolutionList` fetches one page (API default limit 50) and the page ignores `next_cursor`: no 'بیشتر' button. `exportCsv` exports only `items`.
  - Fix: Use an infinite query with a 'بیشتر' button, and fetch every page (or a server-side export) for the CSV.
- **low** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/app/(app)/sites/page.tsx:76` · Interval option '30 ثانیه' violates the API minimum (60) and makes the whole save fail
  - What: The select offers `[30, 60, 300, 900]`, but `SiteIn.interval_s` is `ge=60` (openapi `minimum: 60`; routes/runs.py:326). A stored interval missing from the list (e.g. 120 or 3600) also shows as the first option, '30 ثانیه'.
  - Fix: Offer only values ≥ 60 (e.g. 60, 300, 900, 3600) and include the row's current `interval_s` as an option when it is not in the list.
- **low** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/app/(app)/stars/page.tsx:58` · Company standings with no data render as '0.0 stars' and a blank rank and score
  - What: The contract (CompanyStanding) makes `stars`, `score` and `rank` nullable ('a part without data is null'). The page passes `c.stars` to `<Stars value>`, which computes `Math.max(0, Math.min(5, null))` = 0 and prints `0.0`. `c.rank` and `best.score` render as empty text. The local TS type (`stars: number; score: number; rank: number`) hides this.
  - Fix: Type these fields as `number | null` and render «—» when they are null: in Stars (accept null), for rank, and for the best-company score.
- **low** · ui · `apps/web/src/app/(app)/stars/page.tsx:118` · Star rubric card shows a loading skeleton forever when there is no rubric
  - What: `const rubric = rubrics.data?.find((x) => x.company === (co ?? r?.best_company))` and then `rubric ? … : <Skeleton className="h-32" />`. `best_company` is nullable: GET /stars/rollup returns `best_company: null` and empty lists when no roll-up has been built yet. A company may also have no rubric, or the rubrics request may fail. All three cases fall into the Skeleton branch, which never resolves.
  - Fix: Show the skeleton only while `rubrics.isLoading`. Show an empty state («هنوز آیین‌نامه‌ای نیست») when the data has loaded and nothing matches, and an ErrorState when `rubrics.error`.
- **low** · error-handling · `apps/web/src/app/(app)/support/page.tsx:23` · Support header tiles (and the tools page tiles) stay as skeletons forever if one request fails
  - What: `const ready = queue.data && channels.data && proposed.data && shadow.data` gates all four StatTiles on four queries, and no error state is shown. If any query errors, the four tiles shimmer indefinitely. The same pattern is in tools/page.tsx line 35 (`!s ?` skeleton for useEgressSummary, no error branch), and its request log (line 47) shows an empty list on error.
  - Fix: Render each tile from its own query with its own loading and error («—»), or add an ErrorState with retry when any of the queries has `error`.
- **low** · ui · `apps/web/src/app/(app)/tools/page.tsx:78` · Enabling one MCP server shows a spinner on every server's button; 'disable' has no busy state
  - What: `loading={act.enable.isPending}` reads one shared mutation, so every non-enabled row's «مُهر و روشن» becomes a spinner and is disabled while any one is sealing. The «خاموش کن» button has no loading or disabled state, so a double tap sends two disable calls.
  - Fix: Compare `act.enable.variables === m.key` (and the same for disable) and pass `loading` per row.
- **low** · correctness · `apps/web/src/app/(app)/treasury/page.tsx:95` · Monthly forecast is always drawn to day 30, whatever the month length
  - What: `const daysInMonth = 30` is passed as `forecastTo` to CumulativeChart. `/treasury/breakdown?by=day` returns days from the first of the owner's month, so the dashed 'forecast to the end of the month' line ends on day 30 in 31-day months. In February it is extended 1–2 days past the month's end. The forecast compared against the budget line is therefore wrong.
  - Fix: Compute the length of the owner's current month (Asia/Tehran, or the month of the first day key: `new Date(Date.UTC(y, m, 0)).getUTCDate()` from `days[0].key`) and pass that as `forecastTo`.
- **low** · error-handling · `apps/web/src/app/(app)/work/[id]/page.tsx:39` · Work page 'retry' does nothing for a failed load
  - What: The error comes from the react-query `useWorkDetail` (through `useTrack`). The retry handler is `router.refresh()`, which only re-requests the RSC payload of this client page. It does not refetch the failed query, so the ErrorState stays.
  - Fix: Return `refetch` from `useTrack` (`d.refetch`) and call it from onRetry.
- **low** · error-handling · `apps/web/src/app/(auth)/enroll/page.tsx:40` · Enrolment reports failure when only the follow-up sign-in failed, and a retry cannot succeed
  - What: `await enrol(...)` registers the passkey and consumes the one-time token. Then `await signIn()` runs in the same try. If the second ceremony is cancelled or fails, the catch shows «ثبت انجام نشد» and sets state back to idle. Pressing the button again calls `enrol` with the already-used token, which fails.
  - Fix: Use separate try blocks. After a successful `enrol`, mark it done. If `signIn` fails, show «این دستگاه ثبت شد؛ وارد شو» with a link to /login instead of the enrol error.
- **low** · ui · `apps/web/src/components/brand/seal.tsx:117` · Hold-progress ring of the seal is clipped by its own SVG box
  - What: The ring's radius is `R = s/2 + 5` with `strokeWidth={3}`, so its outer edge sits at s/2 + 6.5 from the centre. The SVG is only `s + 12` wide and high, centred at s/2 + 6, and has no `overflow-visible`. The outer 0.5 px of the stroke is cut on all four sides.
  - Fix: Make the SVG `s + 14` wide and high (centre s/2 + 7, offset -7px), or add `overflow-visible` to the SVG.
- **low** · state · `apps/web/src/components/chat/chat.tsx:100` · Live-reply fallback keeps showing 'thinking' for 2 minutes after the reply arrives, and its poll timer outlives the component
  - What: In `src.onerror` when nothing has streamed yet (`got === false`), a `setInterval` polls messages every 3 s. It sets `live` back to idle only after 40 ticks. It never checks whether the reply message has arrived. The interval id is not kept, so the unmount cleanup (`es.current?.close()`) does not clear it, and a new `start()` does not clear an older poll either.
  - Fix: Keep the interval in a ref. Clear it on unmount and on the next `start()`. Stop polling and set live to idle as soon as the messages query holds a non-owner message newer than the one just sent.
- **low** · correctness · `apps/web/src/components/look/group.tsx:68` · nextRun shows a wrong or missing next run for schedules the hub accepts
  - What: The hub stores normalised RRULEs that may contain `INTERVAL` for HOURLY (a divisor of 24, restarting at local midnight) and comma lists in BYHOUR, BYMINUTE and BYMONTHDAY (domain/recurrence.py). `nextRun` ignores INTERVAL: HOURLY always returns the next whole hour. It uses `Number(k.BYHOUR)`, so `"9,18"` gives NaN and the function returns null; a BYMONTHDAY list gives NaN as well.
  - Fix: Parse every BY* part as a list. For HOURLY, step through hours h with `h % INTERVAL === 0` from local midnight. Take the minimum over all hour and minute (and month-day) combinations.
- **low** · state · `apps/web/src/components/session/session-view.tsx:565` · Close sheet keeps its «لغو فرمان» confirmation armed after it is closed from code
  - What: `sure` is reset only inside the SpringSheet's own onOpenChange (`if (!v) setSure(false)`). The parent closes the sheet through `onClosed` (setClosing(false) after a successful close) and through `onAsk`. A controlled `open` change does not call Radix's onOpenChange, so `sure` stays true.
  - Fix: Reset `sure` whenever `open` becomes false (e.g. `useEffect(() => { if (!open) setSure(false); }, [open])`), or remount CloseSheet with a key on each open.
- **low** · rtl-persian · `apps/web/src/components/shell/command-palette.tsx:34` · Ctrl/⌘+K does not open the palette while a Persian keyboard layout is active
  - What: The shortcut tests `e.key.toLowerCase() === 'k'`. With a Persian layout, the K key gives `e.key === 'ن'`, so the check fails. The owner works in Persian, and the top bar advertises «Ctrl K».
  - Fix: Match the physical key: `(e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && (e.code === 'KeyK' || e.key.toLowerCase() === 'k')`.
- **low** · ui · `apps/web/src/components/v15/runs.tsx:26` · Undefined CSS variable `--hq-card` on the step dots
  - What: `border-2 border-[var(--hq-card)]` uses a token that no stylesheet defines. The defined token is `--hq-surface-card`, and this is the only use of `--hq-card` in the slice. An invalid var() makes border-color fall back to currentColor.
  - Fix: Use the defined token, e.g. `border-[var(--hq-surface-tile)]` or `--hq-surface-card` to match the row's background.
- **low** · accessibility · `apps/web/src/components/v15/runs.tsx:63` · Link nested inside a <button> in RunRow
  - What: The work-ref `<Link href={`/work/${run.work_ref}`}>` sits inside the row's toggle `<button>`. Interactive content inside a button is invalid HTML. Screen readers expose one button and the link is unreachable by keyboard: Tab stops on the button, and Enter toggles the row instead of opening the work.
  - Fix: Move the Link out of the button (e.g. into a sibling row under it) or make the header a div with a separate toggle button.
- **low** · timezone · `apps/web/src/components/v15/treasury.tsx:51` · What-if month window is computed in UTC, while the API reads a date as the owner's (Tehran) day
  - What: `from` is the first of the month in UTC and `to = now.toISOString().slice(0,10)` (UTC date). The what-if endpoint documents a YYYY-MM-DD as "that day in the owner's time zone". Between 00:00 and 03:30 Tehran time, the UTC date is still yesterday.
  - Fix: Build `from` and `to` from the Asia/Tehran calendar date (Intl.DateTimeFormat with timeZone OWNER_TZ, 'en-CA'), as scheduledIso does.
- **low** · rtl-persian · `apps/web/src/components/v15/treasury.tsx:92` · Budget editor rejects Persian digits and grouped numbers, disabling Save with no message
  - What: `valid` checks `Number(r.tokens.replace('٫', '.')) > 0` and `Number(r.usd) > 0`. Persian digits (۰-۹), which a Persian mobile keyboard gives with inputMode=decimal, make Number() return NaN. Thousands separators do the same, although `fromM` strips them. The hiring envelope sheet and clockOf convert Persian digits; this editor does not.
  - Fix: Normalise the input before validating and before fromM: map [۰-۹] and [٠-٩] to ASCII, strip ٬ and ',', map ٫ to '.'. Use the same parser in `valid`, `fromM` and the usd field.
- **low** · ui · `apps/web/src/components/v17/cards.tsx:104` · Decimal inputs that are stored as a Number cannot take a decimal point while typing
  - What: These controlled inputs store `Number(text)` and render it back as `value`: the KPI target (`value={k.target ?? ''}`, onChange `Number(e.target.value.replace(/[^\d.]/g,''))`), the authority limits (app/(app)/authority/page.tsx:49-50, `value={String(num.value)}`), and the substitutes behind_ratio (components/v18/settings.tsx:72, `Number(e.target.value) || 0`). When the text is '2.', Number('2.') is 2, the field re-renders as '2', and the '.' is lost.
  - Fix: Keep the raw text in local state while the field is being edited, as sales.tsx does with `value`, and convert to a number only on save or blur. Or keep a trailing '.' / '0' in the displayed string.
- **low** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/components/v17/cards.tsx:147` · Range KPIs (direction 'range', target_max) are judged as 'up'
  - What: CardKpi and Kpi carry `direction` (which the server allows to be 'range', per migration 0026_1 and kits.py) and `target_max`. `onTarget(v, target, direction)` (v17/bits.tsx:114) only knows up and down, so 'range' falls through to `v >= target` and target_max is ignored. KpiSheet (line 73) shows 'کمتر بهتر' for any direction that is not 'up', including range. The server's own at_target (gov_kpis.py:813) checks `target <= value <= target_max`.
  - Fix: Add 'range' to the direction type, pass target_max, and implement `range: target <= v && v <= (target_max ?? target)`. Label range KPIs «در بازه».
- **low** · concurrency · `apps/web/src/components/v18/advisor.tsx:45` · Marking two agenda items in quick succession fails with 409 (stale If-Match version)
  - What: Each AgendaRow sends `ifMatch(r.version)` from the cached review. The PATCH response (the new WeeklyReview) is not written back into the cache; onSuccess only invalidates. Until the refetch lands, every other row still holds the old version.
  - Fix: In the item mutation's onSuccess, `qc.setQueryData(['reviews','current'], out)` before invalidating, or read the latest version from the query cache when firing.
- **low** · correctness · `apps/web/src/components/v18/health.tsx:68` · Checked-claim row links to /employees/null when the claim has no employee
  - What: Claim.employee is nullable in the OpenAPI (`employee: string | null`), but ClaimsCard always renders `<Link href={`/employees/${c.employee}`}>{c.employee}</Link>`.
  - Fix: Render the link only when `c.employee` is set.
- **low** · correctness · `apps/web/src/components/v18/treasury.tsx:37` · Waste 'vs 7-day average' shows «+0%» when the server has no baseline (vs_7d null)
  - What: WasteDay.vs_7d is nullable: domain/waste.py:351 returns `"vs_7d": None` for days computed on the fly. The UI renders `{d.vs_7d >= 0 ? '+' : '−'}{pct01(Math.abs(d.vs_7d))}`, and `null >= 0` is true while `Math.abs(null)` is 0.
  - Fix: Render '—' when `d.vs_7d == null`, and type it `number | null`.
- **low** · rtl-persian · `apps/web/src/components/v19/legal.tsx:121` · Persian email bodies in the case thread render left-aligned in the Latin figure face
  - What: EmailItem always renders the body as `<Md ... className="fig ... text-left" />`, regardless of `e.lang`. ExactEmail, a few lines above, switches dir and font on `e.lang === 'en'`.

- **low** · correctness · `apps/web/src/components/v20/holds.tsx:121` · «اکنون N سند را میگیرد» counts only the first page of records
  - What: NewHoldSheet computes `hit = records.filter(r => covers(m, s, r))`, where `records` comes from `useRecords({})`, an infinite query with limit 50 whose next pages are never fetched. The footer and preview present this as the current count.
  - Fix: Ask the server for the count (or page through every result), or word the number as a sample of the first 50 records.
- **low** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/components/v20/record.tsx:42` · New-version sheet names the refused file «file_ids» instead of the file
  - What: `refusedName = ... Object.keys(e.fields ?? {})[0]`. For POST /archive/records/{ref}/versions the hub's intake (store.py:244) sends `fields={file_ids: <id>, kinds: ...}`, so `refused` is the string `file_ids`. Line 262 renders «file_ids» رد شد…, and no row matches `refused === f.name`, so none is highlighted.
  - Fix: Resolve the refused file by id: keep the upload id per row and compare `x.id === e.fields?.file_ids`. Show that row's name in the message.
- **low** · error-handling · `apps/web/src/components/v20/record.tsx:238` · New-version upload rows match by file name; a failed or duplicate-name upload blocks the sheet
  - What: Upload progress and results are matched with `x.name === f.name && !x.id`. If two picked files share a name, the first upload's id is written onto both rows and the second upload is lost (same id sent twice). If any upload throws, its row keeps `id: null` with no remove or retry. The send button is disabled by `files.some((x) => !x.id)`, so it stays disabled for good.
  - Fix: Give each row a unique key (crypto.randomUUID()) and match on it. Record the error on the row and offer remove/retry, or exclude failed rows from the disabled check.
- **low** · state · `apps/web/src/components/v20/records.tsx:256` · Adding files overwrites the owner's form edits made while those files upload (stale `touched`)
  - What: `add` captures `touched` from the render it was created in, then awaits all uploads and calls `scan(upsRef.current, touched)`. If the owner edits the form during the upload, `touched` is true in state but still false in that closure. `scan(..., keepForm=false)` then replaces the whole form (class, title, metadata, level, folder) with the suggestion.
  - Fix: Keep `touched` in a ref (`touchedRef.current = true` in `edit`) and pass `touchedRef.current` to scan after the await.
- **low** · state · `apps/web/src/components/v20/schema.tsx:449` · Automation step `lead_days` input is uncontrolled under index keys, so it shows another step's value after a removal
  - What: DoEditor renders steps with `key={i}`, and the lead_days input uses `defaultValue`. When a step above is removed, React reuses the DOM input at that index. The input keeps the removed step's text while `value[i].args.lead_days` now belongs to the next step.
  - Fix: Make the input controlled (keep a text state per step) or key steps by a stable id instead of the index.
- **low** · rtl-persian · `apps/web/src/components/v20/schema.tsx:539` · Retention term inputs turn Persian digits into NaN
  - What: The `keep` editor does `years: e.target.value ? Number(e.target.value) : undefined` (same for months). Unlike the `int` kind (line 512) and `toInts`, it does not map ۰-۹ to ASCII first, and `Number('۵')` is NaN. The input then shows «NaN», and the body serialises it as `null`.
  - Fix: Normalise the digits as the int input does: `Number(e.target.value.replace(/[۰-۹]/g, d => String('۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹'.indexOf(d))))`, and ignore non-finite results.
- **low** · concurrency · `apps/web/src/components/v21/notifications.tsx:433` · «برگردان» silently does nothing after 5 s: UndoBar's timer restarts on every render while the read is already sent
  - What: `<UndoBar undo={undo} onDone={(id) => setUndo(...)} />` passes a new `onDone` on every render. UndoBar (v21/bits.tsx:48-52) has `useEffect(..., [undo, onDone])`, so every re-render clears its 5 s timer and starts a new one. useReads sends the read after its own GRACE_MS=5000 timer. Sending changes the read mutation's state and then invalidates the notifications queries, both of which re-render NotificationsView, which restarts UndoBar's timer. The bar stays up about 5 s longer than the undo window. A click then runs `r.undo(token)`, which finds no waiting entry and returns.
  - Fix: Pass a stable callback, e.g. `const doneUndo = useCallback((id:number) => setUndo(u => u?.id === id ? null : u), [])`. Or make UndoBar keep onDone in a ref and depend only on `undo`, so the bar disappears at the same moment the send happens.
- **low** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/components/v29/report.tsx:226` · Order outputs always show the generic icon and the label «خروجی»
  - What: The icon and label are keyed on `o.kind` being 'reel' / 'post' / 'story' (OutputKind), but the report's `Output.kind` enum is ['final','part','partial'] (OpenAPI `Output`). So the icon is always 'deliverable' and the label is always 'خروجی', and the distinction between final and partial outputs that the hub sends is lost.
  - Fix: Map final/part/partial to Persian labels (e.g. «نهایی», «بخش», «نیمه‌کاره») and keep the icon generic.

The code then appends `holding.data.filter(a => !a.company)` from a second unfiltered query, so every holding cover folder appears twice with the same `key={a.id}`.

The card still renders «این کار انجام میشود» with an empty preview and an enabled «ثبت و مُهر», which posts `{rule: null}` to POST /automations, where `AutomationIn.rule` is a required dict.

The seal path (`act.seal.mutate(said)`) and ApplyDialog (`publishRule(word)`, line 325) pass through `publishRule`, which omits `at` when `clockOf(at)` is null, with no warning.

The hub 'follows the route exactly' (sendOrder29 docs).
  - Fix: Disable send (or drop `route`) while `text.trim() !== settled` or `sec.isFetching`, or compare `preview` with the text it was computed for before using it.
- **low** · ui · `apps/web/src/lib/alerts.ts:35` · The panel and the service worker use different alert tags for notifications with choices, so the owner gets duplicate OS notifications
  - What: keyOf returns `n:${groupKey(n)}`, and groupKey (lib/notify.ts:29) gives `id:${n.id}` for any notification with choices. The service worker's tagOf (public/sw.js:47-52) always uses `n:company|level|link_kind|normalized title` and has no choices branch; the push payload carries no choices or id. The header comment relies on 'the same tag, so nothing is told twice'. Agents can raise notifications with choices at the decision or urgent level (routes/internal.py:167, 1449), and both of those levels are in LEVELS.
  - Fix: Make the two tag rules identical. Either drop the choices special case from keyOf (use the company|level|kind|title key for alerts), or add the notification id to the push data and give sw.js tagOf the same `id:` branch when the push says it has choices.

  - What: closeSystem({ ids }) closes a notification only when `(n.data as { nid?: string }).nid` matches. The panel's own showSystem sets data.nid (line 109). The service worker's push handler (public/sw.js:70-78) sets only `data: { url }`, and the server's PushMessage.data() (channels/__init__.py:38-47) never carries the notification id. So no Web Push notification ever has a nid. The only caller that marks notifications read, components/v21/notifications.tsx:67, calls `closeSystem({ ids })` without tags, even though the push uses a deterministic tag (sw tagOf, the same as keyOf here).

Alternatively, have closeSystem accept Notification objects and compute keyOf for each. Another option is to add the notification id to the push payload (PushMessage.data) and to data.nid in sw.js.
- **low** · contract-mismatch · `apps/web/src/lib/api/client.ts:31` · send_blocked guard reasons are read from a field the server never sends, so the owner never sees why a reply was refused
  - What: ApiError reads `const extra = problem as { blocked_reasons?: string[]; ... }; this.blockedReasons = extra?.blocked_reasons ?? [];`, which is a top-level array. The server builds the Problem body only from the fixed fields type/title/status/code/detail/message_fa/fields/request_id/missing (errors.py:288-299, problem_response). desk.owner_send raises `ApiError(422, "send_blocked", ..., fields={"blocked_reasons": ",".join(composed.verdict.reasons)})` (domain/desk.py:953-957), so the reasons arrive as a comma-joined string in `fields.blocked_reasons`. routes/support.py:306 documents the same, and so do docs/ops/RUNBOOK.md:1296 and PROMPT_10.md:132. A top-level `blocked_reasons` is never present in production. The mock (mock-v16.ts:440) puts it at the top level, which is why this works in demo mode but not on the live hub.
  - Fix: In the ApiError constructor, also take the reasons from `fields`: `const fr = (problem?.fields as Record<string, unknown> | null | undefined)?.blocked_reasons; this.blockedReasons = extra?.blocked_reasons ?? (Array.isArray(fr) ? fr.map(String) : typeof fr === 'string' && fr ? fr.split(',').map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean) : []);`. Optionally align mock-v16.ts:440 to send `{ fields: { blocked_reasons: reasons.join(',') } }` so the demo matches the server.
